*This file contains §09 (Design system and UX specification) and §10 (Screen-by-screen specification) of the Margin master build prompt. Both depend on SYNTHESIS §6 (IA, layout rulings, composer chips, canonical screen list) and §10 (design-system decisions), which they turn into testable requirements; §09 is the token and component contract that every screen in §10 consumes. They assume the pedagogy contract of §04 (Turn, Effort Gate, Help Ladder, intent chips), the Mark contract of §05 (per-AO record, bands, calibration status), the provenance rules of §06, the learner-state objects of §07 and the performance budgets of §11 and §13. Requirement IDs are 09-TAG-NNN / 10-TAG-NNN; "Acceptance:" lines say how a reviewer verifies. Paragraphs marked [founder default] depend on SYNTHESIS §15 decisions.*

# §09 Design system and UX specification ("every pixel")

## 09.1 Design principles
The interface is a pedagogical instrument. Calm removes extraneous load; speed keeps feedback inside the window where the Attempt is still in working memory; one visible next action is the visual form of "one move per Turn". Every token and component below serves one of those three. Each principle is a rule plus the test a reviewer runs; a PR that fails the test does not merge.

| ID | Principle | Rule | Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| 09-PRIN-001 | Calm by default, expressive by exception | Motion, colour and celebration budgets are spent only on rare moments; the product has exactly one celebratory animation (a paper first reaching Exam-ready). | grep the animation registry: one entry tagged `celebrate`; no confetti/particle code in the bundle. |
| 09-PRIN-002 | The source is one gesture away | Every claim about a syllabus, mark scheme, examiner report or textbook carries a citation chip that opens the exact page. | Sample 50 assistant paragraphs with syllabus claims: 50 chips; chip → highlighted page ≤400 ms p95 from cache. |
| 09-PRIN-003 | Reading is the primary activity | Body text 17 px/1.55 at ≤68 ch in chat; 18/1.6 at ≤66 ch in the reader; nothing anywhere wider than 75 ch. | Playwright measures the widest paragraph on every screen at 1280, 1600 and 2560 px. |
| 09-PRIN-004 | Mode is visible and cannot be lost | The Learn · Practise · Mark chip is always in the composer; no study toggle exists anywhere. | Five new users name the current mode within 3 s in a moderated test; no `study_mode` boolean in settings. |
| 09-PRIN-005 | One move, then the pen comes back | A Turn ends with the composer focused and its placeholder naming the next action. | 100 sampled Turns: composer focused after paint in 100%; placeholder ≠ default in ≥90%. |
| 09-PRIN-006 | Evidence on the page | A Mark highlights credited, partial and uncredited spans inside the student's own answer; a credited point without a span is a defect. | Mark eval fixture: 0 credited points without `credited_spans[]`; each span resolves to a DOM range. |
| 09-PRIN-007 | A band wherever the marker is uncertain, an exact mark only where it is verified | Totals render as low–modal–high wherever marker uncertainty exists. A points Mark whose every marking point is SymPy-verified renders as an exact integer with the `exact` badge; a points Mark with any unverified point, and every levels Mark, renders as a band. A bare integer with neither a band nor the `exact` badge is a defect. | Visual regression on Mark view across the four BandTotal states of 09-COMP-001: a levels fixture renders "11–13 / 20"; a fully verified points fixture renders "4 / 5 · exact"; a part-verified points fixture renders a band. |
| 09-PRIN-008 | Hierarchy over recency | The sidebar is Course › Unit › Syllabus point; Recent and Due are secondary groups. | Sidebar snapshot: tree first; no chat list as the primary group. |
| 09-PRIN-009 | Instant acknowledgement, honest waiting | Optimistic echo <50 ms; status text at 800 ms; "Taking longer" + Stop at 6 s; no decorative loaders. | Synthetic 3 s TTFT: three states observed at the right times in a recorded trace. |
| 09-PRIN-010 | Stability while streaming | DOM above the stream cursor is immutable; unclosed math/code is held as a skeleton. | CLS above cursor = 0 in the jank test on 20 golden streams. |
| 09-PRIN-011 | Borders first, shadows second | Surfaces are defined by 1 px hairlines; stacked soft shadows only on floating layers; no shadows on list cards. | Stylelint: `box-shadow` only via `--shadow-*` tokens; shadow-2+ forbidden on `.card-list *`. |
| 09-PRIN-012 | Grey dark mode with a ladder | Dark surfaces are a five-step grey ladder from 16% L; never `#000`; elevation = lighter surface. | Token test: no dark token with L <14%; every pair passes contrast. |
| 09-PRIN-013 | Accessibility numbers are tokens | 24 px targets, 44 pt touch, 2 px/3:1 focus, 4.5:1 text, 320 px reflow are CSS tokens and CI gates, not guidelines. | axe + custom checks green on every PR; failing check blocks merge. |
| 09-PRIN-014 | Keyboard-complete, thumb-complete | Every action has a key on desktop and sits in the bottom 40% of the screen on a phone. | Keyboard traversal script completes each screen's primary flow; mobile primary controls within the thumb zone in screenshots. |
| 09-PRIN-015 | Progress is evidence and reversible | Mastery is four states by shape with a reason; it decays and says why; no bare percentages, no streaks. | Copy corpus policy test: zero strings matching the streak/shame lexicon; no `%` in mastery components. |
| 09-PRIN-016 | Respect attention | ≤1 opt-in factual notification per day; none in Quiet Hours; never loss-framed, never a predicted grade. | Notification scheduler unit test; copy lint on notification templates. |
| 09-PRIN-017 | Math and handwriting are native | LaTeX in, KaTeX + MathML out, keypad and camera are composer states; photo → confirmed transcript before any Mark. | 200-formula render suite incl. `mhchem`; no Mark request without `transcript_confirmed=true`. |
| 09-PRIN-018 | One reading-preference set everywhere | Family, size, leading, tracking, measure are set once and honoured by chat, reader, Cards and print. | Change each preference; screenshot chat, reader, Card, print preview: all reflect it. |
| 09-PRIN-019 | Numbers are honest and tabular | Every changing number uses tabular numerals; every estimate names its basis (series, n, agreement). | Font-feature audit on timers, countdowns, marks; calibration line present on every Mark. |
| 09-PRIN-020 | Limits are visible, never sudden | Usage shows in plain numbers; slow-down replaces hard stops; exam-week grace is stated. | Simulate quota exhaustion mid-Session: no modal, no stop; factual line and cheap-tier continuation. |

Why: the praised 2025–26 redesigns (Linear, Gemini's larger type) reduced noise and raised hierarchy; the criticised ones (pulsing "thinking" backgrounds, flat recency sidebars) added motion or clutter with no task benefit (research/ux-design §0). Students' top interface complaints are walls of text and busy screens (student-voice §0.8, §9).

## 09.2 Typography tokens
**09-TYPE-001** Families. Ship exactly these, self-hosted, subset for Latin + Cyrillic + Cyrillic-ext + Greek + math symbols; no CDN fonts.

| Token | Family | Role | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--font-sans` | Inter (variable, `opsz` axis) → system-ui | UI and reading default at launch | `font-feature-settings: "cv11", "ss03"` off by default; `tnum` on numbers. Default change to a serif only after the v1 reading-fatigue A/B (JUDGING 12). |
| `--font-hyper` | Atkinson Hyperlegible Next (variable, 7 weights) | Reading-comfort option | 150+ languages; b/d remain mirrored, so it is a low-vision face, not a dyslexia cure. |
| `--font-dys` | OpenDyslexic | Reading-comfort option | Individual font effects are real (Wallace et al. 2022) — offer choice, never one "dyslexia font". |
| `--font-serif` | Literata → PT Serif → Georgia | Optional reading face | Must have true Cyrillic italics (09-I18N-004). **PROVISIONAL:** the sans default flips to Literata for reader surfaces only if the reading-face A/B (registered as the sixth experiment of 13-EFF-003, primary outcome comprehension-check accuracy on Reader Items, guard-rail time-on-page) shows a gain; until that experiment reports, Inter is the default everywhere and this row is opt-in. |
| `--font-mono` | Geist Mono → JetBrains Mono → ui-monospace | Code, LaTeX source, `ecf`/annotation badges | Slashed zero on. |

**09-TYPE-002** Scale (px / line-height). Use only these tokens; arbitrary `font-size` values fail stylelint.

| Token | Size/LH | Use | Measure |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--text-xs` | 12/1.35 | captions, chip numerals, interval hints | — |
| `--text-sm` | 13/1.4 | status lines, metadata, calibration badge, mode-chip labels | — |
| `--text-ui` | 14/1.43 | controls, sidebar rows, table cells | — |
| `--text-body` | 17/1.55 | chat Turns, Mark rationale | 68 ch (≈720 px Inter) |
| `--text-read` | 18/1.6 | reader, student answer in Mark view, Card back | 66 ch |
| `--text-focus` | 20/1.6 | reader "Focus" | 60 ch |
| `--text-lg` | 20/1.4 | Card face body | — |
| `--text-h3` | 20/1.3 · 600 | maximum heading inside a Turn | — |
| `--text-h2` | 24/1.25 · 600 | screen section titles | — |
| `--text-h1` | 30/1.2 · 600 | screen titles | — |
| `--text-display` | 36/1.1 · 600, tracking −0.02 em | landing line only | — |

Tracking: display −0.02 em; body 0; caps labels +0.06 em. Paragraph gap 0.75 em in chat, 1 em in reader; no first-line indent. Lists 4 px between items, marker inset 1.25 em. Nothing under 12 px anywhere; nothing under 11 pt on iOS.

**09-TYPE-003** Tabular numerals. `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` on timers, countdowns, marks, bands, due counts, intervals, thresholds and every table numeric column; numeric columns right-aligned. Acceptance: a timer counting from 09:59 to 10:00 does not shift the characters to its right.

**09-TYPE-004** Headings inside assistant content are capped at `--text-h3`; `h1`/`h2` from model markdown are demoted at render. Why: a Turn is not a landing page; students name "walls" as a top pain.

**09-TYPE-005** Weights: 400 body, 500 UI labels and modal marks, 600 headings and the bold modal number in a band. Never 300 or lighter; never 700+ except the modal mark in a band.

**09-TYPE-006** Glyph coverage. CI renders the sentence "Ә Ғ Қ Ң Ө Ұ Ү Һ І — Қазақстан, Өскемен; ёж, щука, ъ" plus "∑ ∫ ≤ ≥ ≠ → ⇌ ° µ" in every family and fails on any fallback glyph (see 09-I18N-002).

**09-TYPE-007** Text-spacing survival. Every screen survives WCAG 1.4.12 (line-height 1.5×, paragraph 2×, letter +0.12 em, word +0.16 em) and 200% font scaling without clipping or overlap. Acceptance: Playwright injects the text-spacing stylesheet; no element reports `scrollWidth > clientWidth` except tables, code and PDF pages.

## 09.3 Spacing, radii, sizes and elevation
**09-SPCE-001** Spacing is a 4 px base; only these tokens: `--space-1..16` = 4 8 12 16 20 24 32 40 48 64. Component internals may use 2 px only for hairline offsets (focus ring offset, chip padding-inline 6 px counts as 4+2). Stylelint rejects any margin/padding/gap not expressed as a token.

**09-SPCE-002** Radii: `--radius-xs` 4 (keycaps, code inline) · `-sm` 6 (rows, chips, citation chips) · `-md` 8 (buttons, inputs) · `-lg` 12 (composer, cards) · `-xl` 16 (user message block, sheets top corners) · `-2xl` 20 (large panels) · `-pill` 9999 (mode chip, intent chips, tags). Nested radii: inner = outer − padding, never larger than outer.

**09-SPCE-003** Sizes.

| Token | px | Applies to |
|---|---|---|
| `--target-min` | 24 | every pointer target (WCAG 2.5.8) |
| `--touch-min` | 44 | every target on coarse pointers |
| `--control-h` | 36 | buttons, inputs, palette input, effort dial |
| `--control-h-lg` | 44 | primary CTA on mobile, sheet buttons |
| `--row-h` | 32 | sidebar rows, palette rows, tree rows |
| `--chip-h` | 32 | mode chip, intent chips |
| `--cite-h` | 20 | citation chip (numeral `--text-xs` 12 px, 2 px inline padding, 24 px hit area) |
| `--composer-min` | 56 | composer at rest; grows to 8 lines |
| `--topbar-h` | 48 desktop / 44 mobile | |
| `--tabstrip-h` | 40 | right-pane tab strip |
| `--banner-h` | 40 | Banner, single line at ≥900 px |
| `--banner-h-2` | 56 | Banner, two lines below 900 px |
| `--rail-w` | 48 | collapsed right pane |
| `--sidebar-w` | 280 (240–360) | persisted per user |
| `--panel-w` | 480 (360–640) | persisted per user |
| `--panel-w-2` | 440 | the second pane of the `wide` grid variant (09-SPCE-005), Mark view and Reader only |
| `--pip` | 10 | mastery pip |
| `--ring` | 64, 4 px stroke | readiness ring |
| `--card-btn-h` | 56 | flashcard Again / Got it |

**09-SPCE-004** Elevation is border-first. `--ring-hairline: 0 0 0 1px oklch(0% 0 0 / .08)` (dark: `oklch(100% 0 0 / .08)`). `--shadow-1` = hairline (cards, inputs). `--shadow-2` = `0 1px 1px /.02, 0 2px 2px /.04` + hairline (popovers, hover cards, dropdown). `--shadow-3` = `0 2px 2px /.04, 0 8px 16px −4px /.04` + hairline (sheets, drawers). `--shadow-4` = `0 1px 1px /.02, 0 8px 16px −4px /.04, 0 24px 32px −8px /.06` + hairline (the rare modal). No single heavy drop; no shadow above 12% alpha; no shadows on cards inside lists or on the Turn canvas. Dark mode conveys elevation by surface step, not shadow strength.

**09-SPCE-005** Grid. Desktop ≥1280: `grid-template-columns: var(--sidebar-w) 1fr var(--panel-w)`; between 1280 and 1600 the panel shrinks first to its minimum; above 1600 surplus goes to centre margins, never to the measure. Tablet 900–1279: `1fr 420px`, sidebar overlay. Mobile <900: one column. **`wide` variant:** at ≥1600 two screens — Mark view (10.7) and the Reader (10.16) — may instead render `var(--sidebar-w) 1fr var(--panel-w-2) var(--panel-w)`, showing two right panes side by side and dropping their tab strip; no other screen may use it, and the centre measure is unchanged by the variant. Acceptance: resize 1280→2560 on every route; centre paragraph width stays ≤68 ch throughout; at 1600 and 2560 the Mark view shows two panes of 440 and 480 px and every other route shows one.

## 09.4 Colour roles (OKLCH, light and dark)
**09-COLR-001** All colour is OKLCH, expressed as semantic roles with Radix 12-step meaning (1–2 backgrounds · 3–5 component fills · 6–8 borders · 9–10 solids · 11–12 text). No raw colour literal outside `tokens.css`; stylelint enforces. One accent hue (250) in the whole product. AO cards, modes, trust badges and subjects are distinguished by label and shape, never by hue.

| Role | Light | Dark | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--bg-app` (1) | `98.5% .004 90` | `16% .006 260` | body |
| `--bg-subtle` (2) | `97% .004 90` | `19% .006 260` | sidebar, rail, PDF surround |
| `--bg-el` (3) | `94.5% .004 90` | `23% .007 260` | user message block, chips, active row |
| `--bg-el-hover` (4) | `92% .004 90` | `26% .007 260` | |
| `--bg-el-active` (5) | `89% .005 90` | `29% .008 260` | |
| `--border-subtle` (6) | `88% .005 90` | `27% .008 260` | decorative separators only: table rows, list dividers |
| `--border` (7) | `83% .006 90` | `32% .009 260` | decorative separators only: card and surface hairlines |
| `--border-control` | `62% .01 90` | `52% .012 260` | **mandatory** on every input, select, text field, checkbox, radio and unfilled button outline — the only border token that carries meaning, gated at ≥3:1 by 09-A11Y-005 |
| `--border-strong` (8) | `74% .008 90` | `42% .01 260` | focus ring outer where it measures ≥3:1 against both neighbours; otherwise 09-A11Y-002 substitutes `--accent` |
| `--accent` (9) | `55% .16 250` (white text) | `72% .13 250` (text `16%`) | Send, primary button, credited underline, progress bar |
| `--accent-hover` (10) | `50% .16 250` | `76% .13 250` | |
| `--accent-subtle` | `95% .03 250` | `26% .04 250` | credited wash, selected chip |
| `--text-muted` (11) | `48% .01 90` (≥Lc 60 on step 2) | `70% .01 260` | metadata, hints |
| `--text` (12) | `20% .012 90` (≥Lc 90) | `94% .005 260` | |
| `--success` | `60% .14 150` | `72% .12 150` | Secure/Exam-ready pip fill, "Got it", ✓ glyph |
| `--warning-fill` | `75% .15 80` (text on it `20%`) | `80% .13 80` | **surfaces only**: warning banner fill, Shaky pip fill behind its outline |
| `--warning-line` | `52% .13 75` | `78% .13 80` | **every line and every text use**: uncredited dotted underline, ER blockquote rule, provisional-badge text, command-word-mismatch text |
| `--danger` | `58% .19 25` | `70% .16 25` | destructive confirm, × glyph, offline banner icon; never a heatmap tone |
| `--highlight-source` | `90% .14 95 / .35` | `85% .14 95 / .40` | cited span in the reader |
| `--highlight-select` | `88% .10 250 / .30` | `70% .10 250 / .35` | user text selection in reader |
| `--scrim` | `0% 0 0 / .40` | `0% 0 0 / .55` | sheets, overlays |

**09-COLR-002** Mastery pip colours. State is carried by shape (empty ring · ¼ · ¾ · full disc) and colour reinforces: Unseen ring `--border-control`; Shaky ¼ `--warning-fill` inside a 1 px `--warning-line` outline (a 10 px graphic carries meaning, so its outline is gated at 3:1); Secure ¾ `--success`; Exam-ready full `--success` with a 1 px `--text` outline. A decaying Secure pip (retrievability <0.7) renders Shaky by shape and colour together. Acceptance: pips remain distinguishable in a greyscale screenshot.

**09-COLR-003** Heatmap: five tones of one hue — `oklch(93% .03 250)`, `86% .07`, `76% .11`, `64% .14`, `52% .16` (dark: `24% .03 250` → `72% .13`) — mapped to learning events per day; empty day = `--bg-el`. Never red; never a streak count.

**09-COLR-004** Span marking colours: credited = 2 px underline `--accent` + `--accent-subtle` wash; partial = 2 px dotted `--accent`; uncredited attempt = 2 px dotted `--warning-line`; ECF = `--accent` underline with `ecf` mono badge. Hover/focus on any span raises the wash one step and shows the reason.

**09-COLR-005** Contrast+ variant: text 12 → `10% / 98%`, borders +1 step, `--text-muted` → `36% / 80%`, all alphas ×1.3, hairlines 1.5 px. Ships with both themes at launch.

**09-COLR-006** CI runs an APCA/WCAG matrix over every text-role × surface-role pair in light, dark and Contrast+; any pair below 4.5:1 (3:1 for ≥24 px/19 px bold and for UI borders/focus rings) fails the build. The same job enforces the role split: a token that measures below 3:1 on `--bg-app` may not be referenced by a `border-*`, `outline-*`, `text-decoration-color` or `color` declaration anywhere in the stylesheet — which is what confines `--border-subtle`, `--border` and `--warning-fill` to decorative separators and surfaces (WCAG 1.4.11 exempts purely decorative boundaries).

## 09.5 Motion
**09-MOTN-001** Durations and easings are tokens; a lint forbids any `transition`/`animation` that does not use them.

```css
--dur-press: 120ms;   /* :active scale .97, chip select */
--dur-tooltip: 150ms; /* tooltips, selection popover, hover card (120ms allowed for cite card) */
--dur-fast: 180ms;    /* dropdown, popover, toast in */
--dur-base: 220ms;    /* delta fade-in, card expand, tab switch */
--dur-page: 260ms;    /* route transitions (mobile push) */
--dur-drawer: 320ms;  /* sheets, sidebar overlay, right pane open */
--dur-celebrate: 400ms; /* the one two-tone ring fade */
--dur-shimmer: 1600ms;  /* skeleton shimmer loop; off under reduced motion */
--stagger: 40ms;      /* max 6 items staggered */
--ease-out: cubic-bezier(.23,1,.32,1);
--ease-in-out: cubic-bezier(.77,0,.175,1);
--ease-drawer: cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1);
```

Springs are not CSS. `motion.ts` exports exactly one, `springGesture = { type: 'spring', duration: 0.5, bounce: 0.15 }`, used only by the drag-to-dismiss handler; the animation-registry lint of 09-QA-023 reads `tokens.css` and `motion.ts` together, and any spring outside that export fails it.

**09-MOTN-002** Rules: animate only `transform`, `opacity` and `clip-path`; entrances start at scale .96 (never 0) from `transform-origin` at the trigger; exits run ~20% faster than entrances; elements that move together share duration and easing; hover effects only under `(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine)`; no transition exceeds 320 ms except the single 400 ms celebration.

**09-MOTN-003** Zero-animation actions (an animation here is a defect): Send/Stop swap, message append, command palette open/close, any keyboard-initiated action, tab switch in the right pane, mode-chip change, pip state change, flashcard button press, timer ticks, sidebar row selection.

**09-MOTN-004** Forbidden: `ease-in` on UI, `scale(0)`, bounce or overshoot outside drag-to-dismiss, pulsing or gradient "thinking" backgrounds, typing-cursor blink faster than 1 Hz, any shimmer loop not driven by `--dur-shimmer`, parallax, auto-playing loops, confetti, particles, mascots, loss animations, layout-affecting animations (height/width/margin).

**09-MOTN-005** Reduced motion (`prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, or forced in Settings): all transforms removed; opacity/colour fades only at ≤160 ms; skeleton shimmer off; sheets appear in place; flashcard flip becomes a 150 ms crossfade; the celebration becomes a 400 ms colour change. Acceptance: Playwright run with reduced motion records no element whose bounding box changes during any transition.

**09-MOTN-006** Haptics (mobile): light impact on card flip and Got it; selection tick on keypad keys; none on Again, on Marks or on any tutor error — the product never punishes.

## 09.6 Iconography
**09-ICON-001** One outline family (Lucide), 24 px grid, 2 px stroke, 1 px optical padding; 16 and 20 px optical variants for dense rows; icon colour = the text colour of its row; never two families on one surface; no filled variants except the Send button glyph and the active mode segment.

**09-ICON-002** Marking glyphs are typographic, never emoji: ✓ (credited), × (uncredited), Ø (nothing creditable), BOD, ECF, NC, CON, RE, GA, I rendered as 16 px `--font-mono` 600 margin badges; ✓ uses `--success`, × `--danger`, the rest `--text-muted`. Every glyph has a tooltip and `aria-label` with the full phrase ("Error carried forward").

**09-ICON-003** Trust badges are text badges (Syllabus · Endorsed textbook · Mark scheme · Examiner report · Revision notes · Your notes) at `--text-xs` 500 caps, `--bg-el` fill, radius 4, never coloured by rank. Provenance chips (OWN · LICENSED · USER) appear only in Library and teacher views.

**09-ICON-004** Emoji are banned from system copy, icons and model output (lint, 09-COPY-006). The only permitted symbols are typographic (· — → ✓ × ▸ ↓ ¼ ¾ ∑).

## 09.7 Component inventory and states
**09-COMP-001** Build the components below as one package (`@margin/ui`) on shadcn primitives under the token skin [founder default, E13/E15 — §15.G]. Every component ships with every state listed, a Storybook story per state, and an axe test. States use the shared vocabulary: `default · hover · focus-visible · active · disabled · loading · streaming · error · offline · gated · empty · selected`.

| Component | Anatomy (px) | States and rules |
|---|---|---|
| **Composer** | 56 rest → 8 lines; padding 12/16; radius 12; hairline border; focus ring step-8. Left: `+` (36) and **ModeChip**. Right: **EffortDial** (28, Quick · Think · Deep, ↗ badge when maths auto-escalates), mic (v1, hidden at MVP), camera, **Send** (36, the only filled button; becomes **Stop** in place, same size). | `text` (Aa) · `math` (MathLive field, 5×4 keypad / 10×4 landscape, 24 px live preview line, decimal comma accepted) · `camera` (see 10.8) · `voice` (v1). `streaming` disables Send only; typing continues. `offline`: Send → "Will send" queue tag. `gated` (levels-Mark quota): camera and Mark mode show reason tooltips, Learn stays enabled. Slash menu (`/learn /practise /mark /quiz /paper /card`). Quiet token hint appears only in the last 15% of a session budget. |
| **ModeChip** | segmented pill 32 h, 13 px 500 labels, 3 segments; active segment `--bg-el-active` + `--text`; inactive `--text-muted` | remembered per Course; keyboard `⌘1/2/3`; never hidden; on mobile the same pill. |
| **IntentRow** | 3 outline chips 32 h, radius pill, gap 8, under the tutor's Turn | rendered only while a gradable Item is open; **Just the answer** `disabled` before one attempt with tooltip "After one attempt."; keys `⌥1 ⌥2 ⌥3`, which fire from anywhere including the focused composer (bare digits are reserved for the ConfidenceSlider and card grading, the only contexts where focus is not in a text field — 09-KEYS-001); disappears when the Attempt closes. |
| **Turn (assistant)** | text on canvas, no bubble; ≤120 words; four-line order right → diagnosis → move → hand-back (behavioural) | `streaming` (cursor block 2 px `--accent`, 1 Hz) · `linted` (regenerated silently) · `template` (fallback after second lint fail, tagged for telemetry) · `error` inline card. Hover/focus reveals MessageActions (copy, retry, explain simpler, quiz me, show in book) as 12 px icons in 24 px targets; on touch a single ⋯. |
| **UserMessage** | `--bg-el` block, radius 16, max 80% width; edit → branch pill "2/3 ◂ ▸" | `queued` (offline) · `edited` · `branch`. **Edit trigger** — desktop: hover or focus reveals a 16 px pencil in a 24 px target, plus `⌘↑` for the last message; touch: a 400 ms long-press or the ⋯ affordance opens a sheet with Edit · Copy · Branch. Every MessageAction reachable by hover on desktop is reachable by long-press and by the ⋯ sheet on touch (09-A11Y-008). |
| **MemoryChip** | 13 px `--text-muted` line under the Turn that caused the write; two text buttons | rendered whenever a Turn writes a memory fact: "Remembered: {fact}" with `Edit` and `Forget`. It is part of the transcript, not a toast, and does not disappear; `Forget` removes with a 30 s Undo and writes the tombstone of 10-SETT-003. |
| **ThinkingRow** | 13 px muted "Thought for 6 s ▸", collapsed, no animation | expands to summary text only; never a live token stream by default. |
| **HelpLadder** | 5 rungs 8×8 stacked vertically in the message margin, gap 6; current filled `--accent`, lower dotted, higher `--border`; "More help" 28 h button + `⇧↓` | tooltips: What is it asking? · Which principle? · Next step · Fill the blank · Full solution. Rung 5 shows a **Seen solution · retest Thu** tag afterwards. Resets per Item. |
| **EffortGateChip** | "Your go first" tag on the Item card with optional 90 s time-box | `open` · `satisfied` (attempt/plan/"don't know + one sentence") · `timeboxed` (tabular countdown, no colour change at zero). |
| **ProbeCard** | 3–5 items, each with **ConfidenceSlider** (Sure · Think so · Guessing, 3-segment control 36 h) | required before reveal; no praise strings; results feed calibration. |
| **MarkHeader** | question id · tariff · command word · AO split; command-word check line beneath | `streaming` (header paints first) · `mismatch` (line in `--warning-line` text, rendered from the interpolated catalogue string `mark.command_word.mismatch` = "{Command} — a judgement is required. None found, so {ceiling_ao} cannot reach Level {n}.", with `ceiling_ao` and `n` taken from the Pack's `CommandWord@1.ao_ceiling`; no AO number is ever a literal, because 9708 and 9702 Packs have no AO4). |
| **AOCard** | label "AO3 Analysis" · "Level 2 of 3 · 4/6" · descriptor relied on (our paraphrase) · quoted spans list · confidence dot | `skeleton` (fixed height until its card streams, ≤8 s each) · `credited` · `capped` · `provisional`. Distinguished by label, never hue. Points papers: M/A/B rows with `ecf` badge. |
| **SpanHighlight** | inline range in the answer per 09-COLR-004 | hover/focus shows reason + marking-point id; click scrolls the AOCard into view (and back). Keyboard: Tab through spans. |
| **BandTotal** | "11–13 / 20", modal 600, range 400 tabular; **CalibrationBadge** 12 px beneath | `gated` ("Calibrated on 9609 P2 · agreement 0.82 · 312 scripts", vendor chip after) · `provisional` (badge in `--warning-line` text, band shown) · `exact` (points Mark whose every marking point is SymPy-verified: "4 / 5 · exact", no range, `exact` badge — 09-PRIN-007) · `uncalibrated` ("Practice estimate — not yet calibrated for this paper", no number, spans and feedback only) · `human-check` ("Sent for human check"). |
| **ERQuote** | blockquote, 2 px `--warning-line` left rule, series badge "9609 m25 ER", ≤40 words, deep link | attributed extract only (see §06). |
| **CitationChip** | `--cite-h` 20, numeral `--text-xs` (12/1.35) 600 tabular, 2 px inline padding, radius 6, `--bg-el`/`--text`, after the sentence, 24 px hit area | `pointer: fine` — hover/focus card in 120 ms (title, page, 2–3-line excerpt with span bold, trust badge, Open); click opens SourcePane at the page ≤400 ms p95. `pointer: coarse` — tap opens the SourcePane sheet at the 50% detent directly (there is no hover card on touch); long-press shows the excerpt card. `unavailable` (licence) → extract + deep link. |
| **SourcePane** | 40 px tab strip Source · Scratchpad · Artifact · Cards; page header with page number, prev/next citation arrows; PDF at DPR with selectable text layer | `rail` (48 px, citation-count badge) · `open` · `loading` (page skeleton) · `low-confidence` ("Verify against the PDF" badge) · `conflict` (notes-vs-canon card). |
| **SelectionPopover** | 150 ms after mouseup / long-press; Ask · Explain · Define · Save as card · Quote | keyboard `⌘⇧A/E/D/S/Q`. `⌘⇧A` is the same binding as the global Ask chord, disambiguated by whether a selection exists (09-KEYS-001), not a second registration of it. |
| **CourseTree** | rows 32, indent 16, 12 px caps headers +0.06 em, pip 10 at row start, mark-yield badge right | `active` (`--bg-el`, radius 6) · `filtered` (⌘K) · `collapsed`; Recent and Due are secondary groups below the tree. |
| **MasteryPip** | 10 px; shapes per 09-COLR-002 | tooltip "¾ — 2 correct unaided, last seen 6 days ago; decays to ¼ on 20 Sep without practice". State change without animation. |
| **ReadinessRing** | 64, stroke 4, `--accent` arc, reason text beneath | `decaying` · `ready` (single 400 ms two-tone fade, once) · hidden in exam week. |
| **TodayCard** (Continue · Due · Next) | 3 cards, title 14 px 500, one 13 px line, one action | `empty` shows work ("Start with a probe on 3.2"), never a greeting. |
| **PlanBlock** | 25/45-min blocks, label = syllabus point + type; drag with ⋯ › Move alternative | `proposed` (dashed border) · `accepted` · `done` · `declined-late` (sleep line). |
| **FlashcardReview** | face centred 20 px, images ≤60% h; buttons **Again** (neutral-destructive) / **Got it** (primary) 50%×56; 3 px progress bar; interval hints 12 px | `flip` (`--dur-base` `--ease-out`; 150 ms crossfade under reduced motion) · `offline` · `end` (three lines, no animation). Hard/Easy behind a setting. Provenance line on every card. |
| **Heatmap** | 12 px cells, 3 px gap, five tones | tooltip minutes + topics; no streak count. |
| **Timer** | tabular 20 px; extra-time % and rest-break button inherited from Course | `running` · `paused-rest` · `overtime` (colour unchanged; label "+02:14"). No sound; no red. |
| **UsageMeter** | session + weekly bars, plain numbers "2 of 3 levels Marks today" | `grace` — plan-conditional and rendered from `limits.yaml`, never hard-coded: Free reads "Exam week: 6 levels Marks a day until {last_paper_date}", paid reads "Exam week: no marking limit until {last_paper_date}" · `slowed`. |
| **Toast** | bottom-left desktop / top mobile, 4 s, one Undo | never stacks more than 2; never for errors that belong inline. |
| **InlineError** | card in place of the failed Turn: cause, primary ("Retry with Think"), secondary ("Copy my message") | never a modal. |
| **Banner** | `--banner-h` 40 single-line at ≥900 px, `--banner-h-2` 56 two-line below; sticky under the top bar | at most **two** stacked (offline and quota genuinely co-occur); a third collapses the older pair into a "2 notices ▾" row; dismissible except offline. |
| **Skeleton** | matches final geometry and radius; shimmer `--dur-shimmer`, off under reduced motion | lists, PDF pages, AOCards, decks; spinners only for <1 s confirmations. |
| **Sheet** (mobile) | handle 36×5, detents 50/92%, `--ease-drawer` 320, tap-to-close scrim | drag has a tap alternative; pinch-zoom inside a PDF disables sheet drag. |
| **CommandPalette** | input 36, rows 32, radius 6, keycaps 20/radius 4, no shadow beyond hairline | zero animation; `Esc` closes; focus returns to composer. |
| **EmptyState** | one sentence + one action; no illustration larger than 64 px, no mascot | per screen in §10. |

Acceptance for the whole inventory: Storybook shows every state; visual regression on light/dark/Contrast+ at 320, 768, 1280 and 1600 px; every interactive element ≥24 px (44 on touch).

## 09.8 The streaming contract
Every streamed surface (Turn, Mark, debrief, generated Item) obeys this contract; it is tested in CI against 20 recorded golden streams with synthetic TTFT of 0.3, 1.5, 3 and 8 s.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 09-STRM-001 | Optimistic echo: the user message appears, composer clears, scroll pins, and a three-dot indicator occupies the assistant slot within 50 ms of Send. | Performance trace: paint ≤50 ms after keydown. |
| 09-STRM-002 | At 800 ms without a first token the dots become one 13 px muted status line drawn from the route ("Reading the 9609 P2 mark scheme…", "Checking the working with SymPy…"); text, never a spinner or pulsing background. | Synthetic 1.5 s TTFT shows the line at 800±50 ms. |
| 09-STRM-003 | At 6 s: "Taking longer than usual" plus Stop; at 20 s the InlineError offers "Retry with Think" and "Copy my message". | Synthetic 8 s and 25 s traces. |
| 09-STRM-004 | Tokens are buffered and painted per frame at ≤90 characters per 16 ms; bursts are paced. | No frame paints >90 new characters. |
| 09-STRM-005 | DOM above the stream cursor is immutable: earlier paragraphs, lists and tables are never re-laid out; markdown is committed block by block. | CLS measured above the cursor = 0 on all golden streams. |
| 09-STRM-006 | Unclosed `$$…`, ``` fences, tables and AOCards hold a skeleton of the expected height until closed; inline `$…$` renders only when closed. | No visible raw LaTeX or fence markers at any frame. |
| 09-STRM-007 | Auto-scroll stops after 120 px of user scroll; a "↓ New" pill (32 h, `--bg-el`, hairline) appears bottom-centre; tapping it re-pins. | Scroll up 150 px during a stream: no yank; pill visible. |
| 09-STRM-008 | Stop replaces Send in place (same position, same 36 px); the partial Turn stays, tagged "Stopped" and carrying the Continue control of 09-STRM-014; no animation on the swap. | Screenshot diff of the button box before/after. |
| 09-STRM-009 | Thinking renders as a collapsed non-animated "Thought for 6 s ▸" row; expanding shows a summary, never a live token stream by default. | Story state. |
| 09-STRM-010 | The lint (§04) runs before paint; a rejected draft regenerates without any visible artefact; the template fallback paints as a normal Turn. | Golden stream with an injected "Great question!" shows no flash of the rejected text. |
| 09-STRM-011 | Mark streaming order: MarkHeader → one AOCard each within 8 s → BandTotal + CalibrationBadge → next-mark advice; spans light in the answer as their card commits. | Recorded Mark stream: header ≤2 s p95, each AO card ≤8 s p95, full ≤45 s p95. These three are the only stream budgets for a Mark; the 90 s landing→Mark figure of 10.1 is a funnel median, not a stream budget, and no section states either as a p50. |
| 09-STRM-012 | Screen readers: the completed Turn is announced once via `role="log" aria-live="polite"`; never per token. Status lines are announced once each. | NVDA/VoiceOver script. |
| 09-STRM-013 | Resumable: on reconnect the client resumes the SSE stream from the last part id; the partial Turn is never duplicated. | Kill the network at 40% of a golden stream; final DOM equals the uninterrupted golden. |
| 09-STRM-014 | A stopped Turn is not terminal. Beside its "Stopped" tag it renders a **Continue** text button for 10 minutes; Continue resumes from the last committed part with the partial re-sent as the prefix (cache-friendly, §08.3) and **appends** to the same Turn, while Retry (`⌘⇧R`) discards the partial and re-runs. Continue and Retry each count as one metered Turn; the partial alone counts as none. | Continue-after-stop on a golden stream produces a DOM equal to the uninterrupted golden, and the continuation call reports `cache_read_input_tokens > 0`. |
| 09-STRM-015 | Interrupt-and-add (03-PAR-004): sending a new message during a stream cancels the current part, keeps the partial Turn with `interrupted: true`, and re-injects it to the model as a `<partial>` block so the next Turn continues rather than restarting the explanation. Interrupted partials are excluded from the praise-audit and leak-audit denominators. | Interrupt at 40% of a golden stream: the partial persists tagged, the next Turn contains no repeated opening sentence, and the audit job's denominator is unchanged. |
| 09-STRM-016 | Cross-device resume: a Session's active stream resumes on any of the user's signed-in devices from the last committed part id, so a Mark started on a laptop continues on the phone that took over via the QR hand-off (10.8). | Start a Mark on client A, open the Session on client B at 50%: B paints the committed parts and receives the remainder; A shows the same final DOM with no duplication. |

## 09.9 Reading-comfort settings
**09-READ-001** One preference object, stored per user (synced) and applied by CSS custom properties on `:root`, honoured by chat, Mark view, reader, Cards, artifacts and print:

```json
{ "family": "inter|hyperlegible|opendyslexic|serif|system",
  "size_px": 17, "leading": "1.4|1.55|1.7", "tracking_em": "0|0.05|0.12",
  "measure": "narrow|medium|wide", "warm_bg": false,
  "preset": "none|comfort|dyslexia", "align_start_only": false,
  "theme": "system|light|dark", "contrast_plus": false, "reduce_motion": "system|on",
  "reduce_transparency": false, "read_aloud_rate": 1.0 }
```

**09-READ-002** Controls (Settings › Reading comfort, also reachable from the reader header and ⌘K "Reading comfort"): family radio with live specimen in each face; size stepper 14–24 px (chat and reader scale together; UI text does not scale below 14); leading and tracking three-segment controls; measure narrow 56 ch / medium 68 ch / wide 75 ch. **Two one-tap presets** under one control, because they serve different readers and each is asserted by its own fixture:

| Preset key | Family | Size / leading / tracking / measure | Also sets |
|---|---|---|---|
| `preset.comfort` | unchanged | 18 px / 1.7 / +0.05 em / 60 ch | warm background `oklch(96% .012 85)` |
| `preset.dyslexia` | `--font-dys` (the radio offers `--font-hyper` as the alternate) | 18 px / 1.6 / +0.12 em / 66 ch | warm background, `reduce_motion: on`, `align_start_only: true` (enforces `text-align: start`; justified text never renders) |

Selecting a preset writes `preset` and the fields it implies; changing any single control afterwards sets `preset: "none"` and keeps the values. Every control has a one-line consequence beneath it.

**09-READ-003** Read aloud: every Turn, Card and reader page has a Read-aloud action (system TTS; 0.8–1.5× rate); math is spoken via MathML + lazily loaded SRE ("x squared plus 3 x"); code is read with punctuation. Acceptance: read-aloud of a Turn with an inline fraction produces the SRE string, not "dollar frac".

**09-READ-004** Size and family changes apply without reload and without layout shift outside the text itself; measure is enforced by `max-width: var(--measure)` on the text column, not by the pane width. Why: dyslexic and ADHD students name busy screens and dual-column text as the reason they cover half the browser (student-voice §9); a switcher beats a single "dyslexia font" (Wallace et al. 2022).

## 09.10 Keyboard map and command-palette verbs
**09-KEYS-001** Global map (macOS ⌘ = Ctrl elsewhere). **This table is the authoritative shortcut map for the whole product; no other section publishes a chord.** Every shortcut is listed in the `⌘/` sheet and rebindable in Settings › Shortcuts; no single-letter global fires while a text field is focused, which is why every chord that must work from inside the composer carries a modifier.

| Keys | Action | Keys | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| `⌘K` | Command palette | `⌘/` | Shortcut sheet |
| `⌘1 ⌘2 ⌘3` | Mode Learn · Practise · Mark | `⌥1 ⌥2 ⌥3` (IntentRow visible) | Teach me · Check my steps · Just the answer |
| `⌘J` | Toggle right pane (rail ↔ open) | `⌘⇧J` | Cycle pane tabs |
| `⌘B` | Toggle sidebar | `⌘⇧M` | Math keypad |
| `⌘⇧P` | Photograph / upload | `⌘E` | Effort dial cycle |
| `Enter` / `⇧Enter` | Send / newline | `Esc` | Stop stream; else close topmost layer; else focus composer |
| `⇧↓` | Next help rung | `⌘↑` | Edit last user message (branches) |
| `⌘⇧C` | Copy last Turn | `⌘⇧R` | Retry last Turn (discards a stopped partial) |
| `⌘⇧B` | Branch this Turn | `⌘⇧K` | Scratchpad |
| `⌘.` | Rewrite weakest paragraph (Mark view) | `⌘⇧L` | Another like this |
| `Space` / `1` / `3` | Flip / Again / Got it (review) | `[` `]` | Previous / next citation |
| `⌘F` | Find in reader; in a Session, find-in-Session (scoped to this Session's Turns, `↵`/`⇧↵` walk hits) | `⌘⇧S` | Save selection as Card |
| `⌘,` | Settings | `⌘⇧N` | New Session (same Course) |
| `⌘⇧A` | Ask (10.26): with a text selection, ask about the selection; with none, a new Ask | `⌘⇧O` | Move this Ask to a Course (10.26 only) |
| `Tab` (Mark view) | Move through spans | `Alt+←` | Back (route stack) |

Bare `1`/`2`/`3` are bound only where focus is not in a text field: the ConfidenceSlider (10.6) and card grading (10.12). A chord may be route-scoped (`⌘⇧O` exists only on `/ask`, `⌘.` only on the Mark view); a route-scoped chord resolves to no handler elsewhere and is asserted absent there, which is not the same as being bound twice. No double-press chord exists anywhere: `Esc` has exactly the cascade above and nothing is triggered by pressing a key twice. Acceptance: the shortcut-registry unit test fails when any chord appears twice in the map, when a §03 parity row has no entry here, or when a bare single-letter binding is declared outside the two contexts named.

**09-KEYS-002** Focus rules: `:focus-visible` only; ring = `0 0 0 2px var(--bg-app), 0 0 0 4px var(--border-strong)` (3:1 against both neighbours); `scroll-padding-bottom` ≥ composer height so the sticky composer never hides a focused element (WCAG 2.4.11); `Esc` always restores focus to the element that opened the layer; after a Turn completes, focus moves to the composer (the hand-back) unless the user is typing elsewhere.

**09-KEYS-003** Command palette (`⌘K`, <150 ms to first results, zero animation). Verb-first fuzzy index across Sessions, Cards, syllabus points, Papers, files, Settings and Sources. Canonical verbs and argument types:

```
Ask <anything>                Learn <syllabus point>          Practise <point|paper|"5 more like this">
Mark <clipboard|photo>        Quiz me on <point>              Open paper <code> <series> <component>
Open <source> page <n>        Explain simpler | deeper        Show me in the book
Save as card                  Start timed section <paper>     Plan this week | Move block
Readiness review              Reading comfort                 Switch Course <name>
New Session                   Find in this Session <text>     Memory: forget <fact>
Export <receipt|cards|session>  Challenge this Mark           End session
Teacher: open class <code>    Go to <screen>                  Toggle dark mode
Sign out
```

Rows show verb, argument, a 12 px context line ("3.2 Motivation · ¾ · due Thu") and a keycap. Recent verbs first; `>` prefix forces verb mode; `?` prefix searches help. Acceptance: each verb has a unit test that it resolves to the right route with the right payload.

## 09.11 Microcopy voice and the copy lint
**09-COPY-001** Voice: an examiner who is on your side. Short, specific, declarative; examiner vocabulary explained in plain words on first use; the mark scheme's own terms ("developed", "application", "judgement") used exactly. No exclamation marks, no emoji, no "Great question", no therapy-speak, no guilt, no urgency framing. Numbers carry their basis. The system never speaks about its own feelings and never apologises more than once.

**09-COPY-002** Canonical strings (do / don't). **§15.E is the string catalogue of record**: every row below exists there under the `key` given here, and the renderer reads §15.E, never this table. This table is the lint-fixture corpus — the "Do" column is the shipped string and the "Don't" column is the counter-example the copy lint must reject. A row whose `key` is missing from §15.E, or whose §15.E text differs byte-for-byte from the "Do" column, fails the catalogue test.

| # | `key` | Situation | Do | Don't |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `gate.prompt` | Effort Gate | "Have a go first — a plan or the first line is enough." | "Let's try it together! What do you think the answer might be? 😊" |
| 2 | `intent.answer.locked` | Just the answer, before attempt | "After one attempt." | "Sorry, I can't give you the answer yet!" |
| 3 | `retest.scheduled` | Seen-solution tag | "Seen solution · retest {weekday}." | "Solution revealed — don't forget to review!" |
| 4 | `mark.command_word.mismatch` | Command-word check | "{Command} — a judgement is required. None found, so {ceiling_ao} cannot reach Level {n}." (slots from `CommandWord@1.ao_ceiling`) | "Great analysis! Just remember to add a conclusion for top marks!" |
| 5 | `mark.span.assertion` | Uncredited span reason | "Assertion without a link in the chain — L1, not L2." | "This point could be developed a bit more." |
| 6 | `mark.er.quote` | ER quote | "Examiners' report, m25: 'simply repeating the business name will not count as application.'" | "Examiners hate it when you do this!" |
| 7 | `mark.calibration.gated` | Calibration badge | "Calibrated on 9609 P2 · agreement 0.82 · 312 scripts." | "AI-powered accurate grading." |
| 8 | `mark.calibration.uncalibrated` | Below-gate Mark | "Practice estimate — not yet calibrated for this paper. Feedback and evidence shown; no mark." | "Estimated score: 14/20 (may be inaccurate)." |
| 9 | `notify.cards_due` | Notification | "18 cards due · about 6 minutes." | "Don't lose your progress! Review now 🔥" |
| 10 | `limits.grace.free` / `limits.grace.paid` | Exam-week grace | Free: "Exam week: 6 levels Marks a day until {last_paper_date}." Paid: "Exam week: no marking limit until {last_paper_date}." Both render the number from `limits.yaml` for the viewer's plan. | "Exam week: marking limits are off until Friday." (false on Free, which has 6/day) |
| 11 | `honesty.quote_unverifiable` | Quote unverifiable | "I can't confirm that quote from the set text, so I won't use it." | "Here's a quote that might be from the text." |
| 12 | `weekly.card` | Weekly card | "4 of 4 sessions. 3.2 Motivation moved to ¾. Paper 2 readiness unchanged: no timed practice this week." | "Amazing week! You're on fire! Keep the streak alive!" |
| 13 | `mastery.decayed` | Mastery decay | "3.4 dropped to ¼ — last unaided practice 19 days ago." | "Oh no, you're losing mastery in 3.4!" |
| 14 | `mark.remark.delta` | Re-mark delta | "+3 · AO4 L1→L2." | "Wow, huge improvement! 🎉" |
| 15 | `limits.reached` | Quota reached | "3 of 3 levels Marks used today. Points marking and Learn continue; the next levels Mark is at 00:00, or tonight on Student." | "You've hit your limit! Upgrade now to keep going!" / any variant naming a price |
| 16 | `system.offline` | Offline | "Offline. Cards work; Marks and new Turns will send when you're back." | "Oops! Something went wrong with your connection." |
| 17 | `distress.night` | Distress at 1 a.m. | "That sounds like a hard night. Your last three Marks went up. Tonight: sleep. Tomorrow's first block is 25 minutes on 3.2. If it feels worse than exam stress, here is someone to talk to." | "Don't worry, you've got this! Take a deep breath and let me explain calmly." |
| 18 | `coursework.refusal` | Coursework request | "I can't write this for you — it would be submitted. I can mark your draft against the criteria and log what we did." | "As an AI, I'm not able to help with that request." |
| 19 | `empty.cards` | Empty Cards | "Cards appear here from your Marks and highlights." | "No cards yet! Start studying to fill this up!" |
| 20 | `teacher.override.saved` | Teacher override | "Override saved: 6 → 8, reason 'application credited'. Both marks kept for calibration." | "Grade updated successfully!" |
| 21 | `stream.stopped` / `stream.continue` | Stopped Turn (09-STRM-014) | "Stopped." / "Continue" | "Response interrupted — sorry about that!" |
| 22 | `memory.written` | A Turn wrote a memory fact | "Remembered: prefers worked examples in maths." | "Memory updated ✅" |
| 23 | `incognito.banner` | Incognito Session | "Incognito — nothing here reaches your Learner Graph. Kept 30 days for safety review, then deleted." | "Incognito — nothing from this session is saved." (false; 30-day retention exists, 03-PAR-009) |
| 24 | `ask.scope` | Ask scope line (10.26) | "Ask — general help. Not marked, not grounded in a syllabus, and not counted as study." | "Ask me anything! 🙌" / any wording implying the answer is exam-grounded |
| 25 | `ask.move_offer` | Ask is asked to mark, card or plan (10-ASKS-003) | "I can mark this properly against {syllabus_code}'s scheme — move this to your {course_name} Course and I'll mark it there." | "I can't mark things here." (a refusal; 00-NON-001) |

Row 4 supersedes SYNTHESIS §10 canonical string 4 ("…so AO4 is capped at Level 2"): the command word sets which AOs are on offer and therefore an AO's **ceiling**, it does not set a level inside an AO that is on offer (§05.4), and three of the launch Packs have no AO4 at all. Row 10 supersedes SYNTHESIS §10 canonical string 9 for the same reason — SYNTHESIS §12's own free-tier rule is 6 levels Marks a day in grace, so "limits are off" is a limit claim the product does not honour.

**09-COPY-003** Copy lint runs on the string catalogue (CI) and on model output before paint (§04/§08). Rules, each a regex or classifier with a fixture: (a) exclamation marks: 0 allowed; (b) emoji and pictographic symbols: 0; (c) person-praise lexicon ("great", "amazing", "brilliant", "well done", "good job", "you're a natural", "genius") outside a quoted span: block; (d) guilt/loss lexicon ("don't lose", "streak", "missed", "you failed to", "falling behind", "last chance"): block; (e) urgency ("now!", "hurry", "only today"): block; (f) therapy-speak ("take a breath", "it's okay to feel", "I hear you"): block in tutor and system copy, allowed once in the distress script; (g) "Great question", "Absolutely", "Certainly", "As an AI": block; (h) hedged marks ("might be", "roughly") in BandTotal: block — the band is the hedge; (i) unlabelled estimates: any grade or mark string must carry a basis token (series, n, or "practice estimate"); (j) vendor names in headlines: block (chip only); (k) price literals: no currency amount or plan price may appear in any in-product notice, banner, tooltip or model output — prices render only in Settings › Usage › Plan and on the pricing page, so a limit notice names the plan, never the number; (l) literal AO numbers in catalogue strings: block — an AO is always a slot filled from the Pack (`{ceiling_ao}`), because 9708, 0455 and 9702 Packs have three AOs. Thresholds: praise rate on the weekly 1% Turn audit <5%; catalogue violations = 0.

**09-COPY-004** Placeholders name the action: after a Turn the composer placeholder is the hand-back ("Try the next line", "Name the stakeholder first", "Paste your rewrite"); default placeholder "Ask, paste an answer, or /practise".

**09-COPY-005** Errors state cause and remedy in ≤2 sentences with no apology theatre: "The marker timed out. Retry with Think, or copy your answer." Never "Oops".

**09-COPY-006** Localised strings follow the same lint per language; Russian and Kazakh catalogues are reviewed by a native examiner-teacher for register (formal "вы", no diminutives).

## 09.12 Accessibility gates
WCAG 2.2 AA is a set of numbers, and every number below is a CI gate (09-PRIN-013). The table is the contract; the prose after it covers the four flows that automated tools cannot judge (streaming, Mark view, math, timers). Tooling: `axe-core` runs on every Storybook story and every route in the Playwright suite; custom checks live in `@margin/a11y-checks`; Lighthouse accessibility score ≥98 on every route; a manual audit (NVDA + Firefox, VoiceOver + Safari macOS and iOS, TalkBack + Chrome) runs on the 12 flows of 09-A11Y-014 before every release and monthly in between. A failing CI row blocks merge; a failing manual row blocks release.

| ID | Criterion | Requirement | Acceptance | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 09-A11Y-001 | Target size (2.5.8) | Every pointer target ≥24×24 CSS px (`--target-min`); ≥44×44 on `pointer: coarse` (`--touch-min`). **Inline exception (WCAG 2.5.8 "inline"):** targets that sit inside a line of running text — text links and CitationChips — are exempt from the 44 px box on coarse pointers and are asserted against a 24 px spacing circle instead; a CitationChip carries that 24 px hit area via padding, never via a transparent overlay, and gets the coarse-pointer behaviour of 09-COMP-001 so a phone still reaches the source. Nothing outside a text line may claim the exception. | Custom check walks every `button, a, [role=button], input, [tabindex]`, asserts the bounding box, and applies the spacing-circle rule only to elements whose computed `display` is inline inside a text block; run on every story at 1280 and on the touch profile at 390. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-002 | Focus appearance (2.4.13, treated as AA) | Ring per 09-KEYS-002; outer 2 px band must measure ≥3:1 against **both** the element surface and the page surface in light, dark and Contrast+. Where `--border-strong` fails on a given surface, the ring outer colour for that surface is `--accent` (the token matrix decides at build time; no per-component overrides). | Contrast matrix computes ring-vs-surface for every surface token; Playwright screenshots a focused control on every surface and measures the ring width ≥2 px on all four sides. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-003 | Focus not obscured (2.4.11) | `scroll-padding-bottom` ≥ composer height + 16 px on every scroll container; sticky top bar and tab strip declare `scroll-padding-top`. | Tab through 40 controls in a Session with the composer at 8 lines; every focused element's box intersects the viewport by ≥100%. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-004 | Reflow (1.4.10) | No horizontal page scroll at 320 px CSS width and at 400% zoom on a 1280 px window; only data tables, code blocks and PDF pages scroll internally, each inside its own `overflow-x: auto` container with a visible scrollbar. | Playwright at 320×568 and at 1280 with `deviceScaleFactor` 4 / zoom 400%: `document.scrollingElement.scrollWidth ≤ clientWidth` on every route. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-005 | Contrast (1.4.3, 1.4.11) | Body and UI text ≥4.5:1; text ≥24 px or ≥19 px bold ≥3:1; **the gated boundary token is `--border-control`**, which every input, select, text field, checkbox, radio and unfilled button outline must use, at ≥3:1 against adjacent colours — as must icons carrying meaning, focus rings, span underlines (`--warning-line`, `--accent`) and the Shaky pip outline; placeholder text ≥4.5:1 (it is copy, 09-COPY-004). Purely decorative hairlines (`--border-subtle`, `--border`: table rows, list dividers, card edges) are the stated exemption under 1.4.11 and are confined to that role by 09-COLR-006. | 09-COLR-006 matrix plus a rendered-DOM pass (axe `color-contrast`) on every story in three themes. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-006 | Use of colour (1.4.1) | No state conveyed by colour alone: mastery pips by shape (09-COLR-002); span states by underline style **and** wash **and** glyph — credited = solid underline + wash, partial = dotted, uncredited = dotted + trailing 12 px × glyph (09-ICON-002); heatmap cells carry a tooltip count; band modal mark by weight; timer overtime by the "+" prefix; offline by the banner text. | Greyscale screenshot suite: every state pair remains distinguishable (perceptual diff ≥ 5 ΔE between states after desaturation). | CI |
| 09-A11Y-007 | Text spacing and resize (1.4.12, 1.4.4) | Per 09-TYPE-007; plus 200% browser zoom and iOS Dynamic Type "AX3" without clipping, overlap or loss of function; UI text never below 14 px after scaling. | Text-spacing injection + 200% zoom on every route; iOS snapshot at AX3 for composer, Cards and Mark view. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-008 | Dragging (2.5.7) | Every drag has a single-pointer alternative: PlanBlock ⋯ › Move; sheets tap-to-close and a "Expand" button; crop handles have "Full page · Top half · Bottom half" buttons; sidebar/panel resize has Settings › Layout width fields; flashcard swipe has the two buttons. | Story audit: every component with `draggable` or pointer-move handlers has a documented alternative that a keyboard script completes. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-009 | Reduced motion (2.3.3) | Per 09-MOTN-005; `prefers-reduced-transparency` removes backdrop blur and raises scrim alpha to .70. | Playwright reduced-motion run: zero bounding-box change during any transition. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-010 | Language of parts (3.1.2) | `lang` on the root follows the UI locale; every quoted passage in another language (Kazakh syllabus wording, a Russian student answer inside an English UI) carries its own `lang`; model output segments detected in another script are wrapped by the renderer. | Unit test on the renderer with mixed-script fixtures; axe `valid-lang`. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-011 | Name, role, value (4.1.2) | Every icon-only control has an `aria-label` from the string catalogue; marking glyphs carry the full phrase (09-ICON-002); the ModeChip is `role="radiogroup"`; the ConfidenceSlider is `role="radiogroup"` with three radios (never a range input); HelpLadder rungs are a `role="listbox"` with the current rung `aria-selected`; the EffortDial is a `role="radiogroup"`. | axe + a snapshot of the accessibility tree per story compared against a committed baseline. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-012 | Status messages (4.1.3) | Non-focus-moving updates use live regions: stream status lines `role="status"`; InlineError `role="alert"`; toasts `role="status"`; the "↓ New" pill `aria-live="polite"` text "New content below"; pip state changes are **not** announced (they are visible on hover/focus with a reason). | Story-level assertion that each component renders the required role. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-013 | Timing (2.2.1, 2.2.2) | Auth idle timeout ≥30 days sliding, never mid-Turn; toasts hold 4 s but every toast action is also available for 30 s via `⌘Z` or the ⋯ menu; the Effort-Gate 90 s time-box can be extended by 90 s once per Item without conditions; timed papers: extra time 0 / 25% / 50% / custom 1–100% and rest breaks are Course settings set **before** start and inherited by every Timer (§05.9), the rest-break button pauses the clock, and a paused paper survives reload; no auto-advance on timer end — the paper locks input and shows the debrief button. | Playwright: set 25% on a 90-minute paper → timer starts at 112:30; pause 3 min, reload, resume: elapsed preserved ±1 s; `⌘Z` 20 s after an Undo toast reverts. | CI |
| 09-A11Y-014 | Keyboard (2.1.1, 2.1.2, 2.4.3) | Twelve flows complete with keyboard only, visible focus at every step, no trap, logical order: (1) Landing → First Mark; (2) Setup sheets; (3) Today → Session; (4) a Learn Turn with a ladder climb and intent chip; (5) Practise Item with confidence + submit; (6) Mark view span traversal → Rewrite → re-mark; (7) Cards review 10 cards; (8) Paper runner 3 parts with the keypad; (9) Plan: move a block; (10) Settings › Reading comfort change; (11) `⌘K` verb → route; (12) Challenge a Mark. | One Playwright script per flow using only `keyboard.press`; a screenshot per step with the focus ring visible is attached to the run. | CI + manual (SR) |
| 09-A11Y-015 | Captions and transcripts (1.2.x) | Voice input (v1) shows a live transcript before send; Read-aloud never removes the text; any audio or video content in Library carries captions or a transcript, enforced at ingestion (§06). | Ingestion rejects media without `transcript_id`. | CI |

### Screen-reader flow for streaming Turns
**09-A11Y-016** The Session transcript is one `role="log"` `aria-live="polite"` `aria-relevant="additions"` region. Each Turn is a `role="article"` with `aria-label` "Tutor, turn 7" / "You, turn 6"; the article is inserted into the log **only when its `done` part arrives** (09-STRM-012), so a screen reader hears the finished Turn once, never token by token. While streaming, the pending article lives outside the live region with `aria-busy="true"`. Status lines (800 ms, 6 s) are one `role="status"` element whose text is replaced, so each is announced once. Stop is a normal button labelled "Stop response"; on Stop the partial article enters the log with the suffix "— stopped". The composer regains focus at `done` unless focus is inside another text field (09-KEYS-002); the placeholder is exposed via `aria-description`, so the hand-back is heard. Intent chips are a `role="group"` "Reply options" of three buttons with `aria-keyshortcuts="Alt+1"/"Alt+2"/"Alt+3"` matching 09-KEYS-001; the disabled Just-the-answer chip is `aria-disabled` with its tooltip as `aria-description` ("After one attempt."). A MemoryChip written by the Turn is inside the Turn's article, so it is read as part of the finished Turn and never announced separately. The HelpLadder announces "Help level 2 of 5, Which principle?" and "More help" carries `aria-keyshortcuts="Shift+ArrowDown"`. Acceptance: NVDA script on a golden stream produces exactly three utterances — the status line, the finished Turn, the composer placeholder — and with focus in the composer, `⌥2` selects "Check my steps" while a bare `2` types the character.

### Screen-reader flow for Mark view and spans
**09-A11Y-017** Reading order is header → command-word line → BandTotal with CalibrationBadge → AO cards → the answer. Before the answer a visually hidden summary reads "Your answer with 3 credited, 1 partial and 2 uncredited passages. Press Tab to move between them." Each SpanHighlight is a `<mark>` with `tabindex="0"`, `role="mark"` (fallback `aria-roledescription="marked passage"`), an accessible name "Credited, AO3 Level 2, marking point 3b-2" and `aria-describedby` pointing at the reason text; Enter on a span moves focus to its AOCard, Enter on the card's "Show in answer" moves it back. Two skip links at the top: "Jump to AO cards" · "Jump to answer". The band is read as "11 to 13 out of 20, most likely 12", never as three bare numbers. ER quotes are `<blockquote>` with `cite`. Acceptance: VoiceOver rotor lists every span under Landmarks › marks; a blind tester (manual audit) can state the band, the capped AO and the first uncredited reason within 90 s on a 20-mark fixture.

### Math read-aloud
**09-A11Y-018** KaTeX renders with `output: "htmlAndMathml"`; the HTML layer is `aria-hidden`, the MathML layer is exposed. Speech Rule Engine loads lazily (first Read-aloud, first `⌘⇧M`, or when Settings › Accessibility › "Describe maths for screen readers" is on) and generates ClearSpeak strings into `aria-label` on the `<math>` element ("x squared plus 3 x over 2"); the expression explorer (step into numerator/denominator with arrow keys) is available on focused display math. Every keypad key has an `aria-label` from the catalogue ("fraction", "square root", "raise to power"). Locale: SRE English for `en`; `ru` speech rules in v1, `kk` falls back to Russian with the fallback stated in Settings. Acceptance: the 200-formula suite (09-PRIN-017) produces a non-empty, non-LaTeX label for every formula; "dollar", "frac" and "backslash" never appear in a label.

### Split of responsibility
**09-A11Y-019** CI owns 09-A11Y-001…015 as automated rows. The manual audit owns: screen-reader comprehension of Turn, Mark view and Cards; switch-access completion of flows 1, 5, 7; a five-person reading panel (two dyslexic, two ADHD, one low-vision) per quarter rating comfort on a 1–5 scale with target ≥4 and no item ≤2; and a cognitive walkthrough of every new empty/error state. Findings are filed with a severity: **blocker** (a flow cannot be completed), **major** (completed with workaround), **minor**; a release ships with zero blockers and zero majors on flows 1–7.

Why: students with extra-time entitlements say the timer and rest breaks are the difference between a usable and an unusable mock (student-voice §"Accessibility across the year"); dyslexic users cover half the browser to cope with dense layouts (student-voice §9) — reflow and reading comfort are the same requirement viewed twice.

## 09.13 Dark-mode rules
Dark mode is a second, complete palette that remaps the semantic roles of 09-COLR-001, not a filter. Every rule here is checked by the token test and the visual-regression suite in dark and Contrast+ dark.

**09-DARK-001** Mode selection. Three states in Settings › Appearance and in `⌘K` "Toggle dark mode": `system` (default; follows `prefers-color-scheme`, switches live without reload), `light`, `dark`. The choice is stored in the reading-preference object (`theme`, 09-READ-001) and mirrored to `<html data-theme>` before first paint from a cookie/localStorage read in a blocking inline script ≤300 bytes, so no flash of the wrong theme. Contrast+ is an orthogonal boolean that applies to whichever theme is active. Acceptance: reload 20 times in dark; zero frames with `--bg-app` light captured by the Playwright video.

**09-DARK-002** Ladder and no pure black. The five dark surfaces are the ladder of 09-COLR-001 (`16% → 19% → 23% → 26% → 29%` L, chroma .006–.008, hue 260). No dark token has L <14%; `#000`, `black` and `oklch(0% …)` are forbidden outside `--scrim`. Elevation is expressed by stepping up the ladder, not by shadow strength: a floating layer sits on the surface one step above its parent (popover on `--bg-el`, sheet on `--bg-subtle`+1 relative to the page), and `--shadow-2…4` keep their geometry but alpha is halved in dark (they exist to separate, not to lift). Hairline in dark is `oklch(100% 0 0 / .08)`. Acceptance: token test asserts min L and no forbidden literals; screenshot of a stacked popover-over-sheet-over-page shows three distinct surface luminances measured ≥2% L apart.

**09-DARK-003** Text and accent remaps. Text `94%` (never 100%: pure white on dark grey halos), muted `70%` (≥4.5:1 on all five surfaces — CI matrix), accent lifted and desaturated to `72% .13 250` with `16%` text on it, `--accent-subtle` `26% .04 250`. Semantic hues drop 2–3 points of chroma in dark (success `72% .12 150`, `--warning-fill` `80% .13 80`, `--warning-line` `78% .13 80`, danger `70% .16 25`) — bright saturated colour on grey vibrates. Credited underline stays `--accent`; the credited wash uses `--accent-subtle` at full opacity, not a light-mode alpha over dark. Acceptance: matrix passes; no dark token has chroma >.16.

**09-DARK-004** Highlight and selection alphas. `--highlight-source` `85% .14 95 / .40` (light .35), `--highlight-select` `70% .10 250 / .35` (light .30); both are applied as a **layer above** the text with `mix-blend-mode: normal`, so highlighted text keeps ≥4.5:1 (the matrix tests text-on-highlight-on-surface as a composite). Heatmap dark ramp `24% .03 250 → 72% .13 250` (09-COLR-003).

**09-DARK-005** Images, diagrams and PDF pages. PDF pages stay paper-coloured (rendered as-is) on a `--bg-subtle` surround with a 24 px gutter and a hairline — inverting a mark scheme is a defect. Product diagrams shipped as SVG use `currentColor` and semantic tokens and re-theme automatically; raster diagrams and student photos are shown unaltered with a 1 px hairline frame; user-uploaded images never receive `filter: invert()`. An optional per-document "Dim page" toggle in the reader header applies `filter: brightness(.85)` to the page canvas only (persisted per user). Charts (Progress, calibration curve) draw from tokens and are snapshot-tested in both themes. Acceptance: reader screenshot in dark shows a light page on a dark surround; no `filter: invert` in the stylesheet.

**09-DARK-006** Code blocks and math. Code uses one syntax theme per mode from tokens (background `--bg-subtle`, hairline, text `--text`, keywords `--accent`, strings `--success`, comments `--text-muted`), never a third-party theme with its own background; inline code inherits the surrounding surface +1 step. KaTeX inherits `currentColor`; fraction rules and radicals are drawn in `--text`, never in a hardcoded black. Acceptance: grep the bundle for `#282c34`, `#1e1e1e`, `#000` and other theme literals → 0.

**09-DARK-007** Print from dark. Print (09.14) always uses the light palette regardless of screen theme: `@media print` resets `data-theme` tokens to light values; highlights print as underline styles plus wash at 15% grey; the scrim, banners, rail and composer are removed. Acceptance: print preview from a dark Session is visually identical to print preview from light (pixel diff <0.5%).

**09-DARK-008** Focus, borders and states. Focus ring in dark: inner `--bg-app` 16%, outer `--border-strong` 42% or `--accent` per 09-A11Y-002; decorative borders `27/32%`, `--border-control` 52% on every input and unfilled control, `--border-strong` 42%; disabled = `--text-muted` at .6 opacity plus a hairline, never a darker fill (it disappears); skeleton shimmer runs from `--bg-el` to `--bg-el-hover`. Selected sidebar row `--bg-el` + `--text` (not accent fill).

**09-DARK-009** Tests. (a) Token test: every light role has a dark role; L and chroma bounds; no literals. (b) Contrast matrix in dark and Contrast+ dark (09-COLR-006). (c) Visual regression of every Storybook state in dark at 320/768/1280/1600. (d) Live-switch test: toggle system theme during a stream — no reflow, no re-render of committed Turns (09-STRM-005), the stream continues. (e) A 10-person night-use panel at 30% screen brightness rates legibility ≥4/5 on the Mark view and reader.

Why: pure black with saturated accents is the most-cited dark-mode failure (ux-design §19); students study late (student-voice §"21:40 on a school night", J3), so dark mode is a primary theme, not a bonus.

## 09.14 Print and export
Print is a first-class surface: teachers file Marks, students revise from paper, and the Learning Receipt (§12) exists to be handed over. Four print templates ship at MVP; each has a Playwright print-to-PDF test on A4 and Letter in both orientations where relevant.

**09-PRNT-001** Common print stylesheet. `@media print` and the PDF export share one stylesheet: light palette forced (09-DARK-007); body `--font-serif` at 11 pt / 1.4 unless the user's reading-comfort family is Hyperlegible or OpenDyslexic, which are honoured (09-PRIN-018); 2 cm margins; measure = full column; page numbers "3 / 7" bottom-right and the document title bottom-left in 9 pt; links printed as underlined text with the URL in 8 pt after them only in the Receipt; math via KaTeX SSR with fonts embedded; images at ≤300 dpi source, never upscaled; `orphans: 3; widows: 3`; `break-inside: avoid` on AOCards, Cards, ER quotes, tables ≤12 rows and code blocks ≤40 lines; a table >12 rows repeats its `<thead>` on each page. Colours: credited = solid underline + 15% grey wash; partial = dotted; uncredited = dotted + ×; the CalibrationBadge prints in full. Acceptance: printing a 20-mark Mark to A4 yields ≤3 pages with no AOCard split across a page.

**09-PRNT-002** What is stripped. Composer, sidebar, rail, tab strip, banners, toasts, hover cards, IntentRow, HelpLadder, MessageActions, skeletons, scrim, the "↓ New" pill, usage meter, any `aria-hidden` decoration, and the celebrate ring animation frame. Citation chips print as superscript numerals with a numbered "Sources" list at the end (title · page · trust badge · deep link text). Turn branches print the active branch only. Acceptance: DOM audit in print emulation finds no element with `data-print="strip"` rendered.

**09-PRNT-003** Templates.

| Template | Trigger | Contents and layout | Page rules |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Mark** | Mark view › ⋯ › Print / Export PDF; teacher console bulk export | Header (Course, paper, question id, tariff, command word, date, model chip, policy version); command-word line; BandTotal + badge; the student's answer with spans **first** (full width), then AO cards in a two-column grid, ER quote, next-mark advice; footer non-affiliation line (09-PRNT-006). Teacher override state prints both marks and the reason. | Portrait; answer never split mid-paragraph from its first span; AO cards `break-inside: avoid`. |
| **Learning Receipt** | Settings › Export; Receipt screen (§10.23) | Cover block: student (display name only), assignment, Course, date range, signed hash (SHA-256, first 16 hex + QR to the verification URL); timeline table (timestamp · event · rung · Mark · seen-solution flag); appendix of drafts with diff bars (added = solid left rule, removed = struck). Public-link variant strips learner state (mastery, calibration, confidence). | Portrait; timeline `<thead>` repeats; hash also in every footer. |
| **Cards** | Deck › ⋯ › Print | 3×3 grid of 63×88 mm cards per A4 (2×4 on Letter), face on odd pages and backs mirrored on even pages for duplex; provenance line 7 pt; cloze printed with a 24 mm rule; math SSR. Option: "One column list" (face · back rows) for revision sheets. | Landscape grid; cut marks 0.2 pt at grid intersections; `break-after: page` per sheet. |
| **Timed paper** | Papers › Start timed mock › "Print instead" (OWN and LICENSED Items only; `ref_only` boards print the official deep link, never text) | Board-style front page (paper code, series label "Margin practice · not a board paper", minutes, marks, extra-time note if set); questions with tariff in the right margin `[6]`, answer lines at 8 mm pitch sized to tariff (≈2 lines per mark, capped at 40), inserts on separate pages; a mark-scheme appendix only when "Include scheme" is ticked, starting on a new page. | Portrait; a question part never starts in the last 25% of a page; answer lines never split from their question stem. |

**09-PRNT-004** PDF export fidelity. Server-side rendering (headless Chromium with the same stylesheet) so the PDF equals print preview: text is selectable and searchable, MathML is embedded as tagged structure (PDF/UA tagging for headings, lists, tables, alt text on images = the accessible name used on screen), fonts embedded and subset, file ≤2 MB for a Mark and ≤10 MB for a Receipt with images. Exports carry document metadata (title, author = student display name, subject = Course, creation date, `margin:export_version`). Anki `.apkg` export of Cards keeps LaTeX and images and writes provenance into a `source` field. ICS export of the Plan (§10.15) and Markdown/DOCX of artifacts (§10.17) follow the same strip rules. Acceptance: pixel diff between browser print preview and server PDF <1% per page on the four fixture documents; `pdfinfo` shows tagged = yes; VoiceOver reads the PDF headings.

**09-PRNT-005** Watermarks. Handwritten-answer images print with a 9 pt "Original image — kept for teacher review" caption, not a diagonal watermark. Below-gate Marks (`uncalibrated`) print "Practice estimate — not yet calibrated for this paper" as the band position and print no number; `provisional` prints the badge. A Mark exported by a student for a teacher carries no watermark; a public Receipt link prints "Public copy — learner state removed". Nothing is ever watermarked "DRAFT" or "CONFIDENTIAL" in a way that fails 3:1 with body text beneath it.

**09-PRNT-006** Non-affiliation notice. Every printed or exported page footer includes, in 8 pt: "Margin is not affiliated with or endorsed by Cambridge International Education or any examination board. Marks are practice estimates, not board marks. Syllabus and mark-scheme references are to the board's published documents." The board name is substituted from the Pack; the string is in the catalogue and localised. Acceptance: string present in every fixture footer; copy lint passes on it.

**09-PRNT-007** Print from mobile. Mobile routes expose "Export PDF" (server render), not `window.print()`, since mobile print dialogs drop the stylesheet; the PDF opens in the system share sheet. Acceptance: iOS Safari export of a Mark produces the same PDF hash as desktop for the same fixture.

## 09.15 Internationalisation
Margin launches with an English UI and English, Russian and Kazakh **explanation** languages; the Russian and Kazakh **UI** ship in v2 (SYNTHESIS §13). Internationalisation is built in from the first commit, so v2 is a catalogue and a font check, not a refactor.

**09-I18N-001** Locale model. Three independent settings in one object, stored per user and overridable per Course:

```json
{ "ui_locale": "en-GB",
  "explain_lang": "en|ru|kk",
  "exam_variety": "en-GB",
  "number_locale": "en-GB|ru-RU|kk-KZ",
  "decimal_comma": false,
  "timezone": "Asia/Almaty",
  "calendar": "gregory", "first_day": "monday",
  "units": "metric" }
```

`ui_locale` drives the catalogue; `explain_lang` is passed to the tutor route as a system-prompt variable (the syllabus terms stay in the exam's language and are glossed, e.g. "маржинальная прибыль (*contribution*)"); `exam_variety` fixes spelling and vocabulary in anything the student will write in an exam; `number_locale` and `decimal_comma` drive `Intl` and the keypad. Default detection: browser `navigator.languages` for `ui_locale`, Course board for `exam_variety`, `explain_lang` asked once in setup (10.2). Acceptance: a `kk-KZ` browser user on a Cambridge Course sees an English UI at MVP with Russian/Kazakh available for explanations, `exam_variety` en-GB, decimal comma on.

**09-I18N-002** Glyph coverage. CI renders the fixture "Ә Ғ Қ Ң Ө Ұ Ү Һ І — Қазақстан, Өскемен; ёж, щука, ъ; Ў ў Ғ ғ Ҳ ҳ" plus "∑ ∫ ≤ ≥ ≠ → ⇌ ° µ ½ ¾ ‰ £ ₸ ₽" in every family of 09-TYPE-001 (Inter, Atkinson Hyperlegible Next, OpenDyslexic, Literata → PT Serif, Geist Mono → JetBrains Mono) at 400/500/600 and fails on any glyph drawn by a fallback font (detected by `document.fonts` + canvas glyph-bounds comparison against the fallback face). Where a family lacks a glyph (OpenDyslexic lacks parts of extended Cyrillic; Geist Mono's Cyrillic is unverified), the family declares a per-script fallback in `@font-face unicode-range` so the fallback is chosen per glyph and looks intentional: OpenDyslexic → Atkinson Hyperlegible Next for U+0400–04FF; Geist Mono → JetBrains Mono for Cyrillic. Subsets shipped: latin, latin-ext, cyrillic, cyrillic-ext, greek, math; total font payload ≤380 KB woff2 for the default UI path. Acceptance: zero fallback glyphs in the test; the Settings specimen line renders each Kazakh letter in the chosen family or its declared fallback.

**09-I18N-003** String catalogue rules. Every user-visible string lives in `catalogue/<locale>.json` keyed by a stable id (`mark.band.aria`, `gate.chip.label`); no string literals in components (ESLint `no-literal-jsx-text` + a runtime check in dev that throws on unkeyed text). ICU MessageFormat for every plural, select and number: `"{n, plural, one {# card due} other {# cards due}}"`; Russian and Kazakh use `one/few/many/other` and CLDR handles Kazakh's `one/other`. Placeholders are named, never positional; no sentence is assembled from fragments; no string is reused across contexts with different grammar (a button "Mark" and a noun "Mark" are two keys). Each key carries `description` and `max_length` for the translator; keys over their length fail the catalogue test.
