Copy lint (09-COPY-003) runs per locale with a per-language lexicon; formal "вы" and no diminutives (09-COPY-006). Acceptance: catalogue test finds 0 missing keys per shipped locale, 0 unused keys, 0 plurals without all CLDR categories.

**09-I18N-004** Typography per script. Serif reading face must have true Cyrillic italics (Literata does; PT Serif as fallback) — a synthesised oblique in a Russian textbook quote is a defect. Cyrillic body runs ≈8–10% wider than English: measures are in `ch` of the active family, so 68 ch holds; the UI must survive **+35% string length** (Russian and Kazakh averages) and **+50%** in buttons without truncation or overflow — labels wrap to two lines inside `--control-h-lg`, never ellipsise. Caps labels at +0.06 em tracking are permitted in Cyrillic; small caps are not (poor Cyrillic support). Acceptance: pseudo-locale run (09-I18N-008) at +35%/+50% shows no clipped text and no horizontal overflow on any route.

**09-I18N-005** Numbers, dates, units and math input. All formatting through `Intl.NumberFormat` / `Intl.DateTimeFormat` / `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat` with `number_locale`; marks and bands keep the exam's convention ("11–13 / 20" with an en dash, never a hyphen); percentages follow locale ("12,5 %" in ru-RU with a narrow no-break space); currency in Business questions stays as printed in the Item ("$", "£", "₸") — never converted; dates default to "Thu 18 Sep" (short weekday + day + short month), 24-hour clock in ru/kk, 12-hour only if the OS says so; countdowns "243 days" via plural rules. Math input accepts both `.` and `,` as decimal separators and normalises to LaTeX `.` on submit while the preview line shows the locale form; thousands separators (space in ru-RU) are stripped on parse; the keypad's decimal key shows the locale glyph. SI units per the Pack's convention (Cambridge sciences: "m s⁻¹" in print, `m/s` accepted). Acceptance: 40-case formatter fixture per locale; entering `3,5` in the keypad yields `3.5` in the Attempt and "3,5" in the preview under ru-RU.

**09-I18N-006** Exam-board spelling and vocabulary. `exam_variety` selects a spelling dictionary and glossary used by the tutor, the generator and the copy lint on model output: Cambridge/AQA/OCR/Pearson/IB(UK English) → en-GB ("organisation", "programme", "labour", "maths"); College Board/AP → en-US; a future Australian board → en-AU. The tutor never "corrects" a student's spelling toward another variety. Board term maps travel with the Pack (`Pack.glossary`: "trade payables" vs "creditors", "revenue" vs "sales turnover", "mark scheme" vs "scoring guidelines"). Acceptance: the model-output lint flags en-US spellings in a 9609 Course at ≥95% recall on a 200-word fixture; 0 corrections of student spelling in the Turn eval.

**09-I18N-007** RTL readiness. Logical properties only (`margin-inline-start`, `inset-inline-end`, `text-align: start`); stylelint bans physical left/right in layout properties except inside `[dir]`-scoped math and code containers. Icons with direction (chevrons, back arrow, send, "↓ New" is vertical and exempt) are mirrored via `[dir=rtl] { transform: scaleX(-1) }` on a `data-mirror` allowlist; math, code, PDFs, timers, marks ("11–13 / 20") and citation numerals are never mirrored (`dir="ltr"` + `unicode-bidi: isolate`). Tab order follows visual order in RTL. No RTL locale ships before v2, but the smoke test runs from MVP. Acceptance: `dir="rtl"` pseudo-locale screenshot suite on every route shows mirrored chrome and un-mirrored math; 0 physical-side properties reported by stylelint.

**09-I18N-008** Pseudo-locales in CI. Three built-in pseudo-locales run on every Storybook story and route: `en-XA` (accented, +35% length, brackets to expose concatenation: "[Ḿâŕķ ţĥîš âñšŵéŕ one two]"), `en-XL` (+50%, buttons and chips only), `ar-XB` (RTL mirrored, Latin text reversed). Failures: any string not wrapped in brackets (a literal), any overflow, any clipped ring or chip, any mirrored math. Acceptance: pseudo-locale job green on every PR.

**09-I18N-009** Russian and Kazakh UI (v2 scope, prepared now). Catalogue keys, plural categories, per-script fallbacks and the +35% layout tolerance are in place at MVP; v2 adds the two catalogues, native examiner-teacher review (09-COPY-006), Kazakh SRE speech (09-A11Y-018), Kazakh `Intl` data checks (Kazakh month names, `kk-KZ` decimal comma), and the Latin-script Kazakh alphabet as a display option when the state transition is stable (founder-owned; default: Cyrillic only). Every date and number surface is already locale-driven, so the v2 change is data, not code. Acceptance (v2): the 12 keyboard flows of 09-A11Y-014 pass in `ru-RU` and `kk-KZ` with no English fallback strings visible.

## 09.16 Pixel-level QA checklist (release gate)
This checklist is the release gate: every row runs on the release candidate, every row has a pass criterion and a named tool, and the release ships only when all rows are green or a founder-signed waiver names the row and the fix date. Rows marked **CI** run on every PR as well; rows marked **manual** are executed by a reviewer on the RC build and recorded in the release note. Tools: `axe` = axe-core in Playwright; `LH` = Lighthouse; `PW-diff` = Playwright screenshot diff against committed goldens (threshold 0.1% pixels unless stated); `PW-script` = Playwright assertion; `stylelint`/`eslint` = the lint rules of §09; `lint-copy` = the copy lint of 09-COPY-003; `manual` = human on the RC.

### Typography
| # | Check | Pass criterion | Tool |
|---|---|---|---|
| 09-QA-001 | Body and reader sizes | Chat Turns computed 17 px/1.55; reader and Mark answer 18 px/1.6; Card face 20 px | PW-script (`getComputedStyle`) · CI |
| 09-QA-002 | Measure | No paragraph wider than 68 ch in chat, 66 ch reader, 75 ch anywhere, at 1280/1600/2560 | PW-script · CI |
| 09-QA-003 | Minimum size | No rendered text <12 px; captions ≥12 px | PW-script · CI |
| 09-QA-004 | Headings in Turns | No `h1`/`h2` inside an assistant Turn; max 20 px/600 | PW-script on 20 golden Turns · CI |
| 09-QA-005 | Tabular numerals | `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` on Timer, BandTotal, countdowns, due counts, numeric table cells; a 09:59→10:00 tick shifts no neighbour | PW-script + PW-diff · CI |
| 09-QA-006 | Glyph coverage | Cyrillic/Kazakh/math fixture renders in-family for all five families | 09-I18N-002 job · CI |
| 09-QA-007 | Text-spacing and 200% | Text-spacing injection and 200% zoom: no clipping/overlap; no `scrollWidth > clientWidth` except tables/code/PDF | PW-script · CI |
| 09-QA-008 | Weights | Only 400/500/600 in the rendered tree (700 only on the modal mark) | PW-script · CI |

| 09-QA-011 | Grid and persistence | `280px 1fr 480px` at 1280; sidebar/panel widths persist across reload; panel close does not widen the measure; at 1600 and 2560 the `wide` variant of 09-SPCE-005 renders two panes (440 + 480) on Mark view and Reader and one pane everywhere else | PW-script · CI |

| 09-QA-018 | No literals, no unknown tokens | 0 raw colour literals outside `tokens.css`; 0 `#000`; no dark token L <14%; **every `--token` name referenced anywhere in §09 or §10 resolves in `tokens.css`** — an unknown name (e.g. `--fg-muted`, `--bg-accent`) fails the docs-lint as well as stylelint | stylelint + token test + docs-lint · CI |

**09-QA-061** Release procedure. The RC build runs the CI subset (all rows except 046 and 053) as one pipeline stage named `pixel-gate`; the manual rows are executed from a checklist issue template that links each row to its ID; the release note lists any waived row with owner and date. A waiver is not available for rows 016–022, 028–035, 048–052 or 056.

# §10 Screen-by-screen specification

## 10.0 Conventions used in this section
§10 specifies the 25 canonical screens of SYNTHESIS §6 one by one, in the order of that list, each against the tokens and components of §09, followed by two surfaces the rest of the document depends on and that list does not name: **10.26 Ask** (the Course-less conversation referenced by §00, §03 and 10.3) and **10.27 Public paper finder** (the free-forever finder that SYNTHESIS §12 makes distribution channel 3 and §14.4 gives an engineering owner). Screens are numbered 10.1–10.27; requirement IDs are `10-<SCR>-NNN` where `<SCR>` is the four-letter screen tag in the table below. Nothing in a screen spec redefines a token or a component; it names the component from 09.7 and the state it is in.

**10-CONV-001** Per-screen template. Every screen subsection contains these blocks in this order; a missing block is a spec defect.

| Block | Content |
|---|---|
| **Purpose** | One sentence: the learning outcome the screen exists to produce. |
| **Stage** | `MVP` or `v1`, and where a screen splits, which regions belong to which. Required on every screen: an unstaged screen is built at MVP by default, which is a spec defect if that was not intended. A docs-lint asserts one `**Stage.**` line per §10 subsection. |
| **Jobs served** | Row ids from §02.2 (J1–J12) and, where relevant, the exam-year moment (02-MOM-*). |
| **Route** | Path pattern, params, and what deep-linking to it requires. |
| **Layout** | Desktop (≥1280): which of the three grid columns (`280px | 1fr | 480px`) the screen occupies and the right pane's state (rail 48 px, open 480, or absent). Tablet (900–1279): `1fr 420px` with sidebar overlay. Mobile (<900): one column; which surfaces become bottom sheets (detents 50/92%). |
| **Regions and elements** | A table of regions → exact elements (component name from 09.7, size token, copy key). |
| **Wireframe** | ASCII at desktop width; a second at mobile width when the mobile layout differs structurally. |
| **States** | `empty · loading · streaming · error · offline · gated/quota`, each with the exact copy and the primary action. A state that cannot occur is listed as "n/a: reason". |
| **Interactions and shortcuts** | Pointer, keyboard (from 09.10), touch gestures, and the drag alternatives. A screen whose interaction model has genuinely distinct modes may split this block into named sub-blocks (10.10's `Timer` / `Submit`, 10.12's `Review interaction`), provided the screen's keyboard shortcuts appear in one of them; the block may not simply be absent. |
| **Data** | Reads and writes by object: Learner Graph (mastery, calibration, memory), Pack (syllabus tree, Items, schemes), Attempt/Mark, Plan, Card, Session, policy object. Writes name the field. |
| **Acceptance** | Criteria a reviewer verifies, in one of exactly two permitted forms: a numbered list, or a `| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |` table whose ids are `10-<SCR>-NNN`. The table form is preferred wherever a criterion is referenced from another section, because a numbered list gives it no id. Performance budgets are cited from the §11.14 route table, never restated as numbers. |

**10-CONV-002** Right-pane rail behaviour (applies to 10.5, 10.6, 10.7, 10.16, 10.17). Default state on every fresh Session is the 48 px rail: a vertical strip with the four tab icons (Source · Scratchpad · Artifact · Cards, 20 px optical) and a citation-count badge (`--text-xs` tabular) under Source. It opens to `--panel-w` on: the first citation-chip click, the first artifact card, any Mark (Mark view opens it to the cited scheme line), the Reader, `⌘J`, or a tab-icon click. It collapses on `⌘J`, the rail toggle, or `Esc` when focus is inside it and no layer is open. Opening does not change the centre measure (the centre column is fixed at 68 ch and centred in `1fr`); the transition is `--dur-drawer` on `transform` only. On tablet portrait the same content is a bottom sheet at the 50% detent; on mobile it is a full-height sheet at 92%. The open/closed state persists per Course. Acceptance: fresh Session screenshot shows exactly two panes and a 48 px rail; a chip click opens the pane within one `--dur-drawer` with the centre paragraph width unchanged.

**10-CONV-003** Routes. All routes are under one SPA shell; the browser back button walks the route stack and, on mobile, the top-bar back arrow does the same. Course-scoped routes carry the Course id so a shared link opens the right Pack version.

| Screen | `<SCR>` | Route | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Landing / First Mark | `LAND` | `/` | anonymous; `?q=<sample_id>` pre-fills a sample |
| Ask (Course-less conversation) | `ASKS` | `/ask/:sessionId` | 10.26; writes no Attempt, Card or memory |
| Public paper finder (logged out) | `FIND` | `/papers` · `/papers/:board/:subject/:series/:component` | 10.27; no account, deep links only |
| Course setup | `SETU` | `/start` · `/start/:step` | six required steps `save · course · papers · date · level · consent`, then optional `import` (10.2) |
| Home / Today | `HOME` | `/home` | default route for a signed-in user |
| Course home | `CRSE` | `/c/:courseId` | tabs `/learn /papers /cards /plan /progress` |
| Session | `SESN` | `/c/:courseId/learn/:sessionId` | `?turn=<turnId>` scrolls to a Turn |
| Practise runner | `PRAC` | `/c/:courseId/practise/:runId` | `?item=<n>` |
| Mark view | `MARK` | `/c/:courseId/marks/:markId` | anonymous first Mark: `/m/:markToken` (24 h) |
| Handwriting capture | `CAPT` | `/capture/:token` | phone hand-off; token bound to a Session or run |
| Papers · Paper runner · Debrief | `PAPR` · `RUNR` · `DBRF` | `/c/:courseId/papers` · `/paper/:runId` · `/paper/:runId/debrief` | |
| Cards · Wrong-question bank | `CARD` · `WRNG` | `/c/:courseId/cards` · `/c/:courseId/cards/wrong` | review: `/review/:deckId` full-screen |
| Progress · Plan | `PROG` · `PLAN` | `/c/:courseId/progress` (`/paper/:paperId`) · `/c/:courseId/plan` | |
| Library / Reader | `LIBR` | `/library` · `/read/:docId?page=n&span=id` | |
| Scratchpad / Artifact | `SCRA` | `/c/:courseId/artifact/:artifactId` | also a right-pane tab (10-CONV-002) |
| Search / Command palette | `SRCH` | no route (layer over any route) | `⌘K`; deep link `?q=` is not supported by design |
| Settings | `SETT` | `/settings/:section` | `account · appearance · reading · language · memory · notifications · usage · shortcuts · data` |
| Teacher · Parent · Challenge · Receipt · Admin | `TCHR` · `PDIG` · `CHLG` · `RCPT` · `ADMN` | `/t/:classId` · `/p/:digestId` · `/c/:courseId/marks/:markId/challenge` · `/receipt/:receiptId` · `/admin/*` | |
| Distress, safety, system and quota states | `STAT` | no route (rendered in place) | 10.24; `/status` is the only page of its own |

Acceptance for this table: it is generated from the router manifest at build time. A route that exists in code but not here, or here but not in code, fails CI — which is also how §11.14's performance table stays in the same vocabulary, since it reads these route patterns rather than restating them.

**10-CONV-004** Breakpoints and grid, restated from 09-SPCE-005 for use in every layout block: `mobile <900` (one column, 16 px gutters, top bar 44), `tablet 900–1279` (`1fr 420px`, sidebar overlay with 40% scrim, portrait <1024 turns the right pane into a sheet), `desktop ≥1280` (`280px 1fr 480px`, top bar 48), `wide ≥1600` (surplus to centre margins). Container queries (`@container`) drive component variants, not viewport queries, so a Mark view inside the teacher console at 900 px behaves like its tablet layout.

**10-CONV-005** Wireframe legend. Wireframes are drawn at 96 columns for desktop (≈1280 px, 1 col ≈ 13 px) and 40 columns for mobile (≈390 px).

```
┌─┐ └─┘ │ ─   pane or card border (1 px hairline)     ═══  sticky bar (top bar, composer)
[Button]      button; [■Send] = the one filled button   (chip) pill chip; (●Learn|Practise|Mark) segmented, ● = active
⟨tab⟩         right-pane tab; ⟨Source*⟩ = active        ¹ ² ³  citation chips after a sentence
○ ◔ ◕ ●       mastery pips Unseen · Shaky · Secure · Exam-ready     ◯ 64  readiness ring with value
▸ ▾           collapsed / expanded                       ⋯  overflow menu   ↓New  re-pin pill
~~~~ ....     credited (solid) / partial or uncredited (dotted) span      ▒▒▒  skeleton
|rail|        48 px collapsed right pane                 ⌨  keypad   📷 → written as [Camera] (no emoji in product; legend only)
```

**10-CONV-005a** Syllabus numbers in wireframes and fixtures. Every syllabus point number and title shown in a §10 wireframe, example string or test fixture ("3.2.1 Motivation theories") is read from one fixture constants file, `fixtures/syllabus_points.ts`, generated from the Pack at build time — never typed into a wireframe or a test. The file is the reason a number can be corrected in one place when 15-OPEN-010 resolves the 2026–28 numbering from the syllabus PDF. Acceptance: a docs-lint matching `\b\d\.\d(\.\d)?\s+[A-Z]` in §04, §07 and §10 resolves every hit against the constants file and fails on an unknown point; the §07.5 and §10.6 examples resolve to the same title for the same number.

**10-CONV-006** Shared acceptance for every screen (not repeated below): all rows of 09.16 pass on the screen; the screen has an `empty` and an `error` story; the primary action is the only filled button; no string outside the catalogue; Web Vitals per 09-QA-056 where the screen is a landing target; the screen's keyboard flow (09-A11Y-014) is scripted when it owns one.

## 10.1 Landing / First Mark
**Purpose.** Give an anonymous visitor an examiner-grade Mark on their own answer inside 90 seconds, before any account exists, so the first thing Margin does is the thing nobody else does (SYNTHESIS §3.3, §5 bet 2).

**Stage.** MVP, in full. This is the acquisition surface: nothing on it is deferred, including `/m/:markToken` sharing of an anonymous Mark, which is the link a student sends a friend.

**Jobs served.** J1 (know what an examiner would give it and why); 02-MOM-001 (September start). Secondary: J4 for the "just mark it" visitor.

**Route.** `/` (anonymous). A sample chip appends `?q=<sample_id>`. Once a Mark starts streaming the URL becomes `/m/:markToken` (24-hour token, shareable read-only) without a page load.

**Layout.** Desktop: no sidebar, no rail; one centred column, composer 640 px wide with its top edge at 40% viewport height; below it the four sample chips; footer with the non-affiliation line (09-PRNT-006). When the Mark streams, the page becomes the Mark view layout (10.7) in place: answer left, Mark right, right pane open at the cited scheme line. Tablet: same, composer 560 px. Mobile: composer full width above the keyboard-safe area, chips wrap to two rows, Mark view stacks (answer on top).

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Top bar (48) | wordmark (text, 14 px 600) · "Sign in" text button · theme toggle. No nav, no pricing link above the fold. |
| Line | `--text-h2` 24/1.25 · 500: "Paste an answer, or photograph one. We'll mark it like an examiner." (key `landing.line`) |
| Composer | Composer in `text` state, placeholder "Paste your answer here — question text first if you have it" · **+** (file: PDF/DOCX/image) · ModeChip locked to **Mark** with Learn/Practise present but `disabled` (tooltip "After your first Mark") · camera (→ 10.8 anonymous path) · **Send**. |
| Sample chips | four outline chips 32 h: "9609 12-mark evaluation" · "0580 vectors" · "9701 mechanism" · "Photograph your own". A sample chip fills the composer with a real OWN Item question and a stored exemplar answer (Tier 0, mid-band) and skips the board/subject sheet. |
| Two-field sheet | Sheet (desktop: 480 px centred panel, `--shadow-3`; mobile: 50% detent) with **Board** typeahead (MVP: Cambridge International live; AQA/OCR/Pearson/IB listed as "coming" and disabled) and **Subject** typeahead by code or name ("9609 Business"). Two fields, one primary button "Mark it". |
| Footer | non-affiliation line · Privacy · Terms · "For teachers" text link. |

```
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 Margin                                                                     Sign in   ☾
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════



                 Paste an answer, or photograph one. We'll mark it like an examiner.

        ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
        │ Paste your answer here — question text first if you have it                  │
        │                                                                              │
        │ [+] (Learn|Practise|●Mark)                            Quick▾  [Camera] [■Send]│
        └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
          (9609 12-mark evaluation) (0580 vectors) (9701 mechanism) (Photograph your own)



 Margin is not affiliated with or endorsed by Cambridge International Education…   Privacy · Terms
```

**States.** `empty` is the default; there is no other landing content. `loading`: after "Mark it" the composer collapses upward over `--dur-base` and the Mark view skeleton (MarkHeader, three AOCard skeletons, BandTotal skeleton) appears; status line at 800 ms. `streaming`: per 09-STRM-011. `error`: InlineError in the Mark column ("The marker timed out. Retry, or copy your answer."); the pasted text is preserved in the composer. `offline`: composer stays editable; Send shows "Marks need a connection"; nothing is queued anonymously. `gated`: the anonymous allowance is the `anonymous:` block of `limits.yaml` (03-LIM-001) — 2 levels Marks per device per 24 h by a cookie + IP heuristic, 0 Deep Turns, Learn and Practise unavailable, nothing queued offline — and this screen renders that number rather than declaring one; the third attempt shows a one-line card above the composer "Two free Marks used today. Save a Course to keep marking — it takes a minute." with **Save this Course**; Learn/Practise stay disabled anonymously. No paywall copy appears before the first Mark.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Paste (`⌘V`) into the composer; Enter sends; a paste longer than 4,000 words shows "Paste one question's answer at a time" inline and marks the first 4,000 words. Drag-and-drop of an image or PDF onto the composer opens the capture/confirm flow. The sample chip swaps the composer content and shows a 13 px muted line "Sample answer — edit it or paste your own". After the first Mark and the first re-mark, an inline card below the delta (not a modal) reads "Save this Course to keep this Mark, your rewrite and your Cards." with Email · Apple · Google; dismissing it leaves a text link in the Mark header. On token expiry (24 h) the `/m/:markToken` route shows the Mark read-only with the same card. `?` in the empty composer opens the palette help.

**Data.** Writes an anonymous Attempt `{anon_device_id, item_id | user_question_text, response, transcript_confirmed, timestamp}` and its Mark; no Learner Graph, no memory, no Cards until Save (Cards are generated but held on the Attempt). An unclaimed anonymous Attempt and its Mark are deleted 30 days after creation and the device id is not retained beyond that (§11.13); the `/m/:markToken` link stops resolving at 24 h regardless. On Save the Attempts migrate to the account and the Course setup (10.2) starts pre-filled with the board and subject. Reads the Pack for the chosen syllabus (scheme items for the sample Items; command-word table for user questions). Telemetry: `landing_view`, `paste`, `sheet_complete`, `mark_done` (with `ttfm_ms`), `remark_done`, `save_click`.

**Acceptance.**
1. Time from Send to MarkHeader ≤2 s p95 and to `done` ≤45 s p95 on the four sample Items (staging soak).
2. No account, cookie banner, email field or pricing appears before `mark_done`; the only blocking UI before the Mark is the two-field sheet (skipped for samples).
3. Web Vitals per the §11.14 route budget for `/` (this screen states no budget of its own); initial JS ≤220 KB gz, fonts ≤380 KB; Lighthouse performance ≥90 on the mid-tier Android profile.
4. `/m/:markToken` renders the full Mark with spans and band for 24 h to anyone with the link, then the expiry card; no learner state is exposed (there is none).
5. Funnel targets instrumented: paste→full Mark ≥55%, Mark→rewrite ≥70%, Mark→Save ≥35% within the session (SYNTHESIS §5 bet 2).
6. Keyboard flow 1 of 09-A11Y-014 completes; the sample chips are reachable by Tab and announce their subject.

## 10.2 Course setup sheets (onboarding as a flow)
**Purpose.** Turn the anonymous visitor's first Mark into a Course — the home object every Attempt hangs on — with the fewest fields that let the scheduler, the Examiner Twin and the Plan work on day one.

**Stage.** MVP for the six required sheets and the class-code path. Within `import` (optional at every stage): mock photos, Anki/Quizlet and class code are MVP; notes PDF, timetable and another assistant's export are v1 — the flow already treats every import as skippable and background, so a v1 row adds a picker option and nothing else.

**Jobs served.** J3 (highest-value 20 minutes needs a Plan, which needs a date), J8 (an honest estimate needs papers and target), J6 (mock import), 02-MOM-001.

**Route.** `/start` → `/start/:step`, steps in order: `save · course · papers · date · level · consent · import`. Each step is a route so the back arrow and browser Back work and a refresh resumes; progress is held in the session store (server-side once `save` completes, local before). Entry points: the "Save this Course" card (10.1), a class code (`/join/:code`, which pre-fills board, syllabus, papers and exam series from the Class and skips to `level`), the app-store first launch (which runs `course → papers → date` first and then routes to a marked Item before `save`, per SYNTHESIS §3.3 last sentence), and "Add a Course" from Home.

**Layout.** Not modals: a flow of sheets. Desktop: a 480 px panel centred in the viewport on `--bg-app`, `--shadow-3`, with a 12 px caps step line ("Step 2 of 6") and a hairline progress track (2 px, `--accent`) at the top; the previous sheet slides out left and the next in from the right over `--dur-page` on `transform` (reduced motion: crossfade). Mobile: each sheet is a full-screen route with the 44 pt top bar (back · step · skip when allowed). Every sheet has ≤2 fields, one primary button 44 h, and a text-button secondary ("Back" or "Skip"). The account step is one sheet; three Course sheets are required; consent is required by policy; import is optional. Anonymous pre-account onboarding (the two-field sheet of 10.1) is the only sheet shown before the Mark — SYNTHESIS §6's "≤3 sheets" bound applies to that pre-Mark path.

**Sheets.**

| Step | Fields (≤2) | Rules and copy |
|---|---|---|
| `save` | **Age band** (Under 13 · 13–15 · 16–17 · 18+), first field · then Email, or Apple / Google buttons | The age band is asked **before any identifier is collected**, because collecting an email from an under-13 and gating afterwards is the COPPA/AADC failure the gate exists to prevent. Selecting Under 13 routes immediately to the school-only page and no email field is rendered, no `user` row is written, and the anonymous Attempt is discarded within 24 h. Otherwise: magic link; no password at MVP. Line: "Save this Course to keep this Mark, your rewrite and your Cards." |
| `course` | **Board** (pre-filled) · **Syllabus** typeahead "9609 Business (2026–28)" with the version chosen by exam series | Version is shown, never hidden: "Pack 9609 · 2026–28 · v14". Level (AS / A Level / IGCSE) is a property of the syllabus row, not a field. |
| `papers` | **Components** multi-select chips from the Pack (P1 · P2 · P3 · P4; AS-only preset) · **Exam series** segmented (June 2027 · Nov 2026 · March 2027 where the Pack has it) | Per-paper dates fill from the Pack timetable when published; else the series' last day with "date to confirm" and a Plan that re-flows when the timetable lands. |
| `date` | **Target grade** segmented (A* A B C D E; AS a–e) · **Study days** 7-day toggle row with two rest days pre-selected | Target drives nothing punitive: it sets the marks-to-next-grade line and the phase mix, and can be changed in Course memory. Rest days are shown as neutral. |
| `level` | **Current level** segmented ("Just started" · "Mid-course" · "Revising, mock done") · **Explanation language** (English · Русский · Қазақша) | Below: "Or let a 3-question probe decide" → a ProbeCard with ConfidenceSlider seeds mastery (SYNTHESIS §3.3 5:00); the self-report becomes a cohort prior only. |
| `consent` | **Parent or guardian email** (required <16; optional 16–17) · **What your parent will see** (a two-line summary linking to 10.21) | The age band is already known from `save`. 13–15 requires a verified parent email before memory and notifications turn on; teen defaults: Quiet Hours 22:00–07:00 on, notifications off, memory on but visible. One-line consequence under each option; links to the policy in 9 pt. |
| `import` (optional, labelled "Optional" rather than numbered) | one picker: **Mock paper photos** (→ 10.8 batch, then §10.11 post-mortem) · **Anki / Quizlet deck** (.apkg, .txt, .csv) · **Notes PDF** (PMT/SME → Library, trust rank 5) · **Timetable** (ICS or photo → Plan busy blocks) · **Another assistant's export** (ChatGPT `conversations.json` + memory summary, Claude `conversations.json` + Topics, Gemini Takeout → memory facts only, per 07-MEM-009: each imported fact carries `source: imported:<vendor>`, is shown in the memory list on the first Session and is deletable; imported transcripts are never ingested as Attempts) · **Class code** | "Skip for now" is always visible; imports run in the background with a Toast on completion; nothing blocks the first Today. |

```
                    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                    │ STEP 3 OF 6 ────────────────────────────      │
                    │                                              │
                    │ Which papers are you sitting?                │
                    │ (●P1) (●P2) (●P3) (●P4)   AS only ▸          │
                    │                                              │
                    │ Exam series                                  │
                    │ (●June 2027 | Nov 2026 | March 2027)         │
                    │ P2 · Thu 27 May 2027 (timetable published)   │
                    │                                              │
                    │ Back                          [■ Continue ]  │
                    └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: each sheet's primary button is disabled until its required field is valid; no red until blur. `loading`: Pack fetch for the syllabus shows a 32 px row skeleton in the typeahead; the primary button shows a 16 px spinner ≤1 s. `error`: inline under the field ("That code isn't a class we know. Check with your teacher."), never a modal; magic-link send failure offers "Try Google instead". `offline`: the flow cannot start; a Banner explains and the pending anonymous Mark stays cached for 24 h. `gated`: a Class whose teacher locked syllabus/papers renders those sheets read-only with "Set by your teacher"; a board without a live Pack shows "Coming — we'll email you when 9700 Biology is ready" and still creates the Course in `waiting` state with Cards and Library working.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Enter = Continue when valid; `Esc` = Back; `⌘.` = Skip where allowed; segmented controls are radiogroups with arrow keys; typeahead accepts code or name and shows the Pack version on the row; the 7-day row toggles with Space. Changing board on `course` after `papers` resets `papers` and says so in one line. The whole flow completes in ≤90 s at the median (telemetry `setup_ms`).

**Data.** Writes the Course `{board, syllabus_code, syllabus_version, level, exam_session, papers[] with dates, target_grade, study_days[], explain_lang, age_band, consent{parent_email, verified_at}, class_id?}`; the anonymous Attempt(s) re-parent to `course_id`; probe answers create Attempts with `confidence` and seed the Learner Graph; imports create Library Sources (with provenance USER), Cards (with `imported_from`), Plan busy blocks or a mock run. Reads Pack metadata (papers, series, timetable), Class (for codes), policy object (age gates).

**Acceptance.**
1. The required path is exactly six sheets (`save · course · papers · date · level · consent`) and the step counter says so ("Step 3 of 6"); `import` carries the word "Optional" instead of a step number; no sheet has more than two fields; a reviewer counts them in Storybook.
2. Refresh on any step resumes at that step with entered values; browser Back moves one step, never exits the flow silently.
3. A class-code entry pre-fills and locks teacher-set fields and lands on `level` in one step.
4. An Under-13 selection on `save` collects no email, writes no `user` row, and discards the anonymous Attempt within 24 h. Under-16 with a pending (unverified) parent email reaches Today with memory visible and notifications off; the state is shown in Settings › Memory.
5. Import of a 200-card `.apkg` with LaTeX and images completes in the background and produces Cards with provenance lines; the flow never waits on it.
6. Keyboard flow 2 of 09-A11Y-014 completes; every segmented control announces its options and current value.

## 10.3 Home / Today
**Purpose.** Answer "what is the highest-value thing to do right now" in one glance and start it in one tap: the scheduler's session mix rendered as three cards, with the week visible and nothing that shames.

**Stage.** MVP. Multi-Course merging is MVP because a student sits several papers; the exam chip and the 7-day strip are MVP.

**Jobs served.** J3 (21:40 on a school night), J12 (review what is due), J8 indirectly (the exam chip), 02-MOM-002…004 (calendar-position behaviour).

**Route.** `/home`. Default after sign-in. Multi-Course users see one Today merged across Courses by mark-yield, each card tagged with its Course; the Course switcher is in the top bar.

**Layout.** Desktop: sidebar shows Courses (not a syllabus tree — Home is above Courses), then a single **Ask** row directly under the Courses group, then Recent and Due; centre column 68 ch; no right pane (no rail either — nothing here cites). Tablet: same without sidebar (overlay). Mobile: single column; the 7-day strip is horizontally scrollable with today centred; the three cards stack; the composer is absent (Home is not a chat — a conversation without a Course is an **Ask**, 10.26, reached in one keystroke from the sidebar row, `⌘K → Ask <anything>`, or `/ask`).

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Top bar | Course switcher (text button with ▾) · usage dot (plain, opens Settings › Usage) · `⌘K` |
| Date line | `--text-ui` muted: "Thursday 18 September · 243 days to P2". No greeting, no name, no emoji, nothing above 20 px except card titles. |
| Week strip | 7 × 44 pt day cells (Mon-first per locale), today with a 2 px `--accent` ring, planned blocks as 4 px marks under the date, exam chips (`--text-xs` tabular "P2 · 27 May") on exam days when within 14 days; rest days rendered in `--text-muted` with a 12 px "rest" label, never crossed out. Tapping a day opens Plan (10.15) at that day. |
| Today cards | three TodayCards in a row (stack <900): **Continue** (the open Session or Item: "3.2 Motivation · your rewrite is waiting"), **Due** ("18 cards · about 6 minutes" · Review), **Next** (the scheduler's next block: "Practise · 9609 P2 · one 8-mark Analyse · 12 min" · Start). Each card: 14 px 500 title, one 13 px line, one action. |
| Session mix line | `--text-sm` muted under the cards: "Today's mix: 60% due retrieval · 25% interleaved practice · 15% new — Phase A, 34 weeks out" with a "Why" text link to the phase rules (§04.13 session composition by phase, §07.5 Mark-Yield). |
| Weekly goal | one row: "This week: 3 of 4 sessions · 2 rest days" as four 10 px discs (filled/empty) plus two muted "rest" discs; no streak, no flame, no counter of consecutive days anywhere. Hidden in exam weeks. |
| Quiet Hours | Banner (`--banner-h`) when inside Quiet Hours: "Quiet Hours until 07:00 — Cards are here if you want them; nothing is due." Not dismissible; no notifications fire. |
| Exam-week collapse | Inside T−7 of a paper, Home collapses to one card: "Paper 2 · Thursday 09:00 · 3 days" with Continue/Due/Next stacked under it, rings hidden, no deltas, no predicted grade (SYNTHESIS §3.4). |

```
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 9609 Business ▾                                                            ● usage    ⌘K
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 Thursday 18 September · 243 days to P2

 ┌ Mon ┐┌ Tue ┐┌ Wed ┐┌(Thu)┐┌ Fri ┐┌ Sat ┐┌ Sun ┐
 │ 15  ││ 16  ││ 17  ││ 18  ││ 19  ││ 20  ││ 21  │
 │ ▂   ││ rest││ ▂   ││ ▂▂  ││ ▂   ││ rest││ ▂   │
 └─────┘└─────┘└─────┘└─────┘└─────┘└─────┘└─────┘

 ┌────────────────────────────┐ ┌────────────────────────────┐ ┌────────────────────────────┐
 │ Continue                   │ │ Due                        │ │ Next                       │
 │ 3.2 Motivation             │ │ 18 cards · about 6 minutes │ │ Practise · P2 · one 8-mark │
 │ Your rewrite is waiting    │ │                            │ │ Analyse · 12 min           │
 │ [Open]                     │ │ [Review]                   │ │ [■ Start]                  │
 └────────────────────────────┘ └────────────────────────────┘ └────────────────────────────┘
 Today's mix: 60% due retrieval · 25% interleaved · 15% new — Phase A, 34 weeks out · Why

 This week  ● ● ● ○  ·  rest  rest
```

**States.** `empty` (new Course, nothing due, no Session): the three cards still show work — Continue: "Start with a probe on 3.2 Motivation" · Due: "No cards yet — they come from your Marks" · Next: "Practise one 4-mark Explain · 8 min"; never a greeting or an illustration. `loading`: three card skeletons with final geometry; the week strip renders from cache immediately. `error` (scheduler unavailable): cards fall back to the last computed queue with a 13 px line "Showing yesterday's plan — refreshing"; never blank. `offline`: Due card is live (local FSRS); Continue/Next show "Needs a connection" as their line with the action disabled; the Banner shows the offline string (09-COPY-002 #16). `gated/quota`: if today's levels-Mark quota is exhausted, Next never proposes a levels Mark; it proposes points Items or Cards and says why in the mix line ("levels Marks resume at 00:00"). Between-papers mode: Next is restricted to the next paper only.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Enter/Space on a card runs its action; the digits are unbound here (the IntentRow uses `⌥1/⌥2/⌥3` per 09-KEYS-001, and Home has no confidence or grading control to claim the bare digits); `⌘⇧N` new Session; `⌘K`. Tapping "Why" opens a popover with the phase rule and the mark-yield list behind Next (top 3 syllabus points with `yield_s`). Long-press/⋯ on Next offers "Something else" → the next two candidates; a decline is logged, not argued. The widget (iOS/Android) mirrors Due and Next with the same strings and deep links.

**Data.** Reads: Learner Graph (due queue with counts and minutes, phase, mark-yield ranking, last open Session/Item), Plan (blocks for the strip, rest days, Quiet Hours, weekly goal), Course (exam dates, papers, between-papers flag), policy object (quotas, teen defaults). Writes: `session_started {source: continue|due|next}`, `next_declined {item_id, reason?}`, `home_view` telemetry. Nothing on Home mutates mastery.

**Acceptance.**
1. Copy corpus test on Home: zero matches for the streak/shame lexicon; no `%` in any mastery string; the mix line's percentages are the only percentages.
2. Every state shows three actionable cards; the empty state has been reviewed in Storybook for a Course with 0 Attempts.
3. TTI ≤1.5 s from cache; cards paint from the last queue before the network responds (stale-while-revalidate).
4. Quiet Hours banner appears exactly inside the configured window across DST changes (unit test with `Asia/Almaty` and `Europe/London`).
5. Exam-week collapse triggers at T−7 days 00:00 local for the nearest paper and reverts after the paper's end time.
6. A moderated test of five students: each names what to do next within 3 s and starts it in one tap.

## 10.4 Course home / Syllabus tree
**Purpose.** Orient the student in the exam's own structure — the official syllabus tree with honest mastery on every point — and make the Course's facts (Pack version, dates, memory, files, class) visible and editable in one place.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J8 (where do I stand), J5 (enter Learn at the right point), J2 (find the point behind a wrong answer), J9 (class link).

**Route.** `/c/:courseId` (the tree is the Learn tab's default state without a Session). Tabs `/learn · /papers · /cards · /plan · /progress` share the Course header.

**Layout.** Desktop: the sidebar **is** the CourseTree (280 px) and the centre column shows the Course header and the selected node's detail; right pane is a rail (opens on a citation from a node's description). Tablet: tree in the overlay sidebar, detail in centre. Mobile: the tree is the screen (route stack: Course → Unit → Point → detail), the header collapses to two rows.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Course header | `--text-h1` "Cambridge International A Level Business 9609" · line: "June 2027 · target A · Pack 9609/2026–28 v14 · updated 2 Sep" (Pack chip opens the changelog) · ReadinessRing per paper (64 px) with reason text ("P2 · 41% · no timed section yet") — hidden inside T−7 · "Next exam in 243 days" tabular. |
| Tabs | Learn · Papers · Cards · Plan · Progress (40 px strip, `--text-ui` 500). |
| Tree filters | chip row: All · Shaky · Due today · High yield · Unseen · per-paper (P1…P4); a 36 px filter input (`⌘K` scoped "in this Course"); sort: Syllabus order (default) · Mark-yield. |
| CourseTree | official numbering and wording from the Pack ("3.2 Motivation" → "3.2.1 Motivation theories: Taylor, Maslow, Herzberg, McClelland, Vroom"); rows 32 px, indent 16, MasteryPip 10 px at row start, mark-yield badge right (`--text-xs` tabular "▲ 4.1 marks" = expected marks recoverable), due-count badge, a small `ecf`-style "seen" tag on points with an unexpired seen-solution. Units collapsible; state persisted. |
| Node detail (centre) | title and Pack wording; pip with its reason sentence; Items summary ("14 Items · 6 questions from 4 series · 3 misconceptions"); row of actions **Learn** (primary) · Practise · Cards · Show in book¹ (citation chip → right pane at the syllabus page); the three misconceptions as one-line rows; last three Attempts with date, tariff and Mark band. |
| Course facts | collapsible groups under the header on mobile / a right-aligned card on desktop: **Memory** (editable rows: "Target A", "Prefers worked examples in maths", "Extra time 25%"; each row with edit/forget), **Files** (uploads with trust badge), **Class** (join/leave, teacher name, locked settings), **Extra time and rest breaks** (inherited by every Timer). |

```
┌──────────────────────────┐┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐|rail|
│ (All)(Shaky)(Due)(Yield) ││ Cambridge International A Level Business 9609             │
│ 🔍 filter…               ││ June 2027 · target A · Pack 9609/2026–28 v14 · 2 Sep      │
│                          ││ ◯41 P2   ◯63 P1   ◯— P3   ◯— P4     Next exam in 243 days│
│ 1 BUSINESS AND ITS ENV   ││ ⟨Learn*⟩ ⟨Papers⟩ ⟨Cards⟩ ⟨Plan⟩ ⟨Progress⟩              │
│ ◕ 1.1 Enterprise   ▲1.2  ││───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│ ● 1.2 Business structure ││ 3.2 Motivation                               ◔ Shaky      │
│ ▾ 3 PEOPLE IN ORGANISAT. ││ ¼ — 1 correct unaided, last seen 6 days ago; decays to ○  │
│  ◔ 3.2 Motivation  ▲4.1  ││ on 20 Sep without practice                                │
│  ○ 3.3 Management  ▲3.4  ││ 14 Items · 6 questions from 4 series · 3 misconceptions   │
│  ◕ 3.4 Comms  seen       ││ [■ Learn]  Practise   Cards   Show in book¹               │
│ ▸ 4 MARKETING            ││ Misconceptions: Herzberg hygiene ≠ motivator · pay as…    │
│ ───────────────          ││ Recent: 12 Sep · 8-mark Analyse · 4–5/8 · 9 Sep · probe   │
│ RECENT  DUE              ││                                                           │
└──────────────────────────┘└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty` (Course just created): every pip is Unseen (empty ring); the centre shows "Start with a 3-question probe on what school is teaching now" with **Start probe** and a "Pick a point" hint; rings show "—" with "No timed practice yet". `loading`: tree rows skeleton at final row height; header from cache. `error`: tree from cache with a 13 px line "Mastery may be out of date"; if the Pack fails to load, "Syllabus unavailable — retry" inline. `offline`: tree and pips from the local Learner Graph snapshot; Learn/Practise actions disabled with the reason; Cards enabled. `gated`: a Pack in `waiting` state (board not live) shows the official tree with pips disabled and the line "Marking for {code} arrives when its Pack is live — {roadmap_date}. Cards and Library work now; for questions in this subject, Ask is general help, not exam-grounded, and it does not count as study." with **Ask about {subject}** as the action (10.26, 03-SCOP-006). A Class-locked Course shows a lock glyph on teacher-set facts.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Click/Enter on a row selects (no animation, 09-MOTN-003); `→/←` expand/collapse; typing filters; `⌘K` "Learn <point>" jumps here then into a Session; hovering/focusing a pip shows the reason tooltip; the Pack chip opens a changelog sheet ("v14: 3.2 wording updated to the 2026 syllabus; 2 Items withdrawn"); "Show in book" opens the right pane at the syllabus page (≤400 ms p95); memory rows edit inline with Enter/Esc and "Forget" removes with an Undo toast; the decay reason names the date and the fix ("practise unaided before 20 Sep").

**Data.** Reads: Pack (tree, wording, Items per node, misconceptions, version, changelog), Learner Graph (mastery state and M_s, retrievability, decay date, mark-yield `yield_s`, seen-solution expiry, last Attempts), Course (dates, target, papers, extra time), memory rows, Library files, Class. Writes: memory edits (`memory_update`), filter/sort/collapse state (per user), `tree_node_selected`, extra-time and rest-break settings (`Course.extra_time_pct`, `Course.rest_breaks`).

**Acceptance.**
1. Pips are distinguishable in a greyscale screenshot and every pip's tooltip contains a count, a last-seen date and a decay date (or "—" with the reason).
2. Node numbering and wording match the Pack byte-for-byte; the Pack version chip matches `Course.pack_version`; a changed Pack shows the new chip within one session start.
3. No percentage appears anywhere in the tree or node detail; rings show a whole number with a reason line, hidden inside T−7.
4. Filter "High yield" sorts by `yield_s` descending and the badge value equals the scheduler's number for the same node (same query).
5. Memory rows are editable and forgettable with Undo; a forgotten row never re-appears from inference (unit test on the memory writer).
6. Tree of 220 nodes renders in ≤120 ms after data and scrolls at 60 fps (virtualised).

## 10.5 Session (Course › Learn workspace)
**Purpose.** The Session is the workspace where tutoring happens: one Course-scoped thread of Turns and Attempts in which the Effort Gate, the Help Ladder, intent chips, citation chips and the source pane are all one gesture apart, and the hand-back returns the work to the student after every Turn.

**Stage.** MVP, in full, including branching, edit-and-regenerate, the Scratchpad tab and incognito. This is the screen §03.2 parity is measured on, so a deferred row here is a parity failure.

**Jobs served.** J2 (I got it wrong and don't know why), J4 (explain this so it sticks), J5 (teach me the point school is on), J11 ("just the answer" without losing the learning); 02-MOM-002 (mid-course), 02-MOM-005 (the 21:40 school night).

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/learn/:sessionId`. `?turn=<turnId>` scrolls to and focuses a Turn; `?item=<itemId>` opens a Session with that Item already posed. `⌘⇧N` mints a new `sessionId` in the same Course. A Session is a container, not a memory boundary: the Learner Graph (§07.3) is read on every Turn regardless of which Session it is in.

**Layout.** Desktop ≥1280: sidebar = CourseTree (280) with Recent and Due beneath; centre = Turn stream at 68 ch centred in `1fr` with the Composer sticky at the bottom edge (56 px rest, grows to 8 lines); right pane = 48 px rail by default, opening to 480 per 10-CONV-002. Tablet 900–1279: `1fr 420px`, sidebar as overlay, right pane a 50 % bottom sheet in portrait. Mobile <900: single column; the tree, the source pane, the math keypad and the mode menu are sheets; the composer sits above the keyboard-safe area and the HelpLadder moves from the message margin to a single "More help" button under the Turn.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Top bar (48) | Course switcher · Session title (auto: the first Item's syllabus point, editable) · ⋯ (Rename · Export Receipt · Delete) · usage dot · `⌘K` |
| Turn stream | alternating **UserMessage** and **Turn (assistant)**; Item cards (question text, tariff, command word, mapped point, **EffortGateChip**); **IntentRow** under the tutor Turn while a gradable Item is open; **HelpLadder** (5 rungs, 8×8) in the left message margin of the Turn that owns the Item; **CitationChip**s after the sentences they support; **MemoryChip** under any Turn that wrote a memory fact ("Remembered: {fact}" · Edit · Forget); Mark cards rendered inline (compact 10.7 in the centre column, "Open Mark ↗" to the full view); **ThinkingRow** collapsed; a stopped Turn keeps its **Continue** control for 10 minutes (09-STRM-014) |
| Composer | ModeChip **Learn · Practise · Mark** (`⌘1/2/3`, remembered per Course) · `+` (file, photo, from Library) · EffortDial · camera · **Send/Stop**; placeholder = the hand-back string of the last Turn (09-COPY-004) |
| Right pane | tabs ⟨Source⟩ ⟨Scratchpad⟩ ⟨Artifact⟩ ⟨Cards⟩; Source shows the cited page with the span highlighted and `[` `]` citation arrows; Scratchpad and Artifact per 10.17 |
| Session end card | after 20 min idle, from ⋯ › End session, or `⌘K → End session`: three lines — what moved, what is scheduled, one next action; no praise, no score. There is no double-press chord: `Esc` has only the cascade of 09-KEYS-001, so dismissing a popover can never end a Session |

```
┌ 9609 ▾ · 3.2 Motivation ─────────────────────────────────────────────── ● ⌘K ─┐|rail|
│ TREE           │                                                              │  ⟨S⟩3
│ ◔ 3.2 Motivat. │  ┌ Item · 9609 P2 · 8 marks · Analyse · 3.2.1 ────────────┐  │  ⟨Sc⟩
│ ○ 3.3 Managem. │  │ Analyse two ways Herzberg's motivators could raise…    │  │  ⟨A⟩
│ ◕ 3.4 Comms    │  │ (Your go first)  90 s ▸                                │  │  ⟨C⟩
│ ─────────────  │  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│ RECENT         │                          Pay is a hygiene factor so it… ┐    │
│ DUE 18         │                                                              │
│                │ ▪ Right: you separated hygiene from motivators.              │
│ ▪              │ ▪ The chain stops at "staff are happier" — Analyse wants the  │
│ ▪              │   effect on *this* firm's output.¹                            │
│ ▫              │ ▪ Name one thing at Zara that would change. ¹ syllabus p.41   │
│ ▫              │ (Teach me) (Check my steps) (Just the answer ·disabled)       │
│ ────────── ⇧↓  │ ↓New                                                          │
│ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│ │ Name the stakeholder first                                                │ │
│ │ [+] (●Learn|Practise|Mark)                     Think▾ [Camera]  [■ Send]   │ │
│ └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty` (new Session): no greeting; the centre shows the Course's next Item proposal as a card ("Start where school is: 3.2.1 Motivation theories · one 8-mark Analyse") with **Start** and a 13 px line "or ask anything about 9609". `loading`: composer live immediately; history pages in as skeletons of the final geometry. `streaming`: 09-STRM-001…013 in full — optimistic echo ≤50 ms, status line at 800 ms drawn from the route, Stop in place, `↓ New` pill after 120 px of user scroll. `error`: InlineError in the assistant slot with cause and "Retry with Think"; the user message is never lost. `offline`: composer stays editable, Send becomes "Will send", messages queue with a `queued` tag, Cards and downloaded sources remain usable, citation chips for uncached pages show "Available when you're back". `gated/quota`: past the Deep-Turn cap the Turn streams on the next route down with a one-line banner naming the change (03-LIM-003); the gate and ladder are never metered (03-LIM-005).

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `Enter` send, `⇧Enter` newline, `Esc` stop-then-close-then-focus-composer; `⌘1/2/3` mode; `⌥1/⌥2/⌥3` fire the IntentRow while it is visible, from anywhere including the focused composer (bare digits type characters — 09-KEYS-001); `⇧↓` next help rung; `⌘E` effort dial; `⌘J` pane, `⌘⇧J` pane tabs; `⌘F` find in this Session; `[` `]` previous/next citation; `⌘↑` edit the last user message and `⌘⇧B` branch this Turn (both create a branch pill "2/3 ◂ ▸"); `⌘⇧C` copy Turn, `⌘⇧R` retry, `⌘⇧S` save selection as Card, `⌘⇧K` Scratchpad, `⌘⇧M` math keypad, `⌘⇧P` photograph. **Editing an earlier message is not keyboard-only:** on desktop, hover or focus on any UserMessage reveals a 16 px pencil in a 24 px target; on touch, a 400 ms long-press or the ⋯ affordance opens a sheet with Edit · Copy · Branch. Hover or focus on an assistant Turn reveals MessageActions (copy · retry · explain simpler · quiz me · show in book); on touch they collapse to the same one ⋯, so every hover-reachable action has a touch path (09-A11Y-008). Selecting text in a Turn or in the source pane opens the SelectionPopover. Clicking a citation chip opens the right pane at the page in ≤400 ms p95 and returns focus to the chip on `Esc`. Long-press on the ladder margin (or the "More help" button on mobile) climbs exactly one rung.

**Data.** Reads: Learner Graph (mastery and `state_reason` for the Item's point, FSRS due, misconception priors, `prefs.intent_by_item_type`, memories, debt level), Pack (Item, scheme, syllabus wording, misconceptions, command-word table), Session history (last N Turns per §07.3), Course (papers, dates, phase, extra time), policy object (quotas, teen defaults). Writes: `turns {role, text, rung, citations[], lint_result, route, latency_ms}`, `attempts {gate_state, gate_reason, gate_latency_ms, entry_rung, rung_reached, intent, seen_solution, confidence?}`, mastery recomputation on Attempt close, FSRS scheduling for seen-solution retests, `cards` from Save-as-card, `sessions {title, ended_at, end_card}`, telemetry `turn_sent`, `rung_climbed`, `chip_used`, `citation_opened`.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-SESN-001 | The hand-back is behaviour: within 100 ms of a Turn completing, focus moves to the composer and the placeholder is that Turn's hand-back sentence, unless the user is typing elsewhere. | Playwright: `document.activeElement` is the composer and `placeholder` matches the Turn's `handback` field on 20 golden Turns. |
| 10-SESN-002 | The HelpLadder renders in the message margin on every Turn owning a gradable Item, shows the current rung filled and never allows a descent or a skip within an Item. | Story audit + suite from 04-LADR-002. |
| 10-SESN-003 | The IntentRow appears only while a gradable Item is open and **Just the answer** is disabled with the tooltip "After one attempt." until `gate_state ≠ open`. | DOM assertion in both states; tooltip string matches the catalogue. |
| 10-SESN-004 | The composer never carries a second chip system: exactly one ModeChip, and no intent chips inside the composer at any breakpoint. | Component test counts chip groups in the composer = 1. |
| 10-SESN-005 | Citation chip → highlighted source page ≤400 ms p95 warm, ≤1.5 s cold, with the centre measure unchanged when the pane opens. | Perf trace on 50 chips; screenshot diff of the centre column. |
| 10-SESN-006 | The full streaming contract passes on this screen (CLS above the cursor = 0; no raw LaTeX or fence markers; resume on reconnect without duplication). | 09.8 golden-stream suite run against `/learn`. |
| 10-SESN-007 | Every assistant Turn in Learn mode is ≤120 words unless depth was requested, asks at most one question, and carries zero praise-lexicon matches. | Corpus test on 500 recorded Turns; violations = 0 after lint. |
| 10-SESN-008 | Session deletion removes Turns but preserves Attempts, Marks and the Learner Graph, and says so in the confirm sheet ("Your marks and mastery stay"). | Delete a Session with 4 Attempts: `attempts` count unchanged; copy matches. |
| 10-SESN-009 | Keyboard flow 4 of 09-A11Y-014 (a Learn Turn with a ladder climb and an intent chip) completes with visible focus at every step. | Scripted run with per-step screenshots. |
| 10-SESN-010 | Every memory write is visible at the moment it happens: 100% of `memories` inserts render a MemoryChip on the Turn that caused them, with working Edit and Forget, and the chip persists in the transcript rather than fading like a toast. | Fixture Session that writes 5 facts: 5 chips in the DOM after reload; Forget on one writes the tombstone and the fact does not reappear from inference. |
| 10-SESN-011 | Editing an earlier user message works on web, iOS and Android: hover pencil on desktop, long-press and ⋯ sheet on touch, `⌘↑` for the last message. Editing message 1 of a 30-Turn Session regenerates from that point and creates a sibling branch with a "2/2 ◂ ▸" counter; the original Attempt is preserved. | Scripted run on the three platforms per 03-PAR-002; `attempts` count unchanged after the edit. |

## 10.6 Practise runner
**Purpose.** Deliver one exam-shaped Item at a time — real tariff, real command word, real AO split — with the Effort Gate in front of it, a confidence rating on every submission, and a Mark at the end, so that practice produces evidence rather than a feeling of having practised.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J5 (practise the point school is on), J7 (exam-style questions that behave like the real ones), J12 (retest what I got wrong); 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-003.

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/practise/:runId`, `?item=<n>` for the current position. A run is created by: Home › Next, the CourseTree node action **Practise**, `⌘K → Practise <point>`, "5 more like this" from a Mark, a Wrong-question retest (10.13), or a teacher assignment (10.20). The run's composition is the scheduler's (§04.13): after first exposure no blocked run exceeds five same-type Items and interleaving pulls from the last three topics.

**Layout.** Desktop: `280px | 1fr | 480px` with the sidebar showing the **run strip** (one row per Item: index, tariff pill, state) instead of the full tree; centre holds one Item at 68 ch; right pane is a rail and opens only after submission (never during the attempt — the source pane would be a leak). Tablet: `1fr 420px`, run strip in the overlay. Mobile: one Item per screen, run strip as a 50 % sheet from the top-bar counter ("3 of 8"), answer area above the keyboard, actions pinned in the bottom 40 %.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Item header | tariff pill (`--text-xs` tabular, "8 marks") · command word verbatim ("Analyse") · AO span for this paper ("AO1–3") · mapped syllabus point with MasteryPip and reason on focus · provenance line ("9609/22 s24 Q3(b)" or "Margin original, P2 format") |
| Question body | question text (OWN/LICENSED) or the Paper Companion line "Answer Q3(b) from your copy"; case/insert material collapsible above; diagrams rendered by the deterministic renderer, never by an image model |
| Effort Gate | **EffortGateChip** "Your go first" with the secondary "Give me 90 seconds with a hint"; while `gate_state = open` the answer area is the only focusable primary surface and rungs 4–5 are unreachable (04-GATE-001/003) |
| Answer area | input tabs **Typed · Maths · Handwritten · Photo**; typed at 17/1.55 with a **structure guide** derived from the scheme's evidence rules, not from the tariff — for an 8-mark Analyse: "two developed chains · two links each · one case fact per chain"; for a 12-mark Evaluate it names the judgement condition. A raw word count remains available but is unlabelled and carries no target, because the marker is instructed that length is not evidence and a words-per-mark constant coaches the behaviour it is built to ignore. Maths = MathLive + keypad (`⌘⇧M`); Photo → 10.8 |
| Confidence | **ConfidenceSlider** (Sure · Think so · Guessing) directly above Submit, **defaulted to "Think so"** with `confidence_default = true` recorded so the calibration analysis can exclude untouched values; Submit is never disabled by it |
| Actions | **Submit** (the only filled button) · Skip (`s`) · Flag (`f`) · Help (opens the ladder at the entry rung; `⇧↓` climbs) · timer chip (soft, per-Item minutes-per-mark from Pack metadata, counts up, never blocks) |
| After Mark | inline compact Mark (10.7) then a row: **Next Item** (primary) · "5 more like this" · "Explain this AO" · "Open full Mark ↗" |

```
┌ run 3 of 8 ─────┐┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐|rail|
│ 1  4 ✓          ││ 8 marks · Analyse · AO1–3 · 3.2.1 Motivation  ◔               │
│ 2  8 ✓          ││ 9609/22 s24 Q3(b)                                             │
│▸3  8            ││                                                               │
│ 4 12            ││ Analyse two ways in which Herzberg's motivators could raise   │
│ 5  4 flagged    ││ productivity at ZW.                                           │
│ 6  8            ││ ┌ Typed · Maths · Handwritten · Photo ──────────────────────┐ │
│ 7 12            ││ │                                                           │ │
│ 8  4            ││ │       two developed chains · two links each · one case  │ │
│ │       fact per chain                                    0 │ │
│ ──────────────  ││ └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
│ 04:12  ~10 min  ││ (Your go first · 90 s ▸)      Help ⇧↓                          │
│                 ││ How sure are you?  (Sure | ●Think so | Guessing)  not rated   │
│                 ││ Skip  Flag                                    [■ Submit]       │
└─────────────────┘└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: a run with zero Items cannot exist — the creator falls back to a generated Item and says so ("No past-paper Item left on 3.2.1 at this tariff — this one is a Margin original in P2 format"). `loading`: header and tariff paint first; the question body skeletons at final height; the timer starts only after the body paints. `streaming`: applies to the Mark after Submit (09-STRM-011) and to generated Items ("Writing an 8-mark Analyse on 3.2.1…" as a status line, ≤6 s p95). `error`: generation or scheme fetch failure shows an InlineError with "Try another Item" and preserves any typed text; a Mark failure keeps the Attempt and offers "Mark it again". `offline`: Items already in the run are answerable and answers are queued with the `queued` tag; Marks stream on reconnect; the Effort Gate and the ladder work from the cached Item (rungs 1–3 only; rungs 4–5 need the pre-solved key). `gated/quota`: when the levels-Mark quota is spent, the run continues and the submission returns evidence-only feedback with the copy of 09-COPY-002 #15 plus "Full Mark at 00:00"; points Items are never gated.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `Enter` submits when the answer area is not focused, `⌘Enter` submits from inside it; `s` skip, `f` flag, `n` next, `p` previous, `⇧↓` next rung, `1/2/3` set confidence when the slider has focus, `⌘⇧M` keypad, `⌘⇧P` camera. Skip records `skipped_reason ∈ {too_hard, seen_it, no_time, other}` from a two-tap row and reschedules the Item rather than deleting it. Flag adds the Item to the run's review list and to the Wrong-question bank if the subsequent Mark loses marks. "5 more like this" holds tariff and cognitive-demand tag fixed (§05.10) and appends to the current run. Leaving mid-run keeps the run open on Home › Continue for 7 days.

**Data.** Reads: Pack (Item, scheme, tariff, command word, AO split, minutes-per-mark, diagram checklist), Learner Graph (mastery, entry rung, misconception priors, interleaving pool, `prefs`), Course (extra time — applied to the soft timer's guide only), policy object. Writes: `attempts {item_id, response, input_mode, confidence, confidence_default, gate_state, gate_latency_ms, entry_rung, rung_reached, latency_s, skipped_reason?, flagged}`, the Mark, `runs {composition, position, source}`, mastery and FSRS updates on Mark commit, Cards generated from uncredited marking points (10.12), `calibration.predict_postdict` rows from confidence vs outcome.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-PRAC-001 | Confidence is collected without coercion: the ConfidenceSlider defaults to "Think so", Submit is never disabled by it, and every Attempt carries a value plus `confidence_default` saying whether the student set it. Brier and the reliability diagram (10.14) are computed from touched values only; defaulted rows are counted separately and shown as "not rated" rather than as a guess. Nothing in the product withholds a result until a metacognitive field is answered (04-CORE-004). | 0 rows with null confidence in a 500-Attempt fixture; a story showing Submit enabled with an untouched slider; the calibration job's n excludes `confidence_default = true` rows. |
| 10-PRAC-002 | While `gate_state = open`, no rung-4/5 content, no worked solution and no correctness confirmation is reachable from any control on the screen. | Adversarial script: click every control before attempting; assert no solution tokens in the DOM. |
| 10-PRAC-003 | The tariff pill, command word and AO split are read verbatim from the Pack and match the source paper byte-for-byte; the answer-area guide is generated from the scheme's evidence rules and no tariff-to-words constant exists anywhere in the codebase. | Fixture diff across 200 Items, mismatches = 0; grep for a words-per-mark constant returns 0. |
| 10-PRAC-004 | The soft timer never blocks, never turns red and never auto-submits; it counts up and is hideable in Settings › Practise. | Visual regression past the guide time; setting toggles the chip. |
| 10-PRAC-005 | After first exposure a run never contains more than five consecutive Items of the same type, and interleaved Items are drawn from the last three topics. | Composition unit test over 100 generated runs. |
| 10-PRAC-006 | Skip reschedules rather than discards; the skipped Item reappears in a later run with `skipped_reason` visible to the scheduler only. | Skip then re-run: Item present; teacher API exposes no per-Item skip reason. |
| 10-PRAC-007 | Overconfident-wrong (confidence Sure + uncredited) halves the Item's next interval and the halving is visible in the Card interval hint. | 10-CARD-007 fixture reused end-to-end from this screen. |
| 10-PRAC-008 | Keyboard flow 5 of 09-A11Y-014 (Item with confidence and submit) completes; the ConfidenceSlider is a three-radio radiogroup, never a range input. | Scripted run; accessibility-tree snapshot. |

## 10.7 Mark view
**Purpose.** Render the Mark object (§05.2) as evidence on the student's own page: every credited, partial and uncredited span highlighted in their words, one card per AO with the descriptor it relied on, the command-word and cover-the-name checks, the band with its calibration basis, and exactly one thing to do next.

**Stage.** MVP. `?compare=<markId>` re-mark deltas and the teacher-flag line are MVP; the `wide` ≥1600 side-by-side layout is MVP-optional (it degrades to the two-tab pane, which is the specified behaviour at every other width).

**Jobs served.** J1 (what would an examiner give this and why), J2 (why did I lose the mark), J10 (how do I turn a Level 2 into a Level 3); 02-MOM-003, 02-MOM-006 (post-mock).

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/marks/:markId`; anonymous first Mark at `/m/:markToken` (24 h, 10.1). Deep links: `#ao=AO4`, `#span=<id>`, `?compare=<markId>` for a re-mark delta. A superseded Mark opens read-only with a banner "Superseded by a re-mark · Open the current Mark".

**Layout.** Desktop 1280–1599: sidebar auto-collapses to its 48 px rail on this route; centre = the answer at 56–68 ch; right 480 px carries **two tabs ⟨Mark⟩ ⟨Source⟩** with Mark active — a citation chip switches to Source and `[` `]` walk citations, `Esc` returns to Mark. Desktop ≥1600: the `wide` grid variant of 09-SPCE-005 shows both side by side (Mark `--panel-w-2` 440, Source `--panel-w` 480) and the tab strip disappears. Tablet: answer above, Mark column below as a 92 % sheet reachable from a sticky "Mark" bar. **Mobile, in this order:** MarkHeader → command-word check line → BandTotal + CalibrationBadge → AO cards, each collapsed to its first credited span → **"Show my answer"** disclosure that expands the spanned answer inline, with a sticky "Jump to answer" chip. The student's own 400-word essay never sits above their result. Tapping a span scrolls to its card and tapping the card's quote scrolls back with a 400 ms highlight (no colour change beyond `--highlight-source`).

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Answer column | question stem (collapsible) · the confirmed transcript or typed answer with line numbers (`L7`, tabular, 12 px, selectable-off) · **SpanHighlight**s: credited = solid underline + wash, partial = dotted, uncredited = dotted + trailing × glyph (09-A11Y-006) · annotation glyphs (✓ × Ø BOD CON RE GA ECF NC I) in the left gutter for points papers · "Show my photo" toggle for handwritten Attempts (image beside the transcript) |
| MarkHeader | question id · tariff · command word · AO split for this paper · `scheme_id` and Pack version as a 12 px line; **command-word check** line beneath in `--warning-line` text when `met = false`, rendered from `mark.command_word.mismatch` with the ceiling AO interpolated from the Pack ("Evaluate — a judgement is required. None found, so AO4 cannot reach Level 3.") |
| AOCard (one per AO) | label "AO3 Analysis" · "Level 2 of 3 · 5/8" · the descriptor relied on in our paraphrase with a CitationChip to the grid page · quoted spans grouped credited / partial / uncredited, each with its `reason_code` sentence · **missing_points** as ≤3 rows "Next mark available here" · confidence rendered "high / medium / check with teacher", never a percentage |
| Checks strip | **application_check** ("Cover-the-name test: 3 of 4 passed · L7 fails") · **examiner_warnings** as ERQuote blocks (≤40 words, series badge, deep link) |
| BandTotal | levels and part-verified points Marks: "11–13 / 20" (modal 600, range 400 tabular) with the tolerance line "Real examiners disagree by about ±2 on items like this."; a points Mark whose every marking point is SymPy-verified: "4 / 5 · exact" with no range and no tolerance line (09-PRIN-007). **CalibrationBadge** beneath: `gated` ("Calibrated on 9609 P4 · agreement 0.82 · 312 scripts"), `provisional` (badge in `--warning-line` text), `uncalibrated` (no number at all; spans, checks and advice only) · vendor chip after the badge, never in the headline |
| Actions | one filled primary from `next_mark_advice.primary_action` ("Rewrite the weakest paragraph" → 10.17 with that paragraph loaded) · secondaries "Explain this AO" · "Another like this" · "Turn into Cards" · text buttons "Show teacher" · "Challenge this Mark" · "Export Receipt" |
| Override strip | present only when a teacher override exists: "Marked 6 → 8 by Ms Aitken · reason: application credited · 14 Feb"; both marks visible, the teacher's is authoritative |

```
┌48┐┌──────────────── your answer ─────────────────┐┌──── ⟨Mark*⟩ ⟨Source⟩ ─────────┐
│⟨S⟩││L5  Zara could use market development to…    ││ 9609/41 s25 Q1 · 20 marks     │
│ 3 ││L7  ....this would help Firm grow ×          ││ Evaluate · AO1 3 AO2 2 AO3 8  │
│⟨Sc⟩││L11 ~~the $40 000 retained profit constraint ││ AO4 7 · Pack 9609 v14         │
│⟨A⟩││    means diversification's set-up costs…~~  ││ ! Evaluate — a judgement is   │
│⟨C⟩││L18 So market development is better.         ││   required. One unsupported…  │
│   ││                                             ││ ┌ AO3 Analysis ─────────────┐ │
│   ││                                             ││ │ Level 2 of 3 · 5/8   high │ │
│   ││                                             ││ │ "Developed analysis: two  │ │
│   ││                                             ││ │  or more links"¹          │ │
│   ││                                             ││ │ ✓ L11–L14 four links      │ │
│   ││                                             ││ │ · L7 single link          │ │
│   ││                                             ││ │ × L7 generic — fails the  │ │
│   ││                                             ││ │   cover-the-name test     │ │
│   ││                                             ││ └───────────────────────────┘ │
│   ││                                             ││ ┌ AO4 Evaluation ▒▒▒▒▒▒▒▒▒▒ ┐ │
│   ││                                             ││ 11–13 / 20   ±2               │
│   ││                                             ││ Calibrated on 9609 P4 · 0.82  │
│   ││                                             ││ · 312 scripts   [claude]      │
│   ││                                             ││ [■ Rewrite the weakest para]  │
│   ││                                             ││ Explain this AO · Another like│
└───┘└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘└ Challenge this Mark ──────────┘
```

Mobile (390 px) — the order is the result first, the answer behind a disclosure:

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ‹ 9609/41 s25 Q1 · 20 marks      ⋯   │
│ Evaluate · AO1 3 AO2 2 AO3 8 AO4 7   │
│ ! Evaluate — a judgement is required.│
│   None found, so AO4 cannot reach L3.│
│ 11–13 / 20        ±2                 │
│ Calibrated on 9609 P4 · 0.82 · 312   │
│──────────────────────────────────────│
│ ┌ AO3 Analysis · L2 of 3 · 5/8 ─── ▾ ┐│
│ │ ✓ L11–L14 four links              ││
│ └───────────────────────────────────┘│
│ ┌ AO4 Evaluation · L1 of 3 · 1/7 ─ ▾ ┐│
│ └───────────────────────────────────┘│
│──────────────────────────────────────│
│ ▸ Show my answer (3 credited, 2 not) │
│──────────────────────────────────────│
│ [■ Rewrite the weakest paragraph ]   │
└──────────────────────────────────────┘
   Jump to answer ▾   (sticky chip)
```

**States.** `loading`: the answer paints immediately (it is local); the Mark column shows MarkHeader then one AOCard skeleton per AO at fixed height. `streaming`: 09-STRM-011 — header ≤2 s, each AOCard ≤8 s, spans light in the answer as their card commits, BandTotal and badge last; the primary action button appears only when `next_mark_advice` has streamed. `error`: InlineError in the Mark column ("The marker timed out. Retry with Think, or copy your answer."); the answer and any confirmed transcript are untouched. `offline`: an already-received Mark is fully readable from cache including spans; new Marks queue. `gated/quota`: `uncalibrated` renders the evidence-only Mark with 09-COPY-002 #8; quota exhaustion renders the same evidence-only card with "Full Mark at 00:00". `superseded`: read-only banner. `teacher-flagged` (`show_teacher_flag = true` from a valid alternative or low AO confidence): a 13 px line "Flagged for your teacher: a valid point outside the indicative content" — never presented as an error.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `Tab` walks spans in reading order; each span announces "credited / partially credited / not credited" plus its reason; `Enter` on a span scrolls its AOCard into view and back. `⌘.` runs the primary action; `⌘⇧L` "Another like this"; `[` `]` citations; `⌘E` expands every AOCard. Hovering a span shows the reason and, for points papers, the `marking_point_id`. Selecting text in the answer offers "Ask about this line" (opens a Session Turn scoped to the span). **Challenge this Mark** is one tap → 10.22 with the AO preselected if an AOCard had focus. **Show teacher** copies a read-only link scoped to the Class. Nothing on this screen edits the student's text — the answer column is not an editor (10.17 owns rewriting).

**Data.** Reads: the Mark object in full (§05.2), the Attempt (transcript, image, input mode, confidence, `gate_state`), Pack (grid paraphrase, command-word definition, ER insights), calibration state for the paper, Class (for Show teacher and override), policy object (`visibility`). Writes: `mark_views {mark_id, spans_focused[], dwell_s}`, `rewrite_started`, `challenge_created`, `cards` on "Turn into Cards", `calibration.predict_postdict` postdict row when the student rated confidence before submitting, `receipt_export`. The Mark itself is immutable (05-MARK-012); re-marks, challenges and overrides create linked rows.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-MARK-001 | Every credited and partial span in the rendered DOM maps to a verbatim `[char_start, char_end)` range of the shown answer; a span that does not resolve blocks render of that AOCard and logs a defect. | Fuzz fixture with fabricated quotes: 0 rendered spans, 1 logged defect each. |
| 10-MARK-002 | Below the gate no number, band or predicted grade appears anywhere on the screen — including tooltips, the tab title and any export. | `uncalibrated` fixture: regex for `\d+\s*/\s*\d+` over the DOM and the PDF export returns 0 matches. |
| 10-MARK-003 | Every total carries a CalibrationBadge stating paper, agreement and n, and is either a range with its tolerance line or — for a points Mark with every marking point SymPy-verified — an exact integer with the `exact` badge. A bare integer with neither is a defect. | Snapshot of BandTotal in all five states of 09-COMP-001 (`gated · provisional · exact · uncalibrated · human-check`), including a fully verified points fixture rendering "4 / 5 · exact" and a part-verified one rendering a band. |
| 10-MARK-004 | Exactly one filled button on the screen, driven by `next_mark_advice.primary_action`, targeting the AO with the largest `(max − marks) × reachability`. | DOM count of buttons filled with `--accent` = 1; unit test on the target selection over 50 Marks. |
| 10-MARK-005 | The command-word check and the cover-the-name result are always rendered when present in the Mark. On mobile the order is MarkHeader → command-word check → BandTotal + badge → AO cards → "Show my answer"; the student's answer is never above their result. | Fixture with `met = false` at 390×844: both lines visible without scrolling past the first AOCard, and the answer column is behind the disclosure. |
| 10-MARK-006 | Span state is distinguishable in greyscale and to a screen reader; no state is conveyed by hue. | Desaturated screenshot diff ≥5 ΔE between states; SR script reads the state word. |
| 10-MARK-007 | A teacher override renders both marks with the reason code and attributes the authoritative mark to the teacher; the machine Mark is never hidden. | Override fixture: both numbers and the reason string present. |
