| 10-MARK-008 | Challenge entry is reachable in one tap from the Mark and from any AOCard, and is never rate-limited per Mark. | Click-count audit; 05-CHAL-001 suite. |
| 10-MARK-009 | Mark render (spans committed, cards laid out) adds no layout shift above the stream cursor and completes ≤45 s p95 for a 20-mark item. | 09.8 golden Mark streams; CLS above cursor = 0. |
| 10-MARK-010 | Keyboard flow 6 of 09-A11Y-014 (span traversal → Rewrite → re-mark) completes. | Scripted run with screenshots. |

## 10.8 Handwriting capture → confirm
**Purpose.** Turn a photograph of handwritten work into a transcript the student has confirmed, so that marking quotes the student's own words with no invented text and no silent OCR error.

**Stage.** MVP. Phone capture, QR hand-off and the confirm step are all MVP; batch mock import (10.2) rides the same flow.

**Jobs served.** J1 and J6 (mark what is in my exercise book / my mock script), J2 (why did this working lose the mark); 02-MOM-006. This screen is the entry point for §05.11 and for the mock import of 10.2.

**Route.** `/capture/:token`. The token is minted by the desktop client (QR + 6-character code), by the composer camera button, by the Paper runner's photo tab (confirmation deferred to after Submit, 10-RUNR-004) or by the mock-import step; it is single-use, bound to a Session, run or Attempt, and expires in 30 minutes. Opening the token on the phone requires no sign-in beyond the bound session's device claim; a re-used or expired token shows "This code has been used — scan a new one on your computer."

**Layout.** Phone (the primary surface, <900): full-bleed camera with a 44 pt top bar (close · flash · page counter "2 of 3") and a bottom action area in the lowest 30 % (shutter 72 px, gallery, **Done**); confirm is a full-screen route with the image on top (pinch-zoom, double-tap to fit) and the editable transcript beneath, split 45/55 with a draggable divider that also has a "Swap" button (09-A11Y-008). Desktop ≥1280: capture is a 480 px sheet showing the QR, the code, a drop zone and a "Use this computer's camera" link; confirm is side-by-side — image left in the centre column, transcript right in the 480 px pane, both scroll-synced by page. Tablet: side-by-side in landscape, stacked in portrait.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Capture | live edge detection (a 2 px `--accent` quad over the detected page, no animation) · auto-shutter when the quad is stable for 400 ms and blur passes (Laplacian variance ≥ threshold) · manual shutter always available · per-shot chips: `Retake` · `Crop` · `Rotate` · `Add page` |
| Crop / deskew | four corner handles plus the button alternatives **Full page · Top half · Bottom half · Auto**; deskew preview applied live; longest side normalised to ≤2,000 px |
| Quality gate | one line under the thumbnail, never a modal: "Too blurry to mark — retake" (blur), "Part of the page is cut off" (edge), "Very dark — try more light"; the shot is kept and can still be used with a "Use it anyway" text button that sets `quality_override = true` on the Attempt |
| Page strip | thumbnails in order with drag reorder **and** ⋯ › Move up / Move down; delete with Undo; max 12 pages per Attempt |
| Transcript | layout-preserving text: line breaks, `~~crossings-out~~` shown struck and never dropped, insertions, arrows, tables, units, superscripts, maths as rendered KaTeX with a "edit as LaTeX" toggle; **low-confidence tokens (<0.7) underlined** with a dotted 2 px rule and a 24 px tap target; `[illegible]` chips are red-free, labelled, and tappable to type the word |
| Confirm bar | count line "6 words to check · 2 pages" · **Looks right** (the only filled button, disabled while any `[illegible]` chip is untouched on a page with >10 % flags) · "Retake page 2" · "Mark without checking" is **not** offered |

```
mobile capture                      mobile confirm
┌──────────────────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ ×            flash    2 of 3 │    │ ‹ page 2 of 3            ⋯   │
│  ┌────────────────────────┐  │    │ ┌──────────────────────────┐ │
│  │                        │  │    │ │  [photo, pinch to zoom]  │ │
│  │   ┌──────────────┐     │  │    │ └──────────────────────────┘ │
│  │   │ page detected│     │  │    │ ── swap ───────────────────  │
│  │   └──────────────┘     │  │    │ Contribution = 20 − 8 = 12   │
│  │                        │  │    │ break-even = 36 000 ÷ 12     │
│  └────────────────────────┘  │    │ = 3̲0̲0̲0̲ units  ~~2500~~       │
│                              │    │ [illegible] per month        │
│  gallery      ( ● )    Done  │    │ 6 words to check             │
└──────────────────────────────┘    │ Retake page 2  [■ Looks right]│
                                    └──────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: no shot yet — the camera is the empty state; the desktop sheet shows the QR with "Scan with your phone · or drop a photo here". `loading`: transcription shows a per-page progress line "Reading page 2…" with the page thumbnail dimmed; target <20 s p95 per page (05-HAND-002). `streaming`: transcript text commits page by page, never token by token, so the editable field is never rewritten under the cursor. `error`: transcription failure gives an InlineError per page with "Retake" and "Type it instead" (a plain textarea that sets `input_mode = typed`); an expired token shows the re-scan line. `offline`: shots are stored locally with their crop, the page strip works, transcription queues and the confirm step opens when connectivity returns; the student is told which pages are waiting. `gated/quota`: camera Marks count against the levels-Mark quota only at Submit, never at capture, so a student is never blocked from photographing work.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Volume keys fire the shutter; long-press the shutter takes a burst and keeps the sharpest frame. On the transcript, `Tab` jumps to the next low-confidence token (desktop), swipe-down on mobile does the same via a "Next to check" chip; editing a flagged token clears its underline; every edit is diffed into `transcript_edits`. Desktop `⌘⇧P` opens the capture sheet; scanning the QR moves capture to the phone while the desktop shows a live "Page 2 received" list and takes over the confirm step when **Done** is pressed on the phone. Pinch-zoom inside the image disables sheet drag (09.7 Sheet).

**Data.** Reads: the bound Session/run/Attempt, Course subject (routes maths pages to Mathpix per 05-HAND-002), tenant retention policy. Writes: `attempt_images {page_index, storage_key, crop, deskew_deg, blur_score, quality_override}`, `attempt.transcript`, `attempt.transcript_confidence[]`, `attempt.transcript_confirmed`, `transcript_edits {token_index, from, to}` (the OCR eval corpus, 05-HAND-009), `input_mode = handwritten`, `illegible_tokens[]`. Originals are kept encrypted per tenant policy (default 12 months), visible to the teacher of record for override review, never used for training (05-HAND-008).

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-CAPT-001 | Marking never starts on an unconfirmed transcript: `transcript_confirmed = true` is a precondition of the mark job. | Attempt to enqueue a Mark with the flag false: rejected with `E_TRANSCRIPT_UNCONFIRMED`; 0 such Marks in production logs. |
| 10-CAPT-002 | Tokens with confidence <0.7 are underlined and reachable in one gesture; `[illegible]` is rendered as a labelled chip and never as a guessed word. | Fixture page with 8 low-confidence tokens: 8 underlines, 8 focus stops, 0 substitutions. |
| 10-CAPT-003 | A page with >10 % flagged tokens shows "Retake for a better Mark" before **Looks right** and records `retake_prompted`. | Fixture at 12 % flags. |
| 10-CAPT-004 | Crossings-out survive to the transcript as struck text and are never silently deleted. | Fixture with a crossed-out wrong answer: `~~…~~` present; the Mark credits neither penalises it. |
| 10-CAPT-005 | QR hand-off works cross-device on one network hop: scan → capture → Done → desktop confirm, with the token single-use and 30-minute-expiring. | Two-device Playwright run; replaying the token fails. |
| 10-CAPT-006 | Multi-page order is the student's: drag **and** a keyboard/tap alternative, with page numbers shown on both the strip and the confirm header. | Reorder by ⋯ only, keyboard-driven; order persists to `attempt_images.page_index`. |
| 10-CAPT-007 | Transcript visible <20 s p95 per page on the mid-tier Android profile; capture-to-confirm never blocks a running exam timer. | Timing soak on 200 pages; trace assertion from 10-RUNR-004. |
| 10-CAPT-008 | Every transcript edit is stored as a diff and feeds the OCR evaluation set; no edit is discarded. | 100 % of confirm events with edits produce `transcript_edits` rows. |
| 10-CAPT-009 | The confirm screen honours the reading-comfort object (09-READ-001), including OpenDyslexic and 24 px text, without breaking the side-by-side alignment. | Screenshot audit at 24 px / 1.7 leading. |

## 10.9 Papers
**Purpose.** Course › Papers is the index of every past paper and original paper-format Item set for the Course, with the student's own status on each. It is a *finder and launcher*, never a host: Margin deep-links to official public PDFs and renders question text only where provenance is OWN or LICENSED (§06).

**Stage.** MVP. (The logged-out public finder is a different object and is 10.27.)

**Jobs served.** J7 (exam-style questions that behave like the real ones), J8 (where do I stand on this paper), J12 (find what I have not done); 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-006.

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/papers`; a row deep-links to `/c/:courseId/papers?series=s24&component=2&variant=2`, and `Start timed mock` mints `/paper/:runId` (10.10). Deep-linking requires Course membership; no paper text is served to a signed-out request from this route.

**Layout.** Desktop: sidebar (Course tree, 280) · centre list (1fr, table max 960 px, not text-measured) · right pane rail (48) that opens to a Paper card on row focus. Tablet: list + 420 px card. Mobile: list route; row tap pushes the Paper card as a full route; filters in a 50% sheet.

```
┌ Papers · 9609 Business · June 2027 ──────────────────── [Filters ▾] [Start timed mock] ┐
│ Series ▾  Component ▾  Variant ▾  Status ▾  Syllabus point ▾  Tariff ▾  ☐ Original items │
├───────┬────────┬───┬──────────────────────┬───────────┬──────────────┬─────────────────────┤
│ s24   │ P2     │ 2 │ Paper  MS  ER  ↗      │ Marked    │ best 41–45/60│ 4 of 6 parts · 1 h  │
│ s24   │ P2     │ 1 │ Paper  MS  ER  ↗      │ Done      │ awaiting Mark│ submitted 2 d ago   │
│ w23   │ P1     │ 1 │ Paper  MS  ER  ↗      │ In progress│ —           │ 2 of 8 parts        │
│ m23   │ P3     │ 2 │ Paper  MS  ↗ (ER n/a) │ Not started│ —           │ 100 marks · 3 h     │
│ OWN   │ P2-like│ — │ 8 original Items      │ Not started│ —           │ generated, gated    │
└───────┴────────┴───┴──────────────────────┴───────────┴──────────────┴─────────────────────┘
```

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Header | title "Papers · {syllabus_code} · {exam_session}", Pack version chip, `Start timed mock` (primary, opens a picker: paper → variant → full paper or "one section, 30 min"), `Filters` |
| Filter row | Series (m/s/w + year, multi), Component (P1–P4), Variant (1/2/3, "any"), Status, Syllabus point (tree picker, uses the Pack's question→point map), Tariff (2–5 · 8–12 · 20+), "Original items only" toggle. Persisted per Course. |
| Row | series · component · variant · **deep-link chips** `Paper` `MS` `ER` (18 h CitationChip style, `↗` glyph, `rel="noopener"`, opens the official page in a new tab) · status pill · best band (BandTotal, tabular) · one 13 px context line (parts done, time, marks) |
| Paper card (right pane) | question list with tariff pill, command word, mapped syllabus points and pips; per-part status (✓ marked · ○ unattempted · ▸ in progress · "Seen solution"); last three Attempts as band chips; `Start timed`, `Practise one question`, `Open official PDF ↗`, `Debrief` (if marked) |

**States.**

| State | Rendering |
|---|---|
| empty (no Pack papers yet) | one sentence "Papers for {code} arrive with the Pack — {n} original Items are ready now." + `Practise` |
| loading | 8 skeleton rows matching row geometry |
| unavailable link | chip renders `MS · not published` in `--text-muted` when the board has not released it (Cambridge lag ≈ 6–9 months); never a broken link |
| licence-blocked text | question text replaced by "Open your copy of {series} {component} and answer Q{n} here" (Paper Companion mode, §06) |
| offline | list and statuses from cache; deep-link chips disabled with tooltip "Needs a connection" |
| gated | `Start timed mock` is always enabled and nothing about a mock is ever blocked. The picker states, before start, how many of the paper's levels parts the remaining quota covers ("This mock has 4 levels parts; 3 of your 3 daily levels Marks are left, so one part gets a practice estimate now and its Mark at 00:00"); the mechanics are 10.10's Submit sheet, and no other sentence in §10 describes mock quota. One full-paper mock a week is marked in full on Free in addition to the daily allowance (`limits.yaml` `mock:`), so the free tier can do the thing the product is for |

**Interactions.** Row click → card; `Enter` on row → Start timed; `o` → official PDF; `d` → debrief; `⌘F` filters. Sorting: series desc, then component, then variant. Statuses: `not_started` → `in_progress` (any autosaved part) → `done` (submitted) → `marked` (all gradable parts have a Mark) ; `best` = highest modal band across Attempts at full-paper scope.

**Data.** Read: `pack.papers[]`, `pack.paper_instances{series, component, variant, links{qp, ms, er}, total_marks, minutes, status ∈ {published, unpublished, ssh_only}}`, `pack.questions[]` (metadata always; text only where `provenance ≠ deep_link`), `attempts` aggregated per instance, tenant uploads (SSH material, teacher-scoped, shown with the `USER` chip and a "Your school's copy" label). Written: nothing except filter preferences.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-PAPR-001 | No board PDF, mark scheme or ER is ever fetched, cached or proxied by Margin's servers; deep-link chips point at the official public URL stored in the Pack. | Network capture during a full Papers session shows no request to a board domain from Margin infrastructure; every chip href host ∈ Pack `official_hosts[]`. |
| 10-PAPR-002 | Every row exposes at least Paper and MS links or an explicit "not published" state; no dead links. | Nightly link check: 0 responses ≥400 on stored URLs, else the chip flips to `unavailable` automatically. |
| 10-PAPR-003 | Status and best band are projections of Attempts and update within 2 s of a Mark committing. | Mark a part; row and card re-render without reload. |
| 10-PAPR-004 | The syllabus-point filter returns only questions whose Pack mapping has `confidence ≥ 0.8`; lower-confidence mappings show a "mapping under review" tag. | Fixture with a 0.6-confidence mapping is excluded from the filter and tagged in the card. |
| 10-PAPR-005 | SSH material uploaded by a teacher is visible only inside that tenant and carries the USER chip. | Cross-tenant query returns 0 rows; RLS test. |
| 10-PAPR-006 | Original Items appear as a paper-format set only after generator checks pass (§05.10: solvability ≥98%, style, near-duplicate). | Set row hidden when any Item has `gen_status ≠ passed`. |
| 10-PAPR-007 | The mock picker states quota consumption before start; nothing is consumed until Submit; starting is never blocked. | Abandon a mock before Submit: meter unchanged. A Free student who has used 3 of 3 daily Marks can still start, submit and fully receive one weekly mock, and the counter names that allowance separately. |

Why: hosting is the licensing kill risk (§06); students already live on official PDFs and "screenshot the questions I get wrong" (student-voice §4) — the value is status, timing and marking on top, not a mirror.

## 10.10 Paper runner / Timed mock
**Purpose.** The Paper runner turns a past paper (or a 30-minute section, or an OWN paper-format set) into a timed, question-by-question sitting with real tariff, real minutes and no help. It produces one Attempt per question part; the debrief (10.11) consumes them.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J7 (exam-style questions under exam conditions), J3 (the highest-value block tonight), J6 (photograph my handwritten answers at the end); 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-006.

**Route.** `/paper/:runId`, `?part=<n>` for the current position; `/paper/:runId/debrief` after Submit. A run is bound to one `paper_instance` and one student; the route refuses a second concurrent run of the same instance and resumes the existing one.

**Layout.** Desktop: the three-pane grid collapses to `72px 1fr 0` — the sidebar becomes a **question nav rail** (72 px, tariff pills), the right pane is closed and cannot open, the centre holds one part at a time at 68 ch. Tablet identical. Mobile: one part per screen, timer in the 44 pt top bar, nav via a bottom sheet (50%) listing parts.

```
┌ 9609/22 · s24 · Paper 2 · 90 min · 60 marks ────────── 47:12 (+25%) ▸ Rest ▸ Submit ┐
│1a 2│ Q3(b)  12 marks · Evaluate · AO1–4 · syllabus 3.2, 5.1                        │
│1b 4│ [Question text — OWN/LICENSED — or "Answer Q3(b) from your copy"]             │
│2a 2│                                                                                 │
│2b 8│ ┌ Typed · Maths · Handwritten · Photo ─────────────────────────────────────┐   │
│3a 8│ │ Typed answer area · 17/1.55 · grows · autosave 5 s                        │   │
│3b12│ └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── 214 words ┘   │
│4  20│                                              [◂ Previous]   [Next ▸]         │
└─────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Header | paper reference · series · minutes · total marks · **Timer** (20 px tabular, extra-time % badge inherited from Course settings, e.g. "+25%") · `Rest` (pauses; count and length from Course `rest_breaks {count, minutes}`) · `Submit` |
| Nav rail | one pill per part: label + tariff ("3b 12"); states unanswered (hairline) · answered (`--bg-el`) · flagged (▸ glyph) · current (`--bg-el-active`); keyboard `⌘↑/↓` |
| Part header | question id · tariff · command word (verbatim) · AO span for this paper · mapped syllabus points (no pips — no mastery hints in timed mode) |
| Question body | OWN/LICENSED text, or the Paper Companion line; insert/case-study material as a collapsible block above Q1 with page anchors |
| Answer area | input modes as tabs: **Typed** (composer without mode chip, effort dial, `+`, slash menu or IntentRow) · **Maths** (MathLive field + keypad, ⌘⇧M) · **Handwritten** (stylus canvas, pressure, undo, one page per part, PNG stored) · **Photo** (one or more photos per part, taken at any time; transcription and confirm (10.8) run **after Submit**, so the timer is never spent on confirmation) |
| Footer | Previous / Next · flag-for-review toggle (`f`) · word count (typed) · "Predict" card before Q1 only |

**Predict-before-start.** Before the timer starts, one card: "Your predicted total for this paper" (a band slider 0–60 in 3-mark steps) + ConfidenceSlider, plus a fourth option **Not sure** that stores `predict = null, predict_skipped = true` and starts the paper. One tap before a 90-minute commitment is a fair ask; a wall is not. Stored as `attempt_set.predict`. Why: predict–postdict–compare is the calibration loop (§04.14).

**No-help rule.** While the runner is open: no tutor Turn can be sent; the ModeChip, IntentRow, HelpLadder, CitationChips, Library and Cards are not rendered; ⌘K exposes only `Go to part`, `Flag`, `Rest`, `Submit`, `Reading comfort`; pasting text from outside the app is permitted but tagged `paste_events[]` on the Attempt (shown to teachers on assignments). Leaving the route shows a sheet "Leave the mock? The timer keeps running for 2 minutes, then the paper submits" — tap alternative to drag.

**Timer.** `minutes × (1 + extra_time_pct)`; ticks without animation; at 0 the label becomes "+00:01" in the same colour and the paper continues (overtime is recorded per part as `overtime_s`); no sound, no red, no modal (09.7 Timer). Rest pauses the timer and greys the answer area; rests beyond the Course allowance are allowed but logged `rest_over_allowance=true`.

**Autosave and resume.** Every 5 s and on blur, per part, local-first (IndexedDB) then server; a crash or tab close resumes at the same part with the timer advanced by wall-clock time; offline continues locally and syncs on Submit.

**Submit.** Sheet lists unanswered and flagged parts, photo parts awaiting confirmation, and the quota line ("Marking this paper uses 3 levels Marks"). Confirm → Attempts close (`timed=true, minutes_used, per-part latency`) → photo parts go through 10.8 confirm → Marks stream in the debrief. Mobile keeps Submit in the bottom 40%.

**States.**

| State | Rendering |
|---|---|
| loading | header + first part skeleton; timer starts only after the first part paints |
| offline | banner; local autosave continues; Submit queues ("Will submit"), Marks stream on reconnect |
| error (save failure) | inline card under the answer: "Not saved since 47:30. Retry · Copy my answer"; timer unaffected |
| quota | if levels Marks remaining < levels parts: start is always allowed; the Submit sheet states which parts receive "Practice estimate" feedback now and a Mark when the quota renews (never a stop). This is the only description of mock quota mechanics in §10; 10.9's picker states the same numbers before start and adds nothing |
| exam-week | identical; the debrief hides predicted grade and rings (§03.4) |

**Data.** Read: `paper_instance`, `questions`, Course `{extra_time_pct, rest_breaks, bedtime}`. Written: `attempt_set {paper_instance_id, predict, started_at, submitted_at, minutes_used, rests[]}`; per part `attempt {response | canvas_png | photos[], input_mode, latency_s, overtime_s, flagged, paste_events[]}`.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-RUNR-001 | No help surface is reachable from the runner. | DOM audit during a mock: 0 instances of ModeChip, IntentRow, HelpLadder, CitationChip; ⌘K verb list = the five allowed verbs. |
| 10-RUNR-002 | Extra time and rest breaks are read from Course settings, never set per paper. | Change Course `extra_time_pct` 0→25: next mock header shows "+25%" and total minutes ×1.25. |
| 10-RUNR-003 | Autosave loses ≤5 s of typing on hard crash. | Kill the tab mid-sentence; resume shows text up to the last 5 s tick. |
| 10-RUNR-004 | Photo confirmation happens after Submit and never consumes timer minutes. | Trace: `timer_stopped_at ≤ confirm_opened_at`. |
| 10-RUNR-005 | Timer overtime changes label only; no colour, sound or modal. | Visual regression at 00:00 and −00:01. |
| 10-RUNR-006 | The predict card is required before a 90-minute commitment, but it is not a wall: a fourth option **Not sure** stores `predict = null, predict_skipped = true` and starts the paper immediately. Predict rows with `predict_skipped` are dropped from the Brier computation rather than coerced into a guess. | 0 `attempt_set` rows that reach `started_at` without one of the four options recorded; a scripted run choosing "Not sure" starts the timer in one tap. |
| 10-RUNR-007 | Mobile: one part per screen; Next/Previous/Submit within the bottom 40%. | Screenshot audit on 360×780. |
| 10-RUNR-008 | Section mode ("30 minutes") selects parts by cumulative tariff × minutes-per-mark ≤ 30 and never splits a multi-part question. | Unit test on s24 P2: Section A only. |

## 10.11 Mock debrief
**Purpose.** The Mock debrief is the Principal-Examiner report on one sitting: what each part earned, where the time went, which AO leaked marks, the three things to fix, and when to retest. It is generated only from the sitting's Marks and the ER corpus (§05.9) — never from a model's impression of the paper.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J1 (what would an examiner give this), J8 (how do I stand against real thresholds), J10 (what turns a Level 2 into a Level 3), J12 (schedule the retests); 02-MOM-003, 02-MOM-006.

**Route.** `/paper/:runId/debrief`, `#part=<id>` opens a part's Mark in the right pane; reachable from Papers (10.9), Home › Continue and `⌘K → Go to debrief`.

**Layout.** Desktop: sidebar shows the question nav (same rail as the runner, now with band chips); centre is the debrief at 68 ch; right pane opens on the Mark of the selected part (10.7 content) and defaults to the first lost-mark cluster. Tablet `1fr 420px`. Mobile: single scroll; parts table collapses to a card list; Mark opens as a full route.

```
┌ Debrief · 9609/22 s24 P2 · sat 6 Sep, 88 of 90 min ───────────────────────────────┐
│ Postdict first: "Now that it's done, what did you score?"  [band slider] [Sure▾]  │
│                                        Show me the marks (appears after 10 s)     │
│ ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  │
│ Component mark   41–45 / 60   Calibrated · agreement 0.82 · 312 scripts            │
│ Predicted 36–39 · postdict 44–47 · gap +6: you under-predicted, AO4 was better than you feared │
│ Thresholds (historical, s24/s23/w23): A 44 · B 38 · C 32   — 1 mark to A on s24 line │
│ Grade band: [only if 05.8 unlock is met — else the unlock checklist]               │
├ Parts ─────────┬──────┬──────────┬───────────┬──────┬──────────────────────────────┤
│ 1(a) Define    │ 2    │ 2 / 2    │ 1.1 vs 3 min│ AO1  │ ✓                             │
│ 1(b) Explain   │ 6    │ 4 / 6    │ 8 vs 9 min  │ AO1–2│ AO2 — one generic example     │
│ 2(a) Analyse   │ 12   │ 7–8 / 12 │ 22 vs 18 min│ AO1–3│ AO3 L2 — chain stops at one   │
│ 2(b) Evaluate  │ 20   │ 13–15/20 │ 31 vs 30 min│ AO1–4│ AO4 L1 — no weighed judgement │
├ AO profile (denominators from the Pack's P2 weights) ─────────────────────────────┤
│ AO1 █████░░░░░ 11/18  AO2 ████░░░░░░ 8/18   AO3 ███████░░░ 8/12   AO4 ███░░░░░░░ 4/12 │
├ Three priorities ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1  Application is generic (AO2, −7). ER s23: "…could apply to any business…" [ER]  │
│ 2  Judgement not weighed (AO4, −6). ER m25 phrase [ER]                             │
│ 3  Time: Q4 overran by 6 min; Q1 under-used. Plan a 20-mark rehearsal at 30 min.   │
│ [Schedule 3 retests]  [Turn lost marks into Cards]  [Challenge a Mark]  [Share receipt] │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| Postdict card | rendered **first**, before any mark is visible; band slider + ConfidenceSlider; stores `attempt_set.postdict`. The rest of the screen stays skeletoned while it is open, but it is not a wall: a plain secondary **Show me the marks** appears after 10 s and stores `postdict = null, postdict_skipped = true`, and the card is auto-skipped when `affect_tag ∈ {distress, crisis}` in the last 24 h or in between-papers mode. Skipped rows are dropped from the Brier computation and the postdict is re-offered once after the marks have been read (10-DBRF-009). |
| Component header | BandTotal for the whole paper (sum of modal marks with combined low/high) + CalibrationBadge (worst status among parts governs: any `uncalibrated` part → whole-paper total not shown, per-part feedback still shown); predict vs postdict vs actual line in plain words; last-three-series thresholds labelled "historical" with the series named; "marks to next grade" per series line |
| Grade band slot | shows "B–A (s24–w23 thresholds) · spread ±4 marks · practice estimate, not a board mark" **only** when §05.8 unlock is met (paper gated, ≥3 series thresholds loaded, ≥3 marked Attempts on this paper by this student). Otherwise a three-row checklist with ✓/○ per condition ("2 of 3 marked attempts on P2"). Hidden entirely in exam week for that component. |
| Parts table | part · command word · tariff · Mark band · **time vs tariff time** (`tariff × minutes_per_mark` from Pack, e.g. 1.5 min/mark on P2) with over/under stated as minutes, never coloured red · AO ceiling · one-line reason from the Mark's `next_mark_advice` |
| AO profile | one bar per AO: marks gained / marks available in *this* paper, where the denominator is computed as `pack.papers[p].ao_weights[ao] × paper.marks` and never hard-coded (10-DBRF-008). For 9609 P2 that is AO1 18 · AO2 18 · AO3 12 · AO4 12 of 60; a Pack with three AOs renders three bars. Label + numbers, single hue |
| Time chart | per-part bars, actual vs tariff time, tabular labels; overtime parts get "+m:ss" text |
| Three priorities | ranked by marks lost × recurrence across the student's last five Marks; each = AO, marks lost, ER phrase (≤40 words, attributed, deep link) and one action; at most three |
| Actions | `Schedule 3 retests` (proposes Plan blocks at 24–72 h / 7 d / pre-exam; opens Plan in proposed state), `Turn lost marks into Cards` (one Card per uncredited marking point, provenance "From your mock · s24 P2 Q3(b)"), `Challenge a Mark` (10.22), `Share receipt` (10.23) |

**Streaming.** Order: postdict gate → per-part Marks stream one at a time in paper order (each obeys 09-STRM-011) → AO profile and time chart compute client-side from committed Marks → the narrative (priorities) streams last and only after all Marks commit. A part whose Mark fails shows an InlineError in its row; the debrief still renders from the remaining parts and states "2 parts not yet marked".

**States.**

| State | Rendering |
|---|---|
| loading | postdict card live; everything below skeletoned to final geometry |
| partial quota | parts beyond quota show "Practice estimate — Mark at 00:00 or on Student" in the band column; component total shows "n of m parts marked" |
| offline | if the sitting was submitted offline: "Marks will stream when you're back"; postdict still collectable |
| exam week | grade slot, thresholds line and any ring hidden; priorities and retests kept |
| challenge pending | affected part shows "Re-mark in progress"; totals recompute on outcome |

**Data.** Read: `attempt_set`, `attempts[]`, `marks[]`, `pack.thresholds{series, component, grade→mark}`, `pack.minutes_per_mark`, ER corpus citations from each Mark. Written: `attempt_set.postdict`, `plan_proposals[]`, `cards[]`, `challenge` rows.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-DBRF-001 | No mark, band or threshold is visible while the postdict card is open, and the card offers **Show me the marks** from 10 s onward. | DOM before the postdict is answered or skipped: 0 numerals in the header region; the escape appears at 10 s and the marks render on click. |
| 10-DBRF-002 | The grade band renders only when all three §05.8 conditions are true; otherwise the checklist renders with live counts. | Fixtures: (gated, 3 series, 2 attempts) → checklist "2 of 3"; (gated, 3, 3) → band. |
| 10-DBRF-003 | Every priority carries an attributed ER extract ≤40 words with a deep link, or no ER line at all; the priority is never invented prose. | Copy audit: each ER line matches an `examiner_insights` row id. |
| 10-DBRF-004 | Thresholds are labelled with the series used and the word "historical"; no threshold for a future series is ever shown. | String test on the header. |
| 10-DBRF-005 | Time vs tariff uses Pack `minutes_per_mark`; deviations are stated in minutes, no colour semantics. | Visual regression in greyscale identical to colour. |
| 10-DBRF-006 | "Schedule 3 retests" creates *proposed* Plan blocks, never accepted ones. | Plan state after click: 3 blocks `proposed`. |
| 10-DBRF-007 | Whole-paper streaming completes ≤45 s × parts p95 with per-part InlineError isolation. | Inject one failing part in a 6-part fixture: 5 Marks render, 1 error card, totals say "5 of 6". |
| 10-DBRF-008 | AO denominators are computed from `pack.papers[p].ao_weights × paper.marks`; a hard-coded denominator anywhere in the debrief fails the render test, and a Pack with three AOs renders three bars. | Render the 9609 P2 fixture: denominators 18/18/12/12 of 60; render a 9708 fixture: three bars, no AO4. Grep the component for numeric denominators returns 0. **PROVISIONAL** until 15-OPEN-010 confirms the P2 structure from the syllabus PDF; the weights, not the fixture, are the source. |
| 10-DBRF-009 | The postdict is requested, skippable and re-offered once — never a gate between a frightened student and their marks. The escape stores `postdict_skipped` with its reason (`user_skipped · distress · between_papers`), the card auto-skips when a distress or crisis affect tag was set in the last 24 h, and Brier simply uses fewer points. | Fixture with `affect_tag = distress` at T−2 h: marks render immediately, no postdict card, `skip_reason = distress`; a normal fixture re-offers the postdict once after the marks are read. |

Why: post-mock is where students burn out or quit (student-voice §8); the debrief must be evidence, thresholds and three moves, not a grade headline.

## 10.12 Cards / Review
**Purpose.** Course › Cards is the retrieval engine: FSRS-6 decks fed automatically by Marks, wrong questions and reader highlights, reviewed with two buttons, tuned to the paper date.

**Stage.** MVP (`calc_regen` cards are v1 and marked so in the card-type table).

**Jobs served.** J12 (review what is due), J4 (make it stick), J2 (retest what I got wrong); 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-005.

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/cards` (deck list) · `/review/:deckId` (full-screen review) · `/c/:courseId/cards/wrong` (10.13). `?new=1` opens the editor.

**Layout.** Deck list: sidebar tree (280) · centre deck table · right pane rail. Review: **full-screen route** on every breakpoint (no sidebar, no pane); mobile buttons in the bottom 20 %. Editor: right-pane Cards tab (desktop) or a 92 % sheet (mobile).

```
┌ Cards · 9609 ── Due today 18 · about 6 min ──────────── [Review due] [New card] [Import] ┐
│ Deck                         │ Due │ New │ Cards │ Retention target │ Last review        │
│ From your Marks              │  7  │  2  │  64   │ 0.90 (P2 in 5 w) │ yesterday          │
│ From wrong questions         │  6  │  0  │  31   │ 0.90             │ yesterday          │
│ From highlights · Hodder ch.3│  3  │  4  │  22   │ 0.85             │ 3 d ago            │
│ Imported · "Business defs"   │  2  │  0  │ 180   │ 0.85             │ 6 d ago            │
└──────────────────────────────┴─────┴─────┴───────┴──────────────────┴────────────────────┘
Review (full-screen):  ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬░░░ 12 of 30
  [ face: "Define *working capital*." ]  (Space to flip)
  From your Mark · 9609 P2 Q1(a) · s24        [Again  ·  <1 d]   [Got it  ·  9 d]
```

**Scheduling rules (FSRS-6, §04.12).** Desired retention by phase: 0.85 (>8 weeks to the paper), 0.90 (3–8), 0.95 (<3); intervals capped at the paper date of the card's mapped component; Cepeda prior for new material inside 8 weeks; overconfident-wrong Items (probe said "Sure", answer wrong) get interval ×0.5; a rung-5 "seen solution" card cannot count toward Secure. **Exam-anchored caps** per Course per day: `review_cap = max(20, planned_review_minutes × 3.5)` (median 17 s per card); `new_cap` = 20 in Phase A, 10 in Phase B, 0 in Phase C unless the student overrides in Settings ("I know — show new cards anyway"); caps are shown as plain numbers on the deck list, never as a lock.

**Card types.**

| Type | Face / back | Generated from |
|---|---|---|
| `basic` | prompt / answer (KaTeX, images ≤60 % height) | highlights, editor |
| `definition` | term / syllabus-verbatim definition + citation chip | syllabus glossary, Marks with AO1 loss |
| `cloze` | text with `{{c1::…}}` (multiple clozes → sibling cards, buried same day) | editor, Turns via "Save as card" |
| `worked_step` | previous steps shown, one step blank / the step + why | points Marks (uncredited M/A step), Help Ladder rung 4 |
| `marking_point` | question stem + "what earns the next mark here?" / marking point in our wording + ER phrase | levels Marks (uncredited attempt), wrong-question bank |
| `misconception` | contrast pair (two cases differing in one feature) / which is which and why | §04.6 misconception diagnosis |
| `calc_regen` (v1) | numeric item with regenerated values / SymPy-checked answer | points Items |

Every card carries `provenance {kind, mark_id | attempt_id | source_id+page, created_by ∈ {system, student, teacher, import}}` rendered as the 12 px provenance line.

**Review interaction.** `Space`/tap flips (200 ms; 150 ms crossfade reduced); `1` Again · `3` Got it; Hard/Easy appear only when Settings › Cards › "Four buttons" is on (`2`/`4`); interval hints under each button (hideable); `e` edit in place; `s` suspend; `⌘Z` undo last grade (one step); `Esc` ends the session with the three-line end card ("30 reviewed · estimated retention 0.91 · next review tomorrow"). No haptic on Again (09-MOTN-006). Provenance line click opens the source Mark or page in the right pane after the session, not during.

**Generation.** On Mark commit: one `marking_point` card per uncredited attempt with a `marking_point_id`, one `definition` card per AO1 term missed; deduplicated by `(item_id, marking_point_id)`; new cards enter as `proposed` in the "From your Marks" deck and a Toast offers "3 cards added · Undo". Wrong-question entries (10.13) generate `misconception` or `worked_step` cards when the diagnosis is `misconception` or `prerequisite`.

**Import / export.** Import `.apkg` (Anki 2.1 schema: notes, cards, revlog → FSRS state seeded from revlog), Quizlet/CSV (`term,definition`), plain text pairs; imported decks are USER provenance, private, never pooled; mapping to syllabus points is offered per deck via the Pack's embedding match with a confirm step. Export: `.apkg`, CSV, JSON (with FSRS state); export never strips provenance lines.

**States.** empty: "Cards appear here from your Marks and highlights." + `Make one`; loading: deck rows skeletoned; **offline: full review with local FSRS-6, grades queued, sync on reconnect with per-card log replay (server wins only on identical timestamps)**; error (sync conflict): silent merge, toast "2 reviews merged"; quota: none — Cards are never metered (SYNTHESIS §12 free-tier rule).

**Data.** Read: `cards`, `card_state{stability, difficulty, due, reps, lapses, last_review}`, Course paper dates, phase. Written: `reviews {card_id, rating ∈ {again, hard, good, easy}, elapsed_s, at}`, `cards` (create/edit/suspend), `card_state`.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-CARD-001 | Two-button grading by default; Hard/Easy only behind the setting. | Fresh account: exactly two buttons in the review DOM. |
| 10-CARD-002 | No interval ever exceeds the mapped paper date; after the paper, the deck archives silently (§03.4). | Unit test: card due > paper date is clamped; post-paper deck `archived=true`. |
| 10-CARD-003 | Desired retention follows the phase table and is displayed per deck. | Move the exam date across the 8-week boundary: deck target flips 0.85→0.90. |
| 10-CARD-004 | Full offline review: 50 cards reviewed in airplane mode sync with zero loss and identical FSRS state to an online run. | Deterministic replay test. |
| 10-CARD-005 | Every generated card has a resolvable provenance target; a card without provenance fails creation. | 0 rows with `provenance = null`. |
| 10-CARD-006 | Anki import seeds FSRS from revlog; 1,000-note deck imports ≤20 s with a progress line. | Fixture deck; timing in CI. |
| 10-CARD-007 | Overconfident-wrong halving is applied and visible in the interval hint. | Probe "Sure" + wrong → next hint half of the baseline fixture. |
| 10-CARD-008 | Median grading time ≤3 s per card; no card face exceeds 60 words without a "long" tag surfaced in the editor. | Telemetry; editor lint. |

Why: students say card-making is procrastination bait and pre-made decks are boring but Anki "is the best thing in the world" (student-voice §4); the fix is cards that arrive from their own lost marks, scheduled to the exam, and never paywalled.

## 10.13 Wrong-question bank
**Purpose.** The Wrong-question bank is the automatic ledger of every lost mark — the thing students build by hand with screenshots — re-attemptable with its marking point, tagged by misconception, scheduled by FSRS, linked to mastery.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J2 (why did I lose the mark), J12 (retest what I got wrong), J10 (stop losing the same AO), J8 (which solutions have I only *seen*); 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-006.

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/cards/wrong`, surfaced as Course › Progress › Wrong questions, `⌘K → Open wrong questions`, and the "Wrong" chip on Course home; `?point=3.2` and `?status=open` are filter params so a link carries a view.

**Layout.** Desktop: sidebar tree acts as the syllabus-point filter; centre list at max 960 px; right pane opens the original Mark (10.7) on row focus, with the lost span pre-scrolled. Mobile: list route; row → Mark route; filters as a sheet.

```
┌ Wrong questions · 9609 · 23 open · 5 due for retest today ──── [Retest 5 due] [Filters ▾] ┐
│ Point  │ Question              │ Tariff│ Lost       │ Tag                 │ Retest  │ Status │
│ 3.2    │ s24 P2 Q3(b) Evaluate │ 12    │ AO4 −4     │ misconception: judgement-as-summary │ today │ open   │
│ 5.1    │ OWN P2-like Q2(a)     │ 8     │ AO2 −3     │ misconception: name-as-application  │ Thu   │ seen solution │
│ 1.4    │ w23 P1 Q1(c) Calculate│ 4     │ M1 −1      │ slip: sign error    │ —       │ retested ✓ (1 of 2) │
│ 2.3    │ s23 P1 Q4 Analyse     │ 8     │ AO3 −2     │ prerequisite: 2.1 elasticity │ Fri │ open   │
└────────┴───────────────────────┴───────┴────────────┴─────────────────────┴─────────┴────────┘
```

**Entry creation (automatic).** On every Mark commit, one entry per lost unit: for levels papers, per AO where `marks < max` (grouped, with `uncredited_attempts[]` and the descriptor gap); for points papers, per marking point not credited (`M1`, `A1`…). Fields:

```json
{ "id": "wq_…", "attempt_id": "…", "mark_id": "…", "item_id": "…",
  "syllabus_points": ["3.2"], "paper_ref": "9609/22 s24 Q3(b)", "tariff": 12,
  "lost": {"ao": "AO4", "marks": 4, "marking_point_id": null, "descriptor_gap": "L1→L2: judgement weighed against context"},
  "diagnosis": {"class": "slip|misconception|prerequisite|unclassified", "label": "judgement-as-summary", "prereq_point": null},
  "seen_solution": false, "retest_due": "2026-09-08", "retests": [{"at": "...", "unaided": true, "correct": false}],
  "status": "open|retested_wrong|retested_correct|mastered|dismissed", "card_ids": [] }
```

`diagnosis` comes from the §04.6 classification when the student gave a reasoning trace; otherwise `unclassified` with a `Diagnose` action (opens a two-Turn Learn exchange that asks for the trace, then classifies). `seen_solution=true` when rung 5 or "Just the answer" was used on the Item; such entries retest on a **near-transfer variant** (same tariff and cognitive-demand tag, §05.10 generator), never the same Item.

**Filters and sort.** Syllabus point (tree), paper/series, tariff band, AO, diagnosis class and label, status, seen-solution only, date range, Course (cross-Course view on Home shows counts only). Default sort: retest due asc, then marks lost desc. Persisted per Course.

**Actions.** Row: `Retest` (opens the Practise runner 10.6 with the Item or its variant, Effort Gate on, entry rung from mastery), `Show Mark`, `Show in book` (citation of the descriptor/marking point), `Make card` (10.12 `marking_point`/`worked_step`), `Dismiss` (reason chips: "Teacher overrode", "Off-syllabus", "Duplicate"; kept in history). Header: `Retest 5 due` (an interleaved set, no more than 5 same-type in a row, §04.13) and `Diagnose 3 unclassified`.

**Status machine and mastery link.** `open` → retest unaided correct → `retested_correct (1 of 2)` → second unaided correct on a different day → `mastered` (feeds M_s via the correctness posterior; a seen-solution first attempt contributes 0, §07.2). Retest wrong → `retested_wrong`, FSRS shortens the next due, diagnosis re-asked once. Entries never delete; `mastered` and `dismissed` move to the History tab. A point's pip tooltip cites the bank: "¼ — 3 open wrong questions on 3.2".

**States.** empty (new Course): "Lost marks collect here after your first Mark." + `Mark an answer`; empty (all mastered): "Nothing open. 14 mastered this month." (no celebration animation); loading: rows skeletoned; offline: list from cache, `Retest` disabled for gradable Items with tooltip, `Make card` works; error: inline row card; quota: retests of *points* Items are unmetered; retests of levels Items follow the levels-Mark meter with the plain-number line before start.

**Data.** Read: `wrong_questions`, `marks`, `items`, `card_state`, mastery. Written: `wrong_questions` (status, diagnosis, retests[]), `attempts` (via runner), `cards`.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-WRNG-001 | Every lost mark in a committed Mark produces exactly one entry within 2 s; no manual step. | Mark fixture with 3 lost units → 3 rows; idempotent on re-mark (updated, not duplicated). |
| 10-WRNG-002 | Seen-solution entries retest on a variant, never the original Item text. | Retest of a `seen_solution=true` row: `item_id ≠ original`, `variant_of = original`. |
| 10-WRNG-003 | `mastered` requires two unaided correct retests on ≥2 distinct days. | State-machine unit test; same-day double correct stays `retested_correct`. |
| 10-WRNG-004 | Diagnosis labels come from the Pack's misconception list (≥3 per point) or `unclassified`; no free-text labels from the model. | Every `label` ∈ `pack.misconceptions[point]`. |
| 10-WRNG-005 | Retest sets interleave: ≤5 consecutive same-type Items. | Set builder unit test. |
| 10-WRNG-006 | Filters return in ≤200 ms p95 for a bank of 2,000 entries (client-side index). | Perf test. |
| 10-WRNG-007 | No shame copy: the header shows counts and due dates only; the lint lexicon (09-COPY-003 d) applies. | Copy catalogue test. |

Why: "screenshot the questions that u get wrong with their model answer then redo them a few days later" is the ritual students already run by hand (student-voice §4); automating it, with the marking point attached, is the product's clearest delta over a chat.

## 10.14 Progress / Calibration
**Purpose.** Course › Progress is the evidence view of the Learner Graph: mastery by syllabus point, readiness per paper, calibration, activity. It is the **only** surface (with the mock debrief) where a predicted grade band may appear, and only on the Paper tab under §05.8 rules.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J8 (where do I stand and how ready is Paper 2), J12 (what changed this week), J10 (am I over-confident); 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-004.

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/progress` with tabs `?tab=overview|paper|calibration|activity`, `/c/:courseId/progress/paper/:paperId`, and `/c/:courseId/cards/wrong` for the fifth tab (10.13).

**Layout.** Tabs (40 px strip): **Overview · Paper · Calibration · Activity · Wrong questions** (10.13). Desktop: sidebar tree (clicking a point filters every tab) · centre at max 960 px · right pane opens the point's evidence (Attempts list, last Mark) on pip focus. Mobile: tabs as a segmented control under the 44 pt bar; heatmaps scroll horizontally inside their own container.

```
Overview
┌ Mastery · 9609 · Pack 2026-28 v3 ─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1 Business and its environment   ○ ◔ ◕ ● ◕ ○ ◔      Secure 3 · Shaky 2 · Unseen 2 │
│ 2 Human resource management      ◕ ● ● ◔ ◕          …                          │
│ 3 Marketing                      ◔ ◔ ○ ◕ ◕ ○        3.2 ¼ — 3 open wrong questions │
│ … (official numbering, hover = reason)                                          │
├ This week ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3.2 Motivation ¼→¾ (2 unaided correct)  ·  1.4 ¾→¼ (last unaided 19 d ago)      │
│ Unaided delta on P2-type Items: +2.1 marks/12 over 4 weeks (n = 9)              │
│ Ladder depth: median rung 2 (was 3)  ·  Mastery velocity: 1.5 points/week → Secure │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
Paper
┌ Paper 2 · Thursday 11 June 2027 · 278 days ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  (ring 64 px)  Readiness ◕  "Decaying: 3 Shaky points weigh 22 of 60 marks."     │
│  Last mocks: s24 v2 41–45 · s23 v1 36–40 · w23 v2 33–37                          │
│  AO profile (last 3 mocks): AO1 0.9 · AO2 0.55 · AO3 0.7 · AO4 0.45 of available │
│  Predicted grade band ─ B–A · s24/s23/w23 thresholds · spread ±4 marks ·         │
│     practice estimate, not a board mark · 1 mark to A on the s24 line            │
│     (or: "No grade band yet: ✓ paper calibrated · ✓ 3 series loaded · ○ 2 of 3 marked attempts") │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Regions and elements.**

| Tab | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| Overview | **Mastery heatmap** = the syllabus tree laid out as rows of MasteryPips (shape-coded per 09-COLR-002, official numbering, unit totals as counts "Secure 3 · Shaky 2"); no percentage anywhere. "This week" list of state changes with the reason string from the graph. Three trend lines as numbers with n: unaided delta (marks per tariff on unseen Items of gated papers, the UMG numerator), median ladder rung, mastery velocity. |
| Paper | one card per component: ReadinessRing (arc = `Σ tariff_exposure_s × M_s / Σ tariff_exposure_s`, reason text = the largest decaying contributors), last three mock bands, AO profile from marks gained/available, **Predicted grade band** slot (rules below), `Start timed mock`, `Debrief` links. |
| Calibration | Brier score overall and per unit, weekly, as a line chart with n per point; **reliability diagram** with the three confidence buckets: "Sure: 40 said · 26 right (0.65)", "Think so: 31 · 19 (0.61)", "Guessing: 12 · 5 (0.42)" and the ideal line; **predict–postdict view** per mock: three bands on one axis (predicted · postdict · actual) with the gap in marks and one sentence generated from the gap sign ("You under-predict on P2: actual above predicted in 4 of 5 sittings"). |
| Activity | 12 px heatmap, five tones (09-COLR-003), tooltip minutes + topics; weekly sessions vs goal with rest days neutral; no streak count, no "longest run". |

**Predicted grade band rules (§05.8, SYNTHESIS §8.7).** Render only when **all** hold: (1) the paper's calibration status is `gated`; (2) ≥3 series of thresholds for that component are loaded in the Pack; (3) this student has ≥3 marked Attempts on that paper at full tariff. Content: option or component grade range from component raw → last-three-series thresholds → PUM; the series used; spread in marks; "marks to next grade" per series line; the fixed label "practice estimate, not a board mark". A* only at option level. When locked, render the three-condition checklist with live counts, never a greyed grade. Never render: on Home, in a notification, in the parent digest unless student-enabled (10.21), in a share card, or in exam week for that component.

**States.** empty (no Attempts): the tree with all pips Unseen and one line "Probes on this week's topics seed this map." + `Start probe`; loading: pips render immediately from cache, trends skeleton; offline: last synced state with a 13 px "as of 09:12" line; exam week: Paper tab hides ring, deltas and grade band for the component in its T−7 window, keeps AO profile and mock bands; error: per-tab inline card.

**Data.** Read: `mastery{point, M, state, retrievability, reason, changed_at}`, `attempts`, `marks`, `attempt_sets` (predict/postdict), `pack.thresholds`, `pack.tariff_exposure`, calibration status per paper. Written: none (view only); the sidebar filter persists.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-PROG-001 | A predicted grade band string appears in the DOM only on Progress › Paper and the debrief, only with the three conditions true. | Route crawl with a fixture meeting the conditions: string present on exactly those two routes; with any condition false: 0 occurrences app-wide. |
| 10-PROG-002 | Every rendered band carries series, spread and the "practice estimate, not a board mark" label. | Regex on the band component. |
| 10-PROG-003 | No `%` character and no numeric mastery appears in Overview; states are the four shapes with reasons. | Copy corpus test + DOM scan. |
| 10-PROG-004 | Ring arc equals the tariff-weighted mastery formula to ±0.01; reason names the largest decaying contributors. | Unit test on a 10-point fixture. |
| 10-PROG-005 | Reliability diagram shows counts, not only rates; buckets with n<5 render "too few to say". | Fixture with Guessing n=3. |
| 10-PROG-006 | Exam-week hiding is per component and time-boxed to T−7 → paper sat. | Clock test at T−8, T−7, T+1. |
| 10-PROG-007 | Pip hover/focus reason resolves in ≤100 ms from local state. | Perf test. |

Why: a predicted grade on Home is an anxiety device in an exam year already full of them (vision-craft §8 dissent on A24; student-voice §8); calibration — "you said Sure 40 times, 26 were right" — is the number that changes behaviour (§04.14).

## 10.15 Plan
**Purpose.** Course › Plan (and the cross-Course Plan on Home) is the calendar from today to each exam: weekly blocks proposed by the scheduler from the phase mix (§04.13), Mark-Yield priorities (§07.5) and decay, edited by the student, never silently applied.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J3 (what is the highest-value thing this week), J12 (fit the retests in), J9 (fit my teacher's assignment); 02-MOM-001, 02-MOM-002, 02-MOM-005.

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/plan`, `?week=YYYY-Www` for a specific week and `?day=YYYY-MM-DD` from the Home week strip; the cross-Course plan is `/home/plan`.

**Layout.** Desktop: sidebar shows the exam spine (all papers of all Courses, dated) · centre week view (7 columns × hours, 44 pt day headers) at max 1040 px · right pane opens "Why this block" on block focus. Tablet: week view + 420 px card. Mobile: day strip (7 × 44 pt) + a single day's block list; week switch by swipe with a button alternative.

```
┌ Plan · week of 7 Sep · Phase A (>8 weeks) · goal 4 sessions, 2 rest days ─ [Accept week] [ICS] ┐
│ Spine: P1 9 Jun · P2 11 Jun · P3 16 Jun (9609) · 9708 P2 3 Jun                                   │
│      Mon        Tue        Wed        Thu        Fri        Sat        Sun                        │
│ 16:00 ┌Review 25┐          ┌Practise 45┐          ┌Retest 25 ┐          ┌Paper 45 ┐               │
│       │Due cards │          │3.2 · 8-mark│          │s24 Q3(b) │          │section A│               │
│       │18 · 6 min│          │Mark-yield 1│          │variant   │          │timed    │               │
│ 17:00 └proposed─┘  rest    └proposed───┘  rest    └proposed──┘          └proposed─┘  Readiness    │
│ 18:30              ┌Assignment 45 · Mr Okafor · locks Fri 18:00 ┐                     review 10 min│
│ 22:00 ── sleep line (bedtime 22:30; nothing scheduled after) ─────────────────────────────────────│
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| Header | week label · phase chip ("Phase B · 5 weeks to P2") · weekly goal with rest days ("4 sessions · rest Tue, Fri") — hidden in exam weeks · `Accept week` · `ICS` · `Why this plan` |
| Exam spine | every component as a chip with tabular countdown; between-papers windows (T−7 per component) shaded; clicking a chip filters blocks to that paper |
| Block | 25 or 45 min; label = type (Review · Practise · Paper · Debrief · Retest · Assignment · Readiness review) + syllabus point or paper ref + one 12 px reason line; states per 09.7 PlanBlock: `proposed` (dashed) · `accepted` · `done` · `declined-late` · `missed` (neutral, rolls forward) · `locked` (assignment) |
| Why this block | the yield line in words and numbers: "3.2 Motivation: 8 marks of exposure on P2 · mastery ¼ · retrievability 0.55 by 11 Jun · est. 1.5 h → yield 2.7 marks/h, rank 1 of 14"; the pip it came from; `Swap for rank 2` |
| Sleep line and quiet hours | bedtime from Settings (default 22:30) greys the hours below; Quiet Hours block notifications, not study; in exam weeks no block ends after 22:00 and the planner declines with one sentence ("Late blocks cost more than they give in exam week — moved to 16:00 tomorrow.") |
| Teacher assignments | appear as `locked` blocks with the lock window ("opens Wed 08:00 · locks Fri 18:00") and allowed-help level; the student can move them inside the window, not outside |

**Scheduling rules.** Session mix by phase enforced when the week is generated (Phase A ≥60 % due retrieval / ≥25 % interleaved / ≤15 % new; B ≥50/≥35 incl. one timed section/≤15; C ≥50 % timed + debrief retrieval, ≤40 % exam-critical due, 0 % new) — the header shows the achieved mix as three numbers on hover of the phase chip. The "new" and "interleaved" slots are filled by Mark-Yield rank; cohort medians until ≥30 Attempts, then the student's own numbers (the reason line says which). Debrief-scheduled retests (10.11) and seen-solution retests (24–72 h) are pinned first. Timetable import (ICS/photo, onboarding) marks busy hours; blocks never overlap them. Weekly session length: 12–25 min weekdays, 25–45 weekends; one Readiness review block on the chosen day (default Sunday).

**Rescheduling.** Drag with `⋯ › Move` as the tap alternative; moving a block keeps its content; a moved timed section must land ≥45 min before the sleep line; `Decline` asks nothing but records `declined_reason` optionally; a missed block rolls forward once, then dissolves into the next generation (no "you missed" copy). Regeneration ("Re-plan from today") keeps accepted blocks and re-proposes the rest. `Accept week` accepts all proposed blocks; individual accept on block focus (`a`).

**States.** empty (no exam date): "Add the exam date to get a plan." + `Set date` — the week still shows due cards; loading: week grid skeleton; offline: read-only view with "as of" line, edits queued; error: inline card at the top of the week; exam week: goal hidden, only the next paper's blocks, 25-min defaults; quota: none.

**Data.** Read: `courses.papers[].date`, phase, `mastery`, `card_state` due counts, `yield_rank[]`, `assignments`, `busy_hours`, Settings `{bedtime, quiet_hours, review_day}`. Written: `plan_blocks {id, type, ref, start, minutes, state, reason, source ∈ {scheduler, debrief, assignment, student}}`, `plan_events {moved, declined, missed}`; ICS export contains accepted blocks only, no learner state.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-PLAN-001 | Scheduler output enters as `proposed`; nothing becomes `accepted` without a student action. | DB assertion after generation: 0 accepted rows without a `plan_events.accept`. |
| 10-PLAN-002 | Generated weeks satisfy the phase mix within ±5 pp of each floor/ceiling. | Property test over 200 synthetic learners × 3 phases. |
| 10-PLAN-003 | No block ends after the sleep line; in exam weeks none after 22:00; the decline copy is the single canonical sentence. | Clock fixtures; copy test. |
| 10-PLAN-004 | Every proposed block has a "Why" that names its source (yield rank, decay, debrief, assignment) with numbers. | 0 blocks with empty `reason`. |
| 10-PLAN-005 | Assignment blocks cannot be moved outside their lock window. | Drag past window → snap back + inline line. |
| 10-PLAN-006 | Missed blocks produce no notification and no loss-framed string. | Notification scheduler test; lint. |
| 10-PLAN-007 | Drag has a keyboard/tap equivalent for every operation (move, resize between 25/45, decline). | Keyboard traversal script. |
| 10-PLAN-008 | ICS export validates (RFC 5545) and contains no mastery, band or grade strings. | Validator + grep. |

Why: month-before sleep explains roughly a quarter of grade variance (SYNTHESIS §3.2, A60); a plan that schedules 23:30 blocks is a worse tutor, not a keener one.

## 10.16 Library / Reader / Source pane
**Purpose.** Library is the Course's sources by trust rank; the Reader is the page-accurate viewer; the Source pane is the same viewer docked in the right pane, opened by citation chips. Together they make "the source is one gesture away" (09-PRIN-002) true: every syllabus, scheme, ER or textbook claim resolves to a page and span.

**Stage.** MVP.

**Jobs served.** J4 (show me in the book), J2 (find the source behind the lost mark), J5 (read what school is on), J9 (upload my teacher's notes); 02-MOM-001, 02-MOM-002.

**Route.** `/library` (Course-scoped by the switcher) · `/read/:docId?page=n&span=id` · the Source pane is the ⟨Source⟩ tab of 10-CONV-002 and has no route of its own.

**Layout.** Library: sidebar Course tree · centre source table · right pane rail. Reader: full route — centre page at 66 ch text (Focus 60 ch/20 px), `--bg-subtle` surround, PDF pages paper-coloured in dark mode (09.13). Source pane: 480 px (360–640) right pane, tab strip Source · Scratchpad · Artifact · Cards, page header with page number and `[` `]` citation arrows. Tablet: pane as a bottom sheet in portrait (50/92 %). Mobile: Reader as a full route pushed from the chip.

```
Library · 9609
┌ Trust ▾  Provenance ▾  Status ▾                                [Upload PDF] [Paste a mark scheme] ┐
│ Syllabus           Cambridge 9609 syllabus 2026–28        OWN-link   96 pp  100 % conf   used 2 h ago │
│ Endorsed textbook  Hodder Business for A Level (ch. 1–8)  LICENSED   412 pp  99 %       [founder default: none] │
│ Mark scheme        s24 P2 v2 — our marking points          OWN        —      —         extracts only │
│ Examiner report    s24 P2 — paraphrased insights           OWN        —      —         extracts only │
│ Revision notes     ZNotes 9609 (treated as USER)           USER       88 pp  91 % · 3 pp to check     │
│ Your notes         "Unit 3 class notes.pdf"                USER       12 pp  84 % · 2 conflicts       │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
Reader (Source pane)
┌ Source · Scratchpad · Artifact · Cards ──────────────────────────────────── ⌘J ┐
│ Hodder ch. 3 · p. 87 · Endorsed textbook       [ ‹ 2 of 5 › ]  Aa  ⌘F  Focus  ↗ │
│ …page image / structured text with --highlight-source span…                       │
│ ┌ Conflict ─ Your notes: "273 °C" · Syllabus: "273 K" — canon shown; Keep mine ▸ ┐ │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| Source table | grouped by trust badge (09-ICON-003 order); columns: title, **provenance chip** OWN · LICENSED · USER (visible here and in teacher views only), pages, ingestion confidence (per-source mean with "n pages to check" link to the per-page list), last used. Rows for deep-link-only sources (board PDFs) show `↗` and "opens the official page". |
| Upload | **Library ingestion accepts PDF only** (§06): a PDF is the only form the two-pass parser can page-address, and page addresses are what citation chips resolve to. The **composer** accepts every type in 03-PAR-013; a non-PDF document attached there is either converted to PDF before ingestion or attached to the Turn without entering retrieval, and the composer says which. Per-file and per-message ceilings are rendered from `limits.yaml` (03-LIM-001), never restated here. Photos route through 10.8 into a PDF; Anki/Quizlet files route to Cards import (10.12). Uploads are USER: private to the user (or tenant when a teacher uploads to a class), never pooled, deletable. Status chips: `queued · parsing · audited (n % conf) · ready · failed (reason)`. |
| Files management | the Course's file count against its ceiling as a plain number ("38 of 200 files"), per-row **Delete** with a 30 s Undo and a line naming what goes with it ("3 citations in past Turns will show 'source removed'"), and at the ceiling an inline line "This Course holds 200 files. Delete one to add another." — never a modal, never a silent failed upload. |
| Paste a mark scheme | textarea → structured `mark_scheme_items` preview (§05.3 schema: marking points, levels, accept/reject) with confidence per row → `Use for my Marks` (USER scope). Verbatim official wording stays USER-scoped and is never displayed to other users. |
| Reader header | title · page · trust badge · citation arrows (prev/next citation in this Session) · reading-comfort quick control · `⌘F` find · Focus · `↗ Open original` (deep link where the source is an official PDF) |
| Page | PDF at device DPR with selectable text layer, or structured text for parsed sources; cited span in `--highlight-source`; selection popover (Ask · Explain · Define · Save as card · Quote); per-page confidence <0.9 shows the "Verify against the PDF" badge |
| Conflict card | when a USER note contradicts canon on the same syllabus point: both citations, canon shown by default, `Keep mine` records the choice on the memory object with a reason; conflicts listed in the Library row |

**Citation resolution.** A chip carries `{source_id, page, span:{start,end}, sha256, confidence, fallback}`. Resolution: local cache (last 20 pages) → page image + text layer → scroll and highlight ≤400 ms p95 (09-PRIN-002). When display rights are absent (`fallback = extract`), the pane shows the ≤40-word attributed extract, the page number and `Open the official page ↗`; extract count per Session is capped and audit-logged (§06). **Show me in the book** (MessageAction, palette verb) takes the current Turn's top citation, or — with none — runs retrieval on the Turn's syllabus point restricted to trust ranks 1–2 and opens the best page with the badge "Best match, not a citation".

**States.** Library empty: "The syllabus and our marking points are here. Add your notes or a textbook PDF." + `Upload`; loading: rows and page skeletons at final geometry; low-confidence page: badge; licence-unavailable: extract card; offline: downloaded units readable (per-unit download toggle on the source row, size shown), others show "Download when online"; error (parse failed): row chip with reason and `Retry` / `Delete`; quota: free tier ≥20 uploads/day with the plain-number meter line at 15.

**Data.** Read: `sources {trust_rank, provenance, licence_basis, source_url, sha256, pages[], page_confidence[]}`, `citations`, memory conflicts. Written: `sources` (USER uploads), `ingest_jobs`, `highlights`, `cards` (from popover), `memory.conflict_choices`, per-unit offline downloads (device-local).

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-LIBR-001 | Chip → highlighted page ≤400 ms p95 from cache, ≤1.5 s p95 cold. | Perf trace on 50 chips. |
| 10-LIBR-002 | Every citation resolves to page+span or to an attributed extract with a deep link; a chip that resolves to neither is a defect. | Fixture crawl: 0 dead chips. |
| 10-LIBR-003 | USER uploads never appear in another user's or tenant's retrieval or Library. | RLS + retrieval isolation test across two tenants. |
| 10-LIBR-004 | Canon wins conflicts by default; `Keep mine` is explicit, reasoned and reversible. | Conflict fixture: default render shows canon; choice stored with reason. |
| 10-LIBR-005 | Board PDFs are deep-linked, never rendered from Margin storage. | Storage audit: 0 objects with a board `source_url` host. |
| 10-LIBR-006 | Per-page confidence <0.9 always shows the verify badge in Reader and Source pane. | Page fixture at 0.85. |
| 10-LIBR-007 | Reader honours the full reading-comfort object (09-READ-001) and Focus = 20 px / 60 ch. | Screenshot audit after preference change. |
| 10-LIBR-008 | Paste-a-mark-scheme output validates against the §05.3 schema and is USER-scoped. | Schema test; cross-user query returns 0. |
| 10-LIBR-009 | The file count, the delete action and the at-limit line are always present; no upload fails silently and no ceiling is hard-coded in the component. | Fixture Course at 199 and at 200 files: the count renders, the 200th upload is refused with the inline line, delete-with-Undo restores; grep the component for a numeric ceiling returns 0. |

Why: hosting or displaying scheme wording without a licence is the takedown risk (§06, SYNTHESIS §9); extract + deep link keeps the trust gesture without the exposure. [founder default] No licensed textbook is assumed at launch; the Endorsed-textbook row appears only when a `LICENSED` source exists.

## 10.17 Scratchpad / Artifact
**Purpose.** Give the student a versioned place to write the thing being marked — an essay through plan → draft → Mark → rewrite, or working-out through steps → check — inside the Session, where the tutor can annotate and mark but can never alter the student's text.

**Stage.** MVP for the Scratchpad (it is where the rewrite happens, and the rewrite is bet 2). Artifact export to `.docx` and the standalone `/c/:courseId/artifact/:artifactId` route are MVP; collaborative or versioned Artifacts are not in scope at either stage.

**Jobs served.** J1, J10 (turn a Level 2 into a Level 3), J6 (draft coursework feedback without a ghost-writer), J4 (work the maths out somewhere that checks it); 02-MOM-003, 02-MOM-006.

**Route.** Right-pane tabs ⟨Scratchpad⟩ and ⟨Artifact⟩ inside a Session (10-CONV-002), plus the full route `/c/:courseId/artifact/:artifactId` for writing at full width and for sharing into a Receipt (10.23). A Mark's primary action "Rewrite the weakest paragraph" opens the Artifact tab with that paragraph loaded and the rest of the answer present but collapsed.

**Distinction.** **Scratchpad** is ephemeral thinking: one document per Session, plain text plus maths, never marked, never cited, kept for 30 days. **Artifact** is a named, versioned document that can be marked: `essay`, `working`, `plan`, `notes`. Artifacts belong to the Course and appear in Course facts and the Receipt.

**Layout.** Desktop: as a right-pane tab at 480 px (min 360, max 640) with the Session visible beside it; the full route gives a 68 ch centre with the Session collapsed to a 48 px rail on the left and the version list as a 240 px right column. Tablet: full route only. Mobile: a 92 % sheet with a segmented header (Write · Versions · Marks) and the toolbar docked above the keyboard.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Header | title (auto from the Item: "9609 P4 Q1 · Evaluate · 20 marks", editable) · type chip · word count, unlabelled and with no target ("214 words") — the marker is instructed that length is not evidence, so no surface in the product prints a words-per-mark goal (10.6 answer area, same rule) · autosave dot ("Saved 12:04") · ⋯ (Duplicate · Export .docx/.md/PDF · Delete) |
| Toolbar | one row, 32 px: **Plan** · **Draft** · headings, bold, italic, list, quote · **∑ math** (MathLive inline, `⌘⇧M`) · **Insert citation** (from the Session's chips) · **Mark this** (the only filled button) |
| Plan mode | a structured outline for levels items — Define · Chain 1 · Chain 2 · Counter · Judgement — each row a one-line field with a tariff hint ("two developed chains = AO3 L3"); Plan converts to Draft with the outline as headings the student can delete |
| Draft mode | the editor: 17/1.55, measure from reading comfort, line numbers matching the Mark's `line_ref`, paragraph anchors so a Mark's span survives editing elsewhere |
| Working pad | for numeric Courses: line-per-step entry with a `= ?` checker per line (SymPy, §11.5) that marks a line ✓ or flags "this line does not follow from the one above" — a check, never a correction |
| Margin comments | tutor annotations anchored to a paragraph, collapsed to a 12 px dot; opening shows the note, its rung and, after a Mark, the AO it belongs to; a comment can never edit the paragraph |
| Versions | a list of `v1 … vn` with timestamp, word delta, and any Mark attached ("v3 · 14 Feb · +180 words · 11–13/20"); **Compare** shows a two-column diff; **Restore** creates a new version, never overwrites |
| Mark strip | after "Mark this": the compact Mark (10.7) docked beneath the editor with spans mapped onto the live text; a re-mark shows the delta chip "+3 · AO4 L1→L2" (09-COPY-002 #14) |

```
┌ 9609 P4 Q1 · Evaluate · 20 marks ───────── 214 / about 500 · Saved 12:04 · ⋯ ─┐
│ [Plan][Draft] B I ≡ " ∑  Insert citation                        [■ Mark this] │
│──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│ L1  Market development is the lower-risk option for ZW because…            •  │
│ L7  This would help the firm grow.                                        ••  │
│ L11 The $40 000 retained profit constraint means diversification's…          │
│ L18 |                                                                        │
│──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│ v3 · 14 Feb 12:04 · +180 · 11–13/20     v2 · 13 Feb · 9–11/20   [Compare]     │
│ ┌ Mark · v3 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
│ │ AO4 Evaluation · Level 2 of 3 · 4/7 · +3 since v2                      │   │
│ │ Next mark: attach one condition from the case to your judgement (L18)  │   │
│ └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: "Write here. Plan first if it is an essay — the plan becomes your headings." with **Plan** and **Draft**; the working pad's empty state is one blank step line. `loading`: editor is interactive before versions load. `streaming`: only the Mark strip and margin comments stream; the editor's text is never touched while a stream runs, and the caret never moves. `error`: "Not saved since 12:01 — retry" inline with local content preserved in IndexedDB; a failed Mark leaves the version intact. `offline`: full editing, local versions, "Mark this" queues with the `queued` tag. `gated/quota`: "Mark this" respects the levels-Mark quota with the standard line and offers points-style checks (working pad) and a self-check rubric meanwhile. `coursework`: when the Course or the title matches an NEA/IA/EE pattern, a persistent Banner (`--banner-h`) reads "Coursework mode: I mark your draft against the criteria and log what we did. I won't write it." (09-COPY-002 #18) and every Mark writes a Receipt entry.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `⌘S` names a version explicitly (autosave every 5 s and on blur creates unnamed versions, coalesced to one per 10 minutes); `⌘.` runs the Mark's primary action on the current paragraph; `⌘⇧M` maths; `⌘⇧D` compare with previous version; `⌘⇧E` export. Selecting a paragraph and pressing `⌘⇧A` asks the tutor about it in the Session without leaving the editor. The tutor's suggestions arrive as margin comments and **Insert** buttons that the student must press; a paragraph the student did not type carries a `pasted_from_tutor` flag on the Attempt and appears in the Receipt.

**Data.** Reads: the Item, the Mark's spans and `line_ref` map, the Session's citations, reading-comfort object, Course type patterns (coursework), quota state. Writes: `artifacts {course_id, type, title, current_version}`, `artifact_versions {n, body, word_count, created_at, named?, hash}`, `attempts` (a "Mark this" creates or updates the Attempt bound to the artifact version), `annotations {paragraph_id, rung, ao?, text}`, `pasted_from_tutor` events, Receipt entries (§12.2), `scratchpads {session_id, body, expires_at}`.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-SCRA-001 | No model output ever mutates the student's text. Every suggestion is a margin comment with an explicit **Insert** control; there is no code path from a model response to an editor mutation. | Static check: the editor's mutation API is unreachable from the streaming handler; adversarial prompt ("rewrite my essay in place") produces a comment, not an edit. |
| 10-SCRA-002 | Every "Mark this" is bound to a specific `artifact_version`; the version is immutable once marked. | Edit after marking creates v+1; the marked version's hash is unchanged. |
| 10-SCRA-003 | Restore never destroys: restoring vN creates vN+1 with the same body. | Version count increases; no row deleted. |
| 10-SCRA-004 | Spans from a Mark re-anchor to paragraphs, not character offsets, so edits elsewhere do not corrupt the highlight; an unresolvable span is hidden with "This paragraph changed since the Mark". | Edit fixture: 20 spans, 0 mis-anchored. |
| 10-SCRA-005 | The working pad's line checker verifies, never corrects: it may mark a step ✓ or flag a non-sequitur, and may not print the next step while `gate_state = open`. | SymPy fixture set; adversarial run asserts no solution tokens. |
| 10-SCRA-006 | Rung 4 on a levels Item inserts at most one model **paragraph** with a blanked sentence, never a model essay (04-LADR-004). | Lint: no inserted block >220 words of continuous answer prose. |
| 10-SCRA-007 | Coursework mode fires on NEA/IA/EE Courses and titles, shows the banner, and logs every interaction to the Receipt. | Pattern fixture; Receipt contains n entries for n Turns. |
| 10-SCRA-008 | Autosave loses ≤5 s of typing on hard crash; offline editing survives a reload with no version loss. | Kill the tab mid-sentence; reload restores to the last tick. |
| 10-SCRA-009 | Export (.docx, .md, PDF) preserves line numbers, maths and the version label, and strips margin comments unless "Include feedback" is ticked. | Export fixture diff. |

## 10.18 Search / Command palette
**Purpose.** One input that reaches every verb, every syllabus point, every Session, Card, paper, source and setting across all of a student's Courses, in under 150 ms, with no animation and no mouse.

**Stage.** MVP. The palette is how every verb in 09-KEYS-003 is reachable without a mouse; a deferred palette would strand them.

**Jobs served.** J5 (get to the point school is on), J2 (find the thing I got wrong), J12; and the expert path for every other screen — the palette is the keyboard interface to §10 as a whole.

**Route.** A layer, not a route: `⌘K` from anywhere (including inside a Session while streaming, which does not stop the stream), `/` in a non-text context, and the search field in the sidebar header on mobile. `?` opens help search. `>` forces verb mode. The Paper runner restricts the verb set to five (10-RUNR-001).

**Layout.** Desktop: a 640 px panel pinned 96 px from the top on a 40 % scrim; input 36 px, rows 32 px, radius 6, hairline border, `--shadow-3`, **no entrance animation** (09-MOTN-003). Tablet: 560 px. Mobile: a full-height sheet with the input under the 44 pt top bar and the keyboard open on mount; rows 44 px.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Input | placeholder "Type a verb, a topic, or a question number"; a scope chip on the left when the palette was opened from a Course ("9609 ▾", `⌫` on an empty input clears it to All Courses) |
| Result groups | in fixed order: **Verbs** · **Syllabus points** · **Sessions & Marks** · **Cards** · **Papers & Items** · **Sources** · **Settings & Help**. Each group shows ≤4 rows with a "Show all (17)" row; group headers are 12 px caps |
| Row | icon (16) · primary label · argument slot in `--text-muted` · a 12 px context line ("3.2 Motivation · ◔ Shaky · due Thu · 9609") · keycap on the right for the first 9 rows (`⌘1…⌘9` is not used; the keycap shows `↵` on the focused row only) |
