| Verb argument step | choosing a verb with a required argument keeps the panel open and swaps the list to argument candidates ("Practise →" then a point list), with `⌫` returning to the verb |
| Recents | with an empty input: **Recent** (last 5 verbs with their arguments) then **Jump back in** (last 3 Sessions with their syllabus point) then **Due today** as a single row |
| Preview | on the focused row, a right-hand 240 px preview at ≥1280 only: for a syllabus point the pip with its reason; for a Mark the band and AO levels; for a Card the face; for a source the page thumbnail |

```
                ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                │ (9609 ▾) practise 3.2                                    │
                │──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
                │ VERBS                                                    │
                │ ▸ Practise  3.2 Motivation            8-mark Analyse   ↵ │
                │ ▸ Learn     3.2 Motivation            ◔ Shaky            │
                │ ▸ Quiz me on 3.2.1                    3 items            │
                │ SYLLABUS POINTS                                          │
                │ ▸ 3.2.1 Motivation theories  ◔  ▲4.1 marks · due Thu     │
                │ ▸ 3.2.2 Financial motivators ○  ▲2.0 marks               │
                │ SESSIONS & MARKS                                         │
                │ ▸ Mark · 9609 P4 Q1 Evaluate · 11–13/20 · 14 Feb         │
                │ PAPERS & ITEMS                                           │
                │ ▸ Open paper 9609 22 s24                Show all (17)    │
                └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Verbs.** The canonical list is 09-KEYS-003; the palette is its only complete surface. Each verb declares `{verb, arg_type ∈ {syllabus_point, paper_ref, source_ref, course, screen, free_text, none}, route, payload, scope ∈ {course, global}, guard}`. `guard` hides a verb where it cannot act (no "Challenge this Mark" without a Mark in context; no "Teacher: open class" without a teacher role; no "Start timed section" inside a running paper). Adding a screen to §10 without registering its `Go to <screen>` row is a spec defect.

**Ranking.** Score = 0.45 × prefix/fuzzy match on label and aliases + 0.25 × recency of the same verb+argument + 0.20 × context (current Course, current syllabus point, open Item) + 0.10 × scheduler priority (`yield_s`, due count). Exact syllabus numbering (`3.2.1`, `9609/22`, `Q3b`) short-circuits to that row at rank 1. Aliases are indexed: "evaluate" → the command-word help row; "essay" → Practise levels Items; "mark scheme" → the paste tool; "dark" → Toggle dark mode.

**States.** `empty` (no query): Recents, Jump back in, Due today; never a blank panel. `loading`: local index answers immediately; remote groups (Sources, Papers) append with a 12 px "searching sources…" line and never reorder rows already shown. `error`: the remote group shows "Sources unavailable — local results shown"; the palette never fails closed. `offline`: verbs that need the network are shown with a muted "needs a connection" note and are not selectable; Cards, syllabus points, downloaded sources and Settings all work. `gated`: a verb whose action is quota-limited still runs and the destination screen carries the banner — the palette never explains a quota.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `↑ ↓` move, `↵` run, `⌘↵` run in a new tab where the target is a route, `⇥` completes the highlighted verb into the input, `⌫` steps back through the argument stack then clears the scope, `Esc` closes and returns focus to the element that opened it (09-KEYS-002). Type-ahead is never destructive: the palette never runs a verb without an explicit `↵`. `⌘/` opens the shortcut sheet, which is the same layer with a different list. Search results are keyboard-only reachable; no result requires hover.

**Data.** Reads: a client-side index (verbs, the Course's syllabus tree, Session titles, Card faces, Settings rows — rebuilt on Course switch and Pack version change, ≤400 KB per Course) and a server index for full-text over Turns, Marks, sources and the item bank, scoped by tenant and by the user's Courses. Writes: `palette_used {verb, arg_type, rank_selected, latency_ms}` and the recents list (per user, synced, last 20). No search query is retained beyond 30 days, and queries are excluded from model-training exports (§11.13).

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-SRCH-001 | First results paint ≤150 ms p95 from keypress on the mid-tier Android profile, from the local index. | Perf trace over 200 keystrokes. |
| 10-SRCH-002 | Every verb of 09-KEYS-003 resolves to the correct route with the correct payload, and every §10 screen has a `Go to <screen>` row. | One unit test per verb; a registry test enumerating §10 screens. |
| 10-SRCH-003 | Zero animation: no fade, scale or slide on open, close or list change. | Reduced-motion-agnostic screenshot diff across frames = 0 bounding-box change. |
| 10-SRCH-004 | Search spans all of the user's Courses by default and can be scoped with the chip; results always name their Course. | Two-Course fixture: an unscoped query returns both; every row shows a Course tag. |
| 10-SRCH-005 | Exact syllabus, paper or question references rank first. | Fixture queries `3.2.1`, `9609/22`, `Q3b`, `s24`. |
| 10-SRCH-006 | No result set leaks another tenant's or user's content, including Session titles and USER sources. | Cross-tenant query test returns 0 rows. |
| 10-SRCH-007 | `Esc` restores focus to the opener; opening the palette during a stream neither stops nor duplicates the stream. | Scripted run with a golden stream. |
| 10-SRCH-008 | Keyboard flow 11 of 09-A11Y-014 (`⌘K` verb → route) completes; the list is a `role="listbox"` with `aria-activedescendant` and announces the group of the focused row. | Scripted run; accessibility-tree snapshot. |

## 10.19 Settings / Memory / Usage
**Purpose.** Put every control that changes how Margin reads, remembers, interrupts, charges and stores — in one scrolling page where each control states its consequence in one line and nothing about the account is hidden from its owner.

**Stage.** MVP for `account · appearance · reading · language · memory · usage · data`; `notifications` ships MVP with in-app and email channels only and gains push at v1 with the Expo builds; `shortcuts` is MVP (it renders 09-KEYS-001, it does not re-specify it).

**Jobs served.** J9 (make it readable for me), J12 (stop it pinging me at night), plus the trust obligations of §12: see what it remembers, see what it costs, take the data out, delete it.

**Route.** `/settings/:section` with the nine sections of 10-CONV-003 — `account · appearance · reading · language · memory · notifications · usage · shortcuts · data`. **Plan and billing live at the foot of `usage`** so that price and limits are read together; there is no separate billing route. `⌘,` opens the last section used; `⌘K → Reading comfort` deep-links to the control, focuses it and outlines it once for 400 ms.

**Layout.** Desktop: one continuous scroll at 68 ch with a sticky 240 px left nav listing the nine sections (current section marked by weight and a 2 px `--accent` left rule, updated by an IntersectionObserver, never by animation); no tabs, no accordions, no modal dialogs anywhere in Settings. Tablet: the nav becomes a sticky horizontal strip. Mobile: the same single scroll with a sticky section chip row; destructive flows (delete account, delete a Course) are full-screen routes, not sheets.

**Regions and elements.**

| Section | Controls (each with a one-line consequence beneath it) |
|---|---|
| **Account** | Signed-in identity (email or provider) · **Change email** (magic-link re-verification, the old address is notified) · linked Apple / Google · **Sign out** · **Sign out everywhere** · active devices with last-seen and per-device **Revoke** · for under-16 accounts, the guardian link with its verification state and a "ask for a change" path. First section in the scroll, because an account the owner cannot leave is not their account. |
| **Appearance** | Theme (System · Light · Dark) · Contrast+ toggle · Reduce motion (System · On) · Reduce transparency. There is no density control: the product has one row height and one measure by design, and a second density doubles the visual-regression matrix for no named student job. |
| **Reading** | Font family radio with a live specimen rendered in each face: Inter · **Atkinson Hyperlegible** · **OpenDyslexic** · Serif · System · Size stepper 14–24 px · Leading (1.4 · 1.55 · 1.7) · Tracking (0 · +0.05 · +0.12 em) · Measure (narrow 56 ch · medium 68 · wide 75) · Warm background · **two one-tap presets** (Comfort · Dyslexia, exactly as defined in 09-READ-002) · Read-aloud rate 0.8–1.5× with a "Hear a sample" button |
| **Language** | UI language (English · Русский · Қазақша) · **Explanation language** (independent of UI — the tutor may explain in Russian while the exam terms stay English) · Decimal comma · Date format · "Keep exam vocabulary in English" toggle, on by default |
| **Memory** | A list, not a paragraph: each memory row shows its text (≤200 chars), `source: you | inferred`, its date, and — for inferred rows — a "why" link to the Attempts behind it; per-row **Edit · Pin · Forget** (Forget removes with a 30 s Undo and writes a tombstone so inference never re-creates it). Above the list: **Pause memory** (this Course · all Courses), **Incognito Session** (`⌘⇧N` variant: nothing written to the Learner Graph, memory, Attempts or Cards, and the Session is excluded from the Receipt; the persistent banner tells the truth — `incognito.banner` = "Incognito — nothing here reaches your Learner Graph. Kept 30 days for safety review, then deleted." — and a "use my memory" tick, **default off**, is the only way an incognito Turn reads Course memory. Ending one offers `incognito.save_offer` = "Save this Session to your Course? Attempts will be recorded from now, not retroactively."), **Sensitive exclusions** (topics never stored). Below: "What Margin never stores: health, family, mood, anything the sensitive classifier flags (07-GRPH-002)." |
| **Notifications** | Channels (push · email · none) per event type: **Due cards** · **Plan block starting** · **Mark ready** · **Weekly card** · **Teacher assignment**. Defaults: teen accounts all off; adults due-cards on. **Quiet Hours** start/end with a timezone line (default 22:00–07:00, on for under-18) and "Nothing sends during Quiet Hours, including from your teacher." **Never sent:** predicted grades, streak reminders, guilt or urgency copy (§03.4). |
| **Usage** | Plain-number meter: session bar (5 h rolling) and weekly bar, then counters, every number rendered from `limits.yaml` for the viewer's plan and none written into this document — "Deep Turns {used} of {cap} today · resets 00:00 Asia/Almaty", "Levels Marks {used} of {cap} today", "Uploads {used} of {cap}", "Voice — not on your plan". Grace state shows the plan-conditional exam-week line (09-COPY-002 #10) and names the number, never "off". A **What each limit means** table rendered verbatim from `limits.yaml` (03-LIM-001). Then **Plan**: current plan, price, renewal date, "Until exams · expires 5 July 2027", change/cancel (cancel is two taps and never routed through a retention interstitial), invoices, regional-price note. |
| **Shortcuts** | The full 09-KEYS-001 map, searchable, each row rebindable with conflict detection; **Reset to defaults**; a "Print this sheet" link. |
| **Data** | **Export everything** (JSON + PDF Receipts + Cards `.apkg` + source list; emailed link, ≤24 h) · **Export this Course** · **Delete a Course** (removes Attempts, Marks, Cards; keeps nothing) · **Delete account** (30-day grace, then hard delete; states what is retained for legal reasons and for how long) · **Who can see what** (a table: you, your teacher of record, your parent/guardian digest, Margin staff under audit — linking to §07.9) · **Training** ("Your work is not used to train models" as a statement of fact, with the audit-log link) |

```
┌ SETTINGS ─────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Appearance            │ Reading                                              │
│▌Reading               │ Aa Inter    Aa Atkinson    Aa OpenDyslexic   Aa Serif│
│ Language              │ Text size  [ − ]  18 px  [ + ]                       │
│ Memory                │  Chat and reading pages scale; buttons stay 14 px.   │
│ Notifications         │ Line spacing (1.4 | ●1.7 | 1.55)                     │
│ Usage                 │ Line width  (narrow | ●medium | wide)                │
│ Shortcuts             │ [ Use the reading-comfort preset ]                   │
│ Data                  │──────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│                       │ Memory · 6 things                    Pause memory ▸  │
│                       │ "Target A in 9609"            you    Edit Pin Forget │
│                       │ "Confuses cash flow with profit — improving"         │
│                       │                          inferred · why · Forget     │
│                       │──────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│                       │ Usage · Deep Turns 12 of 60 today · resets 00:00     │
│                       │ ▇▇▇▇▇▇▇▇░░░░░░░░  Levels Marks 2 of 3                │
└───────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: Memory with no rows reads "Margin hasn't stored anything about your learning yet. It will, and you'll see it here." `loading`: controls render from local preferences immediately; usage counters show their last value with a 12 px "updating" line. `error`: a failed save reverts the control and shows an inline line "Not saved — retry"; never a toast for a setting. `offline`: appearance, reading, language, shortcuts and notification preferences all apply locally and sync later; memory edits, exports and plan changes queue and say so. `gated`: teen accounts show notification and memory rows their guardian controls with "Set by your parent or guardian" and a link to ask for a change; school tenants show tenant-locked rows the same way. `deleting`: the account-delete route shows what will go, what is kept and for how long, requires typing the word `delete`, and offers **Export first** as the adjacent action.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Every change applies immediately — there is no Save button anywhere in Settings, and no change is destructive without Undo except the two delete routes. Reading changes apply without reload and without layout shift outside the text (09-READ-004). `⌘,` opens Settings; `⌘F` searches the page (native find works because Settings is one document); `⌘K → Memory: forget <fact>` reaches a row directly. Rebinding a shortcut listens for one chord, shows conflicts inline, and refuses only chords the browser owns.

**Data.** Reads: `prefs` (reading comfort, language, decimal comma, intent defaults), `memories[]` with provenance, notification config, Quiet Hours, `limits.yaml` and server counters, subscription state, tenant and guardian policy object. Writes: `prefs.*`, `memories` (edit/forget/pin with tombstones), `memory_paused`, `sensitive_exclusions`, `notification_prefs`, `quiet_hours`, `shortcut_overrides`, `export_jobs`, `deletion_requests`, `subscription_changes`. Every write to memory or deletion is audit-logged with actor and timestamp (§11.13).

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-SETT-001 | Settings is one scrolling document with a sticky nav; no modal dialog, no accordion, no tab hides a control. | DOM audit: 0 `role="dialog"` outside the two delete routes; every control reachable by scrolling. |
| 10-SETT-002 | Every control has a one-line consequence string from the catalogue; a control without one fails the copy lint. | Catalogue coverage test: 100 % of Settings controls have a `consequence` key. |
| 10-SETT-003 | Memory is a list of visible, editable, forgettable rows; a forgotten row never returns, including from inference. | Forget then run the inference job on the same Attempts: 0 re-creations (tombstone test). |
| 10-SETT-004 | The usage meter matches server counters within one Turn and is rendered verbatim from `limits.yaml`; a mismatch fails CI. | 03-LIM-001 diff job extended to this screen. |
| 10-SETT-005 | Cancellation is reachable in two taps from `usage` with no retention interstitial, no dark pattern and no "are you sure you want to lose your progress". | Click-path test; copy lint on the flow. |
| 10-SETT-006 | Export produces every object the user owns (Attempts, Marks with spans, Turns, Cards with FSRS state, artifacts, Receipts, sources list, memories) in machine-readable JSON plus PDFs, within 24 h. | Export a 6-month account; schema-validate; row counts match the database. |
| 10-SETT-007 | Quiet Hours suppresses every channel including teacher-triggered notifications, across DST boundaries. | Unit test with `Asia/Almaty` and `Europe/London`; 0 deliveries inside the window. |
| 10-SETT-008 | Font, size, leading, tracking and measure changes apply live to chat, Mark view, Reader, Cards and print, with no layout shift outside the text column. | Screenshot audit on five surfaces; CLS outside the text column = 0. |
| 10-SETT-009 | Teen and tenant-locked rows state who set them and offer a request path; they are never silently disabled. | Under-16 fixture and school-tenant fixture. |
| 10-SETT-010 | Keyboard flow 10 of 09-A11Y-014 (change a reading setting) completes; every radiogroup announces its options and current value. | Scripted run; accessibility-tree snapshot. |

## 10.20 Teacher console (calibrator scope)
**Purpose.** Give the teacher the two powers that make Margin trustworthy and that only a teacher has — setting the conditions of an assignment, and overruling a Mark — while feeding every override back into calibration. At MVP the console is **calibrator scope**: class, assignments, Mark audit, override, calibration queue, disagreement trends. Everything else is labelled v1.

**Stage.** MVP at calibrator scope only (SYNTHESIS §6 row 20): classes by code, locked assignments with an allowed-help level, aggregate attempts and hint depth, Mark audit with override, mock import, calibration tasks. Heatmap, SSO/roster sync and evidence export are v1 and are marked in the regions table below.

**Jobs served.** J9 (teacher: is this thing marking my class the way I would), plus the human-oversight posture of SYNTHESIS §8.11: nothing reaches a school-recorded grade without teacher confirmation. Teacher mode is free forever (SYNTHESIS §12).

**Route.** `/t/:classId` with tabs `roster · assignments · marks · calibration · settings`. `/t` lists classes. Students join at `/join/:code` (10.2). A teacher's own student Courses live at `/c/...` as normal; the console never mixes the two.

**Layout.** Desktop: `280px | 1fr` — left = class list and the tab nav, centre = the working table at up to 1,100 px; no right pane except in the Mark audit, where the Mark view (10.7) opens in a 480 px pane at its container-query tablet variant (10-CONV-004). Tablet: same with the class list as an overlay. Mobile: read-only summaries plus the full override flow — a teacher must be able to overrule a Mark from a phone; assignment creation is desktop/tablet only and says so.

**Regions and elements.**

| Tab | Elements |
|---|---|
| **Roster** | Class code (8 characters, large tabular, **Copy** and **Regenerate**) · join link and printable QR · student rows: name, joined date, Course, last active (day granularity), Attempts this week, **aggregate** ladder depth ("mean rung 2.4"), open challenges. Per-student detail shows Attempts, Marks and assignment work only — never Session transcripts, never per-Turn rungs (§07.9). Remove student (with a confirmation naming what the student keeps). |
| **Assignments** | Create: pick Items (past-paper parts, Margin originals, "5 like this"), set **open** and **due** timestamps, a **lock window** (during it the Paper-runner no-help rule applies: no tutor Turns, no ladder, no citations, no Library — 10-RUNR-001), and an **allowed-help level** for outside the window (`none · rungs 1–3 · full ladder`). Assignment rows show submitted/marked/overdue counts, mean band per AO, and the three most common `reason_code`s across the class. |
| **Marks (audit)** | A queue sorted by `show_teacher_flag`, low AO confidence, `NOT_IN_INDICATIVE_VALID_ALT`, challenge outcomes and random sample. Each row opens the Mark view with the student's answer, spans, scheme paraphrase and the machine rationale. **Override**: two clicks — click the AO level or the total, choose a **reason code**, done; a free-text note is optional. Reason codes: `application_credited · valid_alternative · level_too_generous · level_too_harsh · command_word_met · working_credited · illegible_misread · out_of_scope · other`. Both marks are kept and shown to the student (10.7 override strip). |
| **Calibration** | The teacher-as-calibrator queue: "Mark these 10 scripts blind" — the machine Mark is hidden until the teacher submits; agreement is then shown per AO with the disagreement highlighted. Double-marking credit is recorded; a second teacher's confirmation promotes the item to Tier 2 gold (SYNTHESIS §8.5). Header line: "Your marks train the marker for 9609 P2. 312 scripts so far · agreement 0.82." |
| **Trends** | Disagreement trends per paper per quarter: override rate, direction (generous/harsh), the AO most often overridden, challenge outcomes, and the per-paper calibration state with its gate numbers. One chart, five tones, no red. |
| **Settings** | Class-locked Course fields (syllabus, papers, series — locked fields render as "Set by your teacher" for students, 10.2), school-safe mode (§12.7), mock import (batch photos → 10.8 → Marks), and the teacher's data-visibility statement. |

```
┌ 9609 · Year 13 Set B · code  K7M-Q42P  [Copy] ─────────────────────────────────┐
│ ⟨Roster⟩ ⟨Assignments⟩ ⟨Marks*⟩ ⟨Calibration⟩ ⟨Settings⟩                        │
│ Mark audit · 7 flagged · 2 challenged · 5 sampled                              │
│ Student      Item              Machine   Flag                     Action       │
│ A. Rakhimov  P4 Q1 Evaluate    11–13/20  valid point outside IC   [Open ▸]     │
│ S. Toleu     P2 Q3(b) Analyse   5/8      AO3 confidence: check    [Open ▸]     │
│ L. Ahmed     P2 Q1(a) Define    2/2      challenge outcome: upheld [Open ▸]     │
│──────────────────────────── open ────────────────────────────────────────────  │
│ AO3 Analysis  Level 2 of 3 · 5/8      ◂ set level: (1)(●2)(3)                  │
│ Reason: (application credited)(valid alternative)(too generous)(too harsh)…    │
│ Both marks are kept and shown to the student.               [■ Save override]  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**v1-only (build the routes, hide behind a flag, do not ship at MVP).** Class heatmap by syllabus point; SSO and roster sync (Google Classroom, Wonde, Clever); evidence export for moderation and appeals; per-student intervention suggestions; parent co-visibility; LMS grade passback; multi-class analytics; department-level dashboards. The console must render a one-line "In v1" row where each of these will live, rather than a disabled control.

**States.** `empty`: a new class shows the code at full size with "Give this code to your class. Nothing appears here until a student joins." `loading`: table skeletons at final row height; the audit queue paints its count from cache. `error`: a failed override is retried inline and never lost ("Not saved — retry"); an unavailable Mark shows the student's answer and the scheme so the teacher can still mark blind. `offline`: read-only queue from cache; overrides queue with a `queued` tag and apply in order on reconnect. `gated`: a paper below the calibration gate shows the evidence-only Mark to the teacher too, with the gate numbers and an explicit "no number is shown to your students for this paper".

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `j/k` move the queue, `↵` opens, `Esc` returns to the queue with position preserved; `1…5` select a level on the focused AO, then `r` opens the reason chips, `↵` saves — this is the two-click path on a keyboard. Bulk actions: select rows with `x`, then "Mark as reviewed". Copying the class code is one click and shows the printable QR. Nothing in the console can edit a student's answer, delete an Attempt, or read a Session transcript.

**Data.** Reads: `classes {code, course, locked_fields, teacher_ids}`, roster, `assignments {items[], open_at, due_at, lock_window, allowed_help}`, Attempts and Marks for class members (assignment and marked work only), calibration state per paper, challenge outcomes, aggregate ladder depth. Writes: `class_codes` (create/regenerate), `assignments`, `overrides {mark_id, teacher_id, from, to, per_ao[], reason_code, note?, at}` → a new Mark row with `supersedes` (05-MARK-012) and a Tier-2 gold candidate, `calibration_marks {teacher_id, blind: true, marks}`, `reviewed_at` on audit rows, telemetry `override_created`, `calibration_submitted`.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-TCHR-001 | Override is ≤2 clicks from an open Mark (level/total, then reason code) and stores teacher id, both marks, per-AO deltas and the reason code. | Click-count test; schema check on 100 overrides. |
| 10-TCHR-002 | The teacher's mark supersedes and is shown to the student with the machine mark still visible; neither is deleted. | Student-side fixture renders both (10-MARK-007). |
| 10-TCHR-003 | The console never exposes Session transcripts, per-Turn rungs or memory rows; ladder depth is class- or student-aggregate only. | API contract test: every teacher endpoint's response schema lacks `turns`, `rung`, `memories`. |
| 10-TCHR-004 | Calibration marking is blind: the machine Mark is not present in the DOM or the response payload until the teacher submits. | Network capture on the calibration route. |
| 10-TCHR-005 | A lock window enforces the no-help rule on the student's side for exactly its duration, in the student's timezone, and the student sees the condition before starting. | Two-timezone fixture; student DOM audit during the window. |
| 10-TCHR-006 | Nothing produced here writes a school-recorded grade without an explicit teacher confirmation step. | No export or API path emits a grade field without `confirmed_by`. |
| 10-TCHR-007 | Disagreement trends are computed per paper per quarter and match the gate dashboard's numbers for the same period. | Cross-check job; discrepancy = 0. |
| 10-TCHR-008 | Every v1 capability is represented by an "In v1" line, never by a disabled control or an empty tab. | Screenshot audit of all five tabs. |
| 10-TCHR-009 | The override flow completes on a 390 px phone in portrait. | Scripted mobile run. |


## 10.21 Parent digest
**Purpose.** Give a consenting parent enough evidence to help — and nothing that turns them into a second examiner. Effort and coverage from Attempts; no transcripts, no ranking.

**Stage.** MVP at digest v0: the fortnightly email and its web mirror, with the fixed sections below. Configurable cadence, per-Course digests and the guardian dashboard are v1.

**Jobs served.** J10 (a parent who paid wants to know it is working); J11 (helping without nagging). Serves no learner job directly, so it must cost the learner nothing.

**Route.** `/p/:digestId` (web view of the sent email, auth-gated to the linked `guardian_links.parent_user_id`), `/p` (index of past digests + controls). Email is the primary surface; the web view is the same content plus controls. Cadence default **fortnightly**, Sunday 18:00 local (SYNTHESIS §6 screen 21); the student may set weekly, fortnightly, monthly or paused-for-one-cycle in Settings › Memory › Sharing. Digest ids are opaque ULIDs; links expire after 60 days.

**Layout.** Email: one 600 px column, no images beyond the wordmark, light palette forced (09-DARK-007), tabular numbers. Web: same column at max 640 px plus a controls block. No charts in email; the web view may show the Activity heatmap (10.14) and nothing else from Progress.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Content and rules |
|---|---|
| Header | "Aisha · 9609 Business · 14 days to 28 September" — display name, Course, window. No greeting line, no "Hi there!". |
| Effort (default-shared) | sessions vs weekly goal, minutes, longest gap, Cards reviewed, Attempts marked. Rest days neutral, never misses. |
| Coverage (default-shared) | syllabus point **titles** with official numbering, count of topics that moved, and the sentence "Topics move when two unaided attempts are correct on different days." No pips, no percentages, no per-topic state. |
| Evidence (default-shared) | Marks taken and the unaided delta with n. **No band, no grade, no per-Mark score.** |
| One suggestion | exactly one, addressed to the parent, actionable and non-nagging: "The only free 45 minutes this fortnight is Thursday evening. That block is a timed Paper 2 section — a quiet room helps more than a reminder." Generated from the Plan and busy hours, never from affect signals. |
| Consent-gated block | rendered only where `guardian_links.scope[key] = true` **and** the student's own toggle is on: `predicted_grade` (default **off**, 05-PRED-004), `mark_bands`, `wrong_questions`, `plan_detail`. Each renders with the same labels the student sees, including "practice estimate, not a board mark". |
| Controls (web only) | Study Hours, Quiet Hours (teen default 22:00–07:00), payment, cadence, "Turn the digest off". Every change writes an audit row and shows the student an in-app banner — "Your parent set Quiet Hours 22:00–07:00" (AADC transparency, 11-PRIV-001). |
| Footer | non-affiliation line (09-PRNT-006) · "This digest is built from practice attempts. It contains no conversations." · unsubscribe · "See exactly what was shared" → `/p/:digestId/shared`, visible to the student too. |

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Margin                                                    │
│ Aisha · 9609 Business · 14 days to 28 September           │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ EFFORT      7 of 8 sessions · 3 h 20 m · longest gap 3 d   │
│             62 cards reviewed · 9 answers marked           │
│ COVERAGE    3.2 Motivation · 1.4 Enterprise · 5.1 Costs    │
│             2 topics moved. Topics move when two unaided   │
│             attempts are correct on different days.        │
│ EVIDENCE    Unaided delta, Paper 2 questions: +2.1 / 12    │
│             over 4 weeks (n = 9)                           │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ ONE THING   The only free 45 minutes this fortnight is     │
│             Thursday evening — a timed Paper 2 section.    │
│             A quiet room helps more than a reminder.       │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Study Hours · Quiet Hours · Plan & payment · Cadence       │
│ This digest is built from practice attempts. It contains   │
│ no conversations.   See exactly what was shared →          │
│ Margin is not affiliated with … Privacy · Unsubscribe      │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty` (no activity): send anyway — "No sessions this fortnight." plus the suggestion, never a chasing line; silence reads as an outage. `partial`: window label says the Course started mid-window. `paused` (16+ students only): "This cycle's digest is paused by Aisha. The next one is 12 October." `off`: no email; the web index names the state and who set it. `error`: two retries maximum, logged and shown in `/p`. `offline · streaming · gated/quota`: n/a — a batch job whose only model call is the suggestion, which falls back to a Plan-derived template.

**Interactions.** Links only in the email. "See exactly what was shared" renders the stored payload as a labelled list, the same page for parent and student. Under-16 students cannot switch the digest off (it is a consent condition) but can pause a cycle, can turn every consent-gated block off, and always see the payload; 16+ students can switch it off and the parent is told who did.

**Data.** Read: `attempts`, `marks` (counts and unaided deltas only), `plan_blocks`, `courses`, `guardian_links.scope`, Settings sharing toggles. Written: `digest_sends {id, parent_user_id, student_user_id, window, payload_hash, sent_at, opened_at}` and an `audit_log` row per control change. The payload is materialised and stored so `/p/:digestId/shared` shows what was actually sent, not a recomputation.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-PDIG-001 | The payload is built from an allow-list of aggregate fields; any field not on the list cannot reach the renderer. | Schema test: the builder rejects an injected `turn_text` field; the allow-list is a single constant with a snapshot test. |
| 10-PDIG-002 | No Turn text, answer text, memory content, affect tag, distress or crisis event, moderation flag, or free-text model prose about the student appears in any digest, ever — including inside the one suggestion. | Corpus scan of 1,000 generated digests: 0 hits on the transcript/affect field names; the suggestion generator receives Plan and calendar data only (prompt input allow-list test). |
| 10-PDIG-003 | No ranking, cohort comparison, percentile, class position or "average student" string. | Copy lint on the digest catalogue; generated-string classifier over the 1,000-digest corpus, 0 hits. |
| 10-PDIG-004 | Predicted grade appears only with `guardian_links.scope.predicted_grade=true` **and** the student toggle on, and then with series, spread and the "practice estimate" label. | Fixture matrix of 4 combinations; the string appears in exactly 1 (extends 10-PROG-001). |
| 10-PDIG-005 | The student can see every digest ever sent, byte-identical to the parent's copy, from Settings › Memory › Sharing. | `/p/:digestId/shared` returns the stored payload; hash equals `digest_sends.payload_hash`. |
| 10-PDIG-006 | Guilt, urgency and loss lexicons (09-COPY-003 d, e) are banned in digest copy, including the suggestion. | Copy lint + generated-corpus lint = 0 violations. |
| 10-PDIG-007 | Digest sends respect Quiet Hours of the *parent's* timezone and never send between 22:00 and 07:00; a bounced or unopened digest never triggers a second "reminder" email. | Scheduler test across three timezones; 0 rows of type `digest_reminder` in the notification table. |
| 10-PDIG-008 | Turning the digest off (16+) or pausing a cycle takes effect before the next scheduled send with no confirmation dialogue for the parent to override. | Toggle at T−1 min before the job; 0 sends. |

Why: parents who receive transcripts stop being parents and start being markers, and students who know transcripts are shared stop telling the tutor what they do not understand — which destroys the diagnosis the whole product depends on (student-voice §4; 03-PAR-027).

## 10.22 Challenge-a-mark
**Purpose.** Make disagreement with the Examiner Twin a one-tap, examiner-voice procedure rather than a support ticket — and turn every disagreement into calibration data (§05.7). The screen is the visible half of 05-CHAL-001…006; nothing here re-specifies the marking logic.

**Stage.** MVP. A product that publishes an agreement number must let the student contest a Mark from day one; stage-2 human review is MVP at the calibrator's desk (10.20), not a separate v1 queue.

**Jobs served.** J1 (trust the Mark), J7 (teacher of record confirms anything that matters), 02-MOM-003 (mock season, when a wrong band costs a week of misdirected work).

**Route.** `/c/:courseId/marks/:markId/challenge`. Entry: the `Challenge` tertiary action in the Mark view (10.7) header overflow and the same action in the mock debrief per question part. Deep-linkable; the teacher's copy of the same object is `/t/:classId/challenges/:challengeId`.

**Layout.** Desktop: the Mark view stays mounted in the left column (student answer with spans, dropped to 0.6 opacity while Stage 1 runs) and the right pane switches to the **Compare** tab at `--panel-w`, showing original vs re-mark per AO. Tablet: Compare replaces the centre column with a back arrow. Mobile: a full route; the two rationales stack (original first, re-mark second) with a sticky per-AO delta bar at the top.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| Trigger sheet | one line "A different marker will look at this. Your original Mark is kept." · optional one-line reason (200 chars, placeholder "Optional — which part, and why") · optional AO multi-select chips · one filled button `Send for a second look`. No mention of "appeal", no promise of a higher mark. |
| Status strip | stage chip (`Stage 1 · second look` / `Stage 2 · teacher` / `Stage 2 · examiner`), elapsed timer for Stage 1 (target <60 s), SLA line for Stage 2 ("An examiner is checking AO4 — up to 72 hours"), and the family provenance chip for each marker (09-COPY-003 j: chip only, never a headline). |
| Compare table | one row per AO: original level and marks · re-mark level and marks · delta · the deciding span quoted from the student's answer with its line ref. Changed rows carry the delta in tabular figures; unchanged rows read "unchanged". |
| Rationale pair | the two rationales side by side (stacked on mobile), each ≤60 words, examiner-voice per 05-CHAL-004, each citing its descriptor or marking point with a citation chip that opens the Source pane. |
| Outcome card | `upheld` · `revised` · `split`, with the canonical sentence and the resulting band. `revised` shows the new BandTotal with "supersedes" and a link to the superseded Mark (05-MARK-012). |
| Escalation | `Still disagree` (always available once, per stage) → teacher of record if a Class exists, else the contracted-examiner queue. Below it, one factual line: "Challenges are logged and feed the calibration page for this paper." |
| Teacher/examiner result | the human Mark, the reason code, both machine Marks collapsed behind "Show what the markers said", and — where the Class records grades — the confirmation checkbox that is the Annex III human-oversight gate (§08.11, 11-PRIV-005 §6). |

```
┌ Mark · 9609/22 s24 Q3(b) Evaluate · 12 marks ──────── Stage 1 · 00:41 ─┐
│ …student answer, spans dimmed while the second look runs…              │
├───────────────────────────── Compare ──────────────────────────────────┤
│ AO   original        second look     delta                              │
│ AO1  L2  2/2         L2  2/2         unchanged                          │
│ AO2  L2  3/4         L2  3/4         unchanged                          │
│ AO3  L2  5/8 …       L2  5/8         unchanged                          │
│ AO4  L1  1/4         L2  3/4         +2      ← deciding span, line 18   │
│ "…but the finance constraint outweighs product dependence here…"        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Original: "No weighing of one factor against another — L1."¹            │
│ Second look: "Line 18 weighs finance against product dependence — L2."² │
│ Outcome: revised. Band 33–37 → 35–39.  [Still disagree]  [Back to Mark] │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: n/a (the screen only exists for a Mark). `loading/streaming`: the Compare table skeletons at final geometry and fills per AO as the re-mark streams (09-STRM-011); the deciding span highlights last. `error`: "The second marker is unavailable. Your challenge is queued — we'll notify you." with the challenge persisted (`stage1_queued`); never a lost challenge. `offline`: `Send for a second look` queues in the outbox (11-OFF-002) and the strip reads "Queued — sends when you're back". `gated/quota`: challenges are **never** metered and never rate-limited per Mark (05-CHAL-001); a challenge does not consume a levels-Mark quota unit. `exam week`: Stage 2 SLA copy becomes "after your paper" when the component is within 72 h (05-CHAL-006) and no predicted-grade recomputation runs. `below gate`: the Compare table shows levels and spans with no number, matching the parent Mark.

**State machine.** `none → submitted → stage1_running → {upheld | revised | split} → (still_disagree) → stage2_queued → stage2_human → closed`. One challenge per Mark per stage; `revised` creates a new Mark with `supersedes`; `closed` is terminal and the Mark's `challenge_state` records the last outcome. A teacher override taken independently (§05, 10.20) closes an open challenge with outcome `teacher_override` and notifies the student in the same neutral voice.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `⌘⇧C` from an open Mark opens the trigger sheet; Enter sends; `[` `]` walk AO rows; `d` toggles "show only changed AOs"; Escape returns to the Mark with state preserved. Notification on Stage 2 completion respects Quiet Hours and carries no verdict in the preview text ("Your Paper 2 challenge has a result").

**Data.** Read: `marks` (original and second-marker rows), `mark_scheme_items`, `attempts`, Class membership. Written: `challenges {id, mark_id, stage, families[], reason_text?, aos[], outcome, delta_per_ao, human_id?, created_at, closed_at}` per 05-CHAL-005; a new `marks` row on `revised`; a Tier 2 gold candidate on human confirmation (§08.11).

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-CHLG-001 | Challenge is reachable in one tap from any Mark, including a below-gate Mark and an anonymous landing Mark (which prompts Save first). | Route crawl: the action exists on 100% of Mark fixtures. |
| 10-CHLG-002 | Stage 1 renders a complete Compare table with both rationales in ≤60 s p95; the re-marker family differs from both families used on the original. | Soak on 200 fixtures; assert `families ∩ original_families = ∅`. |
| 10-CHLG-003 | The copy contains no apology, no praise, no "sorry", no "you were right", and no promise about the outcome before it exists. | Copy lint over the challenge catalogue + generated rationale corpus. |
| 10-CHLG-004 | Every outcome writes a `challenges` row with `delta_per_ao`; the per-paper challenge rate and `overturn_rate` on the calibration page (05-GATE-008) are computed from this table and no other. | Reconciliation job: dashboard numbers equal a direct SQL count. |
| 10-CHLG-005 | Product-side targets are instrumented and alarmed, in one direction and one vocabulary: `challenge_rate = challenges ÷ marks` <3%, and `overturn_rate = revised ÷ challenges` <30%, per paper per month. "Upheld" is an outcome of a single challenge (the state machine above), never a metric — a rate named `upheld_rate` anywhere in code, dashboards or copy fails the metrics lint, because the same number read as "upheld" and as "overturned" alarms in opposite directions (08-ENS-007, 05-CHAL-005). | Dashboard with alarm; a paper breaching either pages the content lead (11-RISK-001 #1). |
| 10-CHLG-006 | A challenge never changes a school-recorded grade without a teacher confirmation action. | Fixture: `revised` outcome in a Class with `grade_sync=true` leaves the recorded grade unchanged until the checkbox is set; audit row present. |
| 10-CHLG-007 | Challenging is free of quota and of any friction copy that discourages it (no "are you sure", no counter of how many you have raised). | Meter fixture; DOM scan for confirmation dialogues = 0. |

Why: a marker students cannot argue with is a marker they stop believing; the challenge flow is also the cheapest source of Tier 2 gold the product has (SYNTHESIS §5 bet 1, §8.6).

## 10.23 Learning Receipt / Share / Export
**Purpose.** Let a student prove *how* a piece of work was done — attempts, help rungs, drafts, Marks, timestamps — to a teacher who suspects AI, and let anyone share a Mark or a result without leaking learner state. Contents and cryptography are specified in §12.2; this screen is how they are read, shared and exported.

**Stage.** Split. **MVP:** the Receipt cover, timeline and exports (`.pdf`, `.csv`, `.json`) and Mark sharing — `/m/:markToken` already exists at MVP (10.1) and the WhatsApp link is the acquisition loop. **v1:** public revocable Receipt and Artifact tokens at `/r/:token`, and the server-rendered share cards at `/share/mark/:markId`. Build the MVP half first; the v1 half adds a token table and an image renderer and changes no data on this screen. This supersedes any reading of 03-PAR-025 that defers all sharing to v1: the row means Receipts and Artifacts.

**Jobs served.** J9 (be believed), J7 (hand a teacher evidence), J12 (leave with your data). 02-MOM-002 (coursework season), 02-MOM-004 (results day).

**Route.** `/receipt/:receiptId` (owner view, auth-gated) · `/r/:token` (public, learner state stripped, revocable) · `/verify/:receiptId` (signature verification, no auth, §12.2) · exports from Settings › Data. Share cards are generated at `/share/mark/:markId` and `/share/receipt/:receiptId` as server-rendered images.

**Layout.** Desktop: a single 720 px reading column — cover block, timeline table, drafts appendix — with a 280 px sticky right rail carrying `Export PDF`, `Copy public link`, `Show teacher`, `Revoke link` and the verification QR. Mobile: one column; the rail becomes a bottom action bar of two buttons (`Export PDF`, `Share`) plus an overflow.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| Cover | student display name, Course, assignment or Session title, date range, Pack version, policy version, the first 16 hex of the SHA-256 content hash, the Ed25519 key id, and a QR to `/verify/:receiptId`. |
| Timeline | one row per event: timestamp (learner timezone, ISO in the export) · event (`attempt · hint · rung · draft · mark · seen_solution · challenge · teacher_override`) · rung reached · Mark band where the event is a Mark · a **Seen solution** chip where `seen_solution=true`. Rows are never editable and never deleted; a redaction is a row, not a gap. |
| Drafts appendix | each draft as stored, with diff bars against the previous (added = solid left rule, removed = struck) per 09-PRNT-003. The tutor's words are never presented as the student's. |
| Integrity summary | three counts in one line: attempts before first help, help rungs used (median and max), seen-solution events — the numbers a teacher actually reads. |
| Share cards | two templates, 1200×630 PNG server-rendered: **Mark card** (question ref, tariff, command word, band *or* "practice estimate — not yet calibrated", one credited span quoted ≤25 words, calibration badge, non-affiliation line) and **Receipt card** (assignment title, the three integrity counts, hash prefix, verification URL). Both carry the wordmark and no learner identity beyond the display name the student typed. |
| Exports | Receipt PDF (09-PRNT-003) · Receipt JSON (§12.2 schema, signature detached) · Mark PDF · Cards `.apkg` · Plan `.ics` · full account ZIP (11-PRIV-004). Each export names, above the button, exactly what it contains in one line. |
| Notices | the non-affiliation line (09-PRNT-006) on every rendered page, print page and share card; on the public variant additionally "Public copy — learner state removed." |

```
┌ Learning Receipt ─────────────────────────────  [Export PDF] [Copy link] ┐
│ Aisha K. · 9609 Business · "Unit 3 essay draft" · 2–9 Sep 2026            │
│ Pack 9609 2026–28 v14 · policy v31 · sha256 4f9c2ab77e10d3c8 · key mk-2   │
│                                                    ▣ verify → /verify/…   │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 02 Sep 18:04  attempt   own plan, 180 words          rung —               │
│ 02 Sep 18:19  hint      conceptual pointer           rung 2               │
│ 03 Sep 17:40  draft     v1 (620 words)               —                    │
│ 04 Sep 17:12  mark      AO2 L2 · AO4 L1 · 8–11       —                    │
│ 05 Sep 16:55  attempt   rewrite of ¶3                rung —               │
│ 06 Sep 19:02  seen_solution  exemplar ¶ shown   [Seen solution] rung 4    │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 4 attempts before first help · rungs: median 2, max 4 · 1 seen solution   │
│ Margin is not affiliated with … Marks are practice estimates.             │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**What is never shareable.** The public variant and every share card strip: mastery states and pips, calibration status of the student, Brier and confidence data, predicted grade band (extends 10-PROG-001), Plan contents, wrong-question bank, memory objects, affect or distress signals, moderation events, other users' identities, parent or teacher identities, the raw uploaded image, and any Turn text that is not a draft the student wrote. Verbatim official mark-scheme or examiner-report wording is never exported (§06); the Mark cites our paraphrase and a page link.

**States.** `empty` (no receipt yet): "Receipts are created when you work on a piece with a deadline. Start one from any Session." + `Start a receipt`. `generating`: the hash and signature take ≤3 s; the page shows the timeline immediately with the cover block skeletoned. `revoked`: `/r/:token` returns a plain page "This link was turned off by its owner." with no content and no cached copy (`Cache-Control: no-store`). `expired`: public tokens expire after 180 days by default with the same page and a "the owner can re-share" line. `error`: signing failure blocks publication — never publish an unsigned receipt; the card reads "The signature failed. Nothing was shared. Retry." `offline`: the owner view renders from cache; export and share are disabled with a tooltip. `quota`: exports are 2/day per account (11-API-005) with a plain-number line.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** `⌘K → Export receipt`; `Show teacher` copies the public link and, when a Class exists, posts the receipt to the teacher's queue instead of the clipboard; `Revoke link` is immediate and irreversible for that token (a new token can be issued); a share card is previewed before it is generated, and the preview is the exact file.

**Data.** Read: `receipts`, `attempts`, `turns` (rung, help-event metadata only), `drafts`, `marks`, `challenges`. Written: `receipts {id, subject_ref, window, payload_json, content_hash, signature, key_id, created_at}`, `receipt_tokens {token, receipt_id, created_at, revoked_at, expires_at, view_count}`, an `audit_log` row on every share, revoke and export.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-RCPT-001 | Every rendered receipt (screen, PDF, JSON) verifies against `/verify/:receiptId`; a byte-modified export fails verification with a named reason. | Tamper test: flip one character in the JSON → `invalid_signature`; PDF re-export of the same receipt gives an identical `content_hash`. |
| 10-RCPT-002 | The public variant contains none of the stripped fields listed above. | Field-diff test between owner and public payloads; the strip list is one constant with a snapshot test; 0 leaks over 500 fixtures. |
| 10-RCPT-003 | Share cards render server-side, deterministically, and carry the non-affiliation line and the calibration state of the Mark. | Image hash stable across two renders; OCR of 50 cards finds both strings. |
| 10-RCPT-004 | Revocation takes effect in ≤5 s globally, including CDN and any generated share card URL. | Revoke then fetch from three edge regions: 404/410 with no body. |
| 10-RCPT-005 | Seen-solution events and help rungs appear in the receipt exactly as recorded; the receipt cannot be edited, filtered or "cleaned up" by the student before sharing. | UI has no edit affordance; API rejects a partial receipt request; a fixture with 3 seen-solution rows shares all 3. |
| 10-RCPT-006 | A receipt is never generated or shared automatically; every share is an explicit student action with the audience named. | 0 shares without a matching `audit_log` action row. |
| 10-RCPT-007 | Receipt PDF ≤10 MB, tagged PDF/UA, printable per 09-PRNT-003, with the hash in every footer. | `pdfinfo` + Playwright print test on the fixture set. |

Why: 64% of US high-schoolers fear being falsely accused of AI use and ESL writers are flagged 2–3× more often by detectors [A59]; a signed process log is the only defence a student can hold that does not depend on a detector being right.

## 10.24 Distress, safety, system and quota states
**Purpose.** Specify the surfaces that appear when something is wrong — with the student, the network, the model route, the quota, or the platform — so that none of them is invented at build time. Every one is an inline card or a banner in the flow; none is a modal (09-PRIN-009). The scripts and escalation logic live in §12.4–12.8; the pixels and the exact strings live here.

**Stage.** MVP for rows 1–12 of the catalogue and for the whole crisis-resource config: a product that marks teenagers' work at 23:40 ships its distress, offline, quota and maintenance surfaces on day one. Nothing here is v1.

**Jobs served.** All twelve, in the moment they break. 02-MOM-003 (mock season) and 02-MOM-005 (the night before) are where most of these fire.

**Route.** No route of its own. These are components rendered in place: `SafetyCard` and `DistressCard` in the Session (10.5) and anywhere a Turn streams; `Banner` under the top bar for connection, degradation and quota; `InlineError` inside the failing region; `/status` and the maintenance page for platform-wide events.

**Layout.** Distress and crisis cards render in the Turn stream at full centre measure with a 1 px `--border-strong` rule and no icon; they are never dismissible by scrolling past — dismissal is an explicit `Close`. Banners are `--banner-h` 40 px single-line at ≥900 px and `--banner-h-2` 56 px two-line below, sticky under the top bar, stacking at most **two** (a third collapses the older pair into "2 notices ▾"); 44 px is `--touch-min` and is not a banner height. Error cards sit inside the region that failed and preserve the user's input above them.

**The catalogue.** Copy is quoted here so the state is legible in one place; the shipped text is the §15.E catalogue entry under the `key` column, and where 09-COPY-002 lists the same key its "Do" column and this row are the same object. The catalogue test compares all three against §15.E byte-for-byte and fails on any drift, so a string is edited in §15.E and nowhere else.

| # | State | `key` | Trigger | Copy (canonical string) | Primary action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Distress | `distress.night` | `affect = distress` (04.9) | "That sounds like a hard night. Your last three Marks went up. Tonight: sleep. Tomorrow's first block is 25 minutes on 3.2. If it feels worse than exam stress, here is someone to talk to." | `Show me tomorrow's block` · text link `Someone to talk to` |
| 2 | Crisis | `crisis.card` | `affect = crisis` | "I'm not the right help for this, and I don't want to guess. Here are people who answer day and night — {resources}. If you are in danger now, call {emergency}." Tutor content is suspended for the Session; the Item is not continued in the same Turn. | `Call {primary}` (tel: link) · `Text {shortcode}` · `Back to the Plan` |
| 3 | Session-length nudge | `wellbeing.break` | First nudge at 50 min continuous use, one repeat at 110 min and no more that day; 25 min in exam week (T−14 to the last paper); 40 min while `wellbeing_state = watch` (12-WELL-002); suppressed inside a timed paper, within 30 min of an exam, and inside a Card review under 10 min | "50 minutes. A 5-minute break makes the next 25 worth more." | `Take 5` (pauses timers) · `Keep going` |
| 4 | Late-night notice | `wellbeing.late_night` | a Turn after 23:00 local in exam week, or after bedtime + 60 min | "It's 23:40 and Paper 2 is Thursday. One card deck, then sleep — the last hour before sleep is the one that sticks." | `Last deck (6 min)` · `Keep going` |
| 5 | Offline | `system.offline` | `navigator.onLine=false` or 3 failed sends | "Offline. Cards work; Marks and new Turns will send when you're back." | `Show what's queued` |
| 6 | Reconnected | `system.reconnected` | connection restored with a non-empty outbox | "Back online. 2 Marks and 1 rewrite sent." | auto-dismiss 4 s |
| 7 | Degraded route (§08.7) | `route.degraded` | any rung change on the Turn's route | On the Turn, not a banner: "Answered on Think — Deep is busy." / "Marked by our second marker — Provisional for this paper." / "Quick answers until 18:00 — you've used today's Think budget." | `Why?` opens a 3-line explanation with the route names |
| 8 | Marking paused (05-GATE-005) | `mark.paused` | seeded-QA auto-pause on the paper | "Marking is paused for this paper; you have feedback and spans, the mark will be added automatically." | `Notify me` (on by default) |
| 9 | Quota soft slow-down | `limits.reached` | `levels_marks` or `cheap_turns` cap reached (11-BILL-003) | "3 of 3 levels Marks used today. Points marking and Learn continue; the next levels Mark is at 00:00, or tonight on Student." | `See limits` → Settings › Usage. Never a modal, never mid-Session termination |
| 10 | Exam-week grace | `limits.grace.free` / `limits.grace.paid` | T−14 to the last paper | Plan-conditional, rendering the number from `limits.yaml` for the viewer's plan — Free: "Exam week: 6 levels Marks a day until {last_paper_date}." Paid: "Exam week: no marking limit until {last_paper_date}." A hard-coded "limits are off" fails the copy lint (09-COPY-002 #10), because Free grace is 6/day, not none | none (informational, one appearance per day) |
| 11 | Inline error | `error.generic` | any failed request | Cause + remedy in ≤2 sentences, no apology theatre: "The marker timed out. Retry with Think, or copy your answer." | `Retry` (idempotent, 11-API-002) |
| 12 | Maintenance | `system.maintenance` | planned window or platform incident | "Margin is down for {n} minutes. Your work is saved. Cards and downloaded units still work offline." with the `/status` link and the ETA in absolute local time | `Open Cards` |

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ⓘ  3 of 3 levels Marks used today. Points marking and Learn continue;│
│    the next levels Mark is at 00:00, or tonight on Student.  See limits│
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 …Turn…
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ That sounds like a hard night. Your last three Marks went up.        │
│ Tonight: sleep. Tomorrow's first block is 25 minutes on 3.2.         │
│ If it feels worse than exam stress, here is someone to talk to.      │
│                                                                       │
│ [ Show me tomorrow's block ]        Someone to talk to      Close     │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Crisis resources by region.** Config-driven, never model-generated:

Author `config/crisis_resources.yaml`; the build emits `config/crisis_resources.json`, which is the file §12.4 (12-TEEN-005) reads. **The numbers below are defaults, not verified facts. Counsel and operations must confirm every line — that the service exists, that it is free, that its hours and languages are as stated, and that a minor may call it — and set `verified_on` before that region ships.** A region with `verified_on: null` is not served to any account.

```yaml
schema: crisis_resources@1
# The renderer exposes exactly four placeholders to the §10.24 copy — no others exist.
placeholders:
  resources: "services[] where show_in_card, rendered '{name} {phone} ({hours})', joined with '; '"
  primary:   "the service with role: primary; for age_band != 18plus, the role: youth service when one exists"
  shortcode: "the first service with sms_shortcode set; the [Text {shortcode}] action is not rendered when null"
  emergency: "regions[].emergency; the sentence 'If you are in danger now, call {emergency}.' is dropped when null"
regions:
  GB:
    emergency: "999"
    services:
      - {name: "Samaritans",  role: primary, phone: "116 123",      sms_shortcode: null,            hours: "24h", languages: [en], under_18: false, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
      - {name: "Childline",   role: youth,   phone: "0800 1111",    sms_shortcode: null,            hours: "24h", languages: [en], under_18: true,  show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
      - {name: "Shout",       role: text,    phone: null,           sms_shortcode: "SHOUT to 85258", hours: "24h", languages: [en], under_18: false, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
  US:
    emergency: "911"
    services:
      - {name: "988 Suicide & Crisis Lifeline", role: primary, phone: "988",            sms_shortcode: "988",          hours: "24h", languages: [en, es], under_18: false, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
      - {name: "Childhelp",                     role: youth,   phone: "1-800-422-4453", sms_shortcode: null,           hours: "24h", languages: [en, es], under_18: true,  show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
      - {name: "Crisis Text Line",              role: text,    phone: null,             sms_shortcode: "HOME to 741741", hours: "24h", languages: [en], under_18: false, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
  KZ:
    emergency: "112"
    services:
      - {name: "111 national children's helpline", role: youth, phone: "111", sms_shortcode: null, hours: "24h", languages: [kk, ru], under_18: true, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
  RU:
    emergency: "112"
    services:
      - {name: "Детский телефон доверия", role: youth, phone: "8-800-2000-122", sms_shortcode: null, hours: "24h", languages: [ru], under_18: true, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
  DE: {inherits: _eu}          # every EU member ships as `{inherits: _eu}` until its own entry is verified
  _eu:                         # not an ISO code; reachable only through `inherits`
    emergency: "112"
    services:
      - {name: "116 123 emotional-support line", role: primary, phone: "116 123", sms_shortcode: null, hours: "{{per_country}}", languages: [{{per_country}}], under_18: false, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
      - {name: "116 111 child helpline",         role: youth,   phone: "116 111", sms_shortcode: null, hours: "{{per_country}}", languages: [{{per_country}}], under_18: true,  show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
  _default:                    # no country resolved, or the resolved country has no verified entry
    emergency: null
    services:
      - {name: "Find a Helpline", role: primary, phone: null, url: "https://findahelpline.com", sms_shortcode: null, hours: "24h", languages: [en], under_18: false, show_in_card: true, verified_on: null, source_url: "…"}
```

**States.** The catalogue above is this screen's state list; there is no separate `empty`/`loading` story because these surfaces are themselves the states of other screens. Two composite states are specified here rather than per row: **stacking** — at most two banners, ordered offline > maintenance > marking-paused > quota > grace, a third collapsing the older pair into "2 notices ▾"; and **suppression** — while a distress or crisis card is open no banner, nudge, notification or upsell renders anywhere in the app until it is closed (12-WELL-003), because a quota notice under a crisis card is the worst screen this product can draw.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Every card is keyboard-reachable in the flow position where it renders; `Esc` closes a dismissible card and returns focus to the element that preceded it, and does nothing on the crisis card, which closes only through its explicit `Close`. Resource links (`tel:`, `sms:`, `url`) come first in the tab order of the crisis card (10-STAT-004). Banners are not focus traps and do not steal focus on appearance; a screen reader announces them once through the same `role="status"` region, never per re-render. `Take 5` pauses every timer including the Plan block and the soft item timer; `Keep going` records `nudge_dismissed` and does not re-arm before the next threshold in row 3.

**Data.** Reads: `affect` and `wellbeing_state` (04.9, §12.5), `policy.tenant_region` / `profile.country` / IP country and `age_band` (10-STAT-001), `config/crisis_resources.json`, `config/limits.yaml` for rows 9 and 10, route state from §08.7, the incident feed for row 12. Writes: `wellbeing_events {kind, shown_at, dismissed_at, action}`, `crisis_card_shown {region_resolved, resolution_level, age_band}` — resolution level and region only, never the message that triggered it, and never a transcript excerpt — `nudge_dismissed`, `crisis_config_fallback` counter, `quota_notice_shown {meter, plan}`. No row here writes to the Learner Graph, and no row here is a training signal.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-STAT-001 | Resolve the region in exactly this order and stop at the first hit: tenant region (`policy.tenant_region`) → account region (`profile.country`) → IP country → `_default`. UI language never selects a region; a `13_15` or `16_17` account prefers the `role: youth` service as `{primary}`. | Fixture matrix of the four resolution levels × three age bands; a KZ account inside a GB school tenant renders the GB block. |
| 10-STAT-002 | The model never emits a helpline number, shortcode or URL. The renderer builds `{resources}`, `{primary}`, `{shortcode}` and `{emergency}` from config only; if the config is missing, unparsable, or the resolved region has no `show_in_card` service, it fails **closed** to `_default` and logs `crisis_config_fallback`. Model text matching a phone/helpline-URL regex blocks the Turn. | Delete `crisis_resources.json` in staging → the crisis card still renders the `_default` block; counter increments; 0 numbers in model output across the red-team corpus (§12.10). |
| 10-STAT-003 | Operations re-verify every entry quarterly and before each exam series, writing `verified_on`. `pnpm safety:lint` fails the build when any served region has an entry with `verified_on` null or older than 120 days (12-TEEN-005), and names the region and service. | Backdate one `verified_on` by 121 days → CI fails with that region named. |
| 10-STAT-004 | `Call {primary}` is a real `tel:` link and `Text {shortcode}` a real `sms:` link, built from the config values, tappable on mobile and keyboard-reachable before any other control in the card (§09 A11Y). A service with `url` and no phone renders as a link, never as copyable text. | DOM test: `href` equals `tel:` / `sms:` plus the config value; tab order puts resource links first. |
| 10-STAT-005 | No region is served until counsel has signed off its entries and the safety case doc (11-PRIV-001) records the sign-off date; until then that country resolves to `_default`. | Region-readiness table in the safety case; a fixture for an unsigned country renders `_default`. |



## 10.25 Admin / Compiler (internal)
**Purpose.** The internal console where Packs are built and shipped: the ingestion DAG (§06.4), the review UIs that human-confirm mapping and scheme structuring, the provenance ledger and kill switch, the per-paper calibration and gate dashboards (§05.6), eval runs (§08.9) and feature flags. It is built from the same tokens as the product (06-COMP-006) because the people who use it stare at it for eight hours a day.

**Stage.** MVP. Internal-only, behind the staff role; it is how a Pack reaches the gate at all (§06), so it cannot be deferred behind the surfaces that depend on it.

**Jobs served.** None of J1–J12. It serves the content lead, the ex-examiner calibrators, the ML lead and the on-call engineer; its output is the Pack readiness state every learner surface depends on.

**Route.** `/admin/*`, behind SSO with hardware second factor and a role claim: `ingest · queue · map · scheme · er · ledger · calibration · evals · flags · tenants`. Every route is audit-logged with the actor; no route is reachable from the product shell (separate app bundle, separate cookie scope).

**Layout.** Two panes, keyboard-first: left = work queue (virtualised list, filter row, counts), right = the item with **the source crop always visible beside the proposal** (06-COMP-006). No mobile layout — desktop ≥1280 only; below that a line says so.

**Regions and elements.**

| Route | Elements and rules |
|---|---|
| `ingest` | DAG view of stages 0–16 per document set: node = stage, colour-free status glyph, throughput, error count; click a node → the documents in it. Actions: `Replay stage` (keyed by `(doc_id, doc_version, stage, rules_version)`, 06-COMP-005), `Pause pipeline`, `Open rules file`. Live counters: pages/hour, `parse_confidence` distribution, pass-disagreement %, cost to date (06-COMP-004). |
| `queue` | The human queues in one place with age p95 and owner: classify-unknown, ambiguous paper IDs, unparsed formulas, low-confidence pages, caption checks, disagreement pairs. Sort by age; SLA badge at >3 working days. |
| `map` | **Section → syllabus mapping** review (stage 8): left the source section with its heading path and page crop, right the proposed `syllabus_node_id` with siblings and `objective_text`, confidence, and the source's own numbering shown as *its* numbering. Keys `y` accept · `n` reject · `e` edit target · `j/k` move · `b` batch-accept the current ≥0.95 block. Reviewer acceptance rate shown live. |
| `scheme` | Marking-point editor: the paste-to-structured output (§06.7) as an editable 05.3 record — marking points with type, conjunctive atoms, accept/reject, AO; levels grids with every level present; an 8-gram overlap meter against the source that must read 0 before `Confirm`. Nothing here can edit official text, only our paraphrase and flags. |
| `er` | Examiner-report mining review: paraphrase left, source crop right, overlap meter, and the heuristic test-run against 20 gold scripts showing hit count before accept. |
| `ledger` | `provenance_ledger` search by `sha256`, `source_url`, `doc_id` or title, answering in ≤1 s with the full derivation closure (chunks, items, schemes, insights, cache keys). Each row: class, `licence_basis`, expiry, uploader org, hash-chain status. **Kill switch** is a per-document toggle with a mandatory reason and a confirm-by-typing-the-title step; flipping it removes the document from prefilter, practice selection, Pack builds and caches within the 24-hour takedown SLA and writes an immutable ledger event (06-PROV-007). |
| `calibration` | Per paper: state (`not yet calibrated · provisional · gated`), QWK, level agreement, within-tolerance %, signed error, n scripts by gold tier, the measured human–human line, fairness buckets (region, L1, handwritten, length), series covered, policy version, challenge and upheld rates, last audit. Actions: `Run gate`, `Publish to the public calibration page`, `Pause this paper` (drops Marks to the second family with the Provisional badge). Seeding auto-pause events appear as a timeline under the numbers. |
| `evals` | Eval runs by suite (pedagogy 30-turn, praise lint, marking gold per paper, retrieval Recall@8, cache assertion, a11y, CWV) × policy version × model; diff against the previous run; a red row blocks the release train and links to the failing cases. |
| `flags` | Feature flags and kill switches with owner, default, rollout %, experiment link and expiry date; flags without an expiry cannot be created. Named kill switches at minimum: `anon_landing_mark`, `levels_marking`, `predicted_grades`, `parent_digest`, `voice`, `public_receipts`, `debt_guard`. |
| `tenants` | Tenant policy inspector (read-mostly): residency, allowed providers, retention, budget, consent coverage, DPA state. Editing a policy requires two-person approval. |

```
/admin/calibration                                        content-lead@ · SSO ✓
┌ Papers ──────────────┐┌ 9609 P2 (levels) ───────────────────────────────────┐
│ 9609 P1   gated      ││ state gated · policy v31 · last audit 6 Sep          │
│ 9609 P2   gated  ◄   ││ QWK 0.82  human–human 0.79  ✓   level agree 0.86     │
│ 9609 P3   provisional││ within ±1 0.91   signed error +0.12   n 312 (T0 88 / │
│ 9609 P4   provisional││ T1 201 / T2 23)                                       │
│ 0450 P1   not calib. ││ fairness: region −0.02 · L1 −0.03 · handwritten −0.04 │
│ 9708 P2   gated      ││ challenge 1.9% · overturn 22%                           │
│ ─ queues ─           ││ ── seeding timeline ──                                │
│ map        41        ││ 4 Sep auto-pause Q3(b) family · resumed 4 Sep 19:10   │
│ scheme     12        ││ [Run gate] [Publish] [Pause this paper]               │
│ ledger     0         │└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
└──────────────────────┘
```

**States.** `empty`: a queue with nothing in it shows the throughput number instead of an illustration. `loading`: virtualised rows skeleton at final height. `error`: stage failures render the exception and the replay command; a failed publish publishes nothing (06-COMP-004/16). `offline`: n/a — admin is online-only and says so. `gated/quota`: n/a. `two-person`: destructive actions (kill switch, tenant policy edit, publish, pause a paper) render an approval strip naming the requester and the approver, and stay pending until a second qualified actor confirms.

**Interactions and shortcuts.** Everything reachable by keyboard: `g` then the route letter jumps; `j/k` moves; `y/n/e` in review UIs; `⌘Enter` confirms; `?` lists the map. Bulk actions are always previewable ("This will kill 1 document, 214 chunks, 38 items, 6 insights — type the title to confirm").

**Data.** Read: `documents`, `chunks`, `ingest_jobs`, `mapping_table`, `mark_scheme_items`, `examiner_insights`, `provenance_ledger`, `calibration_records`, `eval_runs`, `flags`, `tenants`. Written: review decisions with reviewer id, ledger events, gate results, flag changes, and an `audit_log` row for every action including reads of a tenant policy.

**Requirements.**

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| 10-ADMN-001 | Admin is a separate bundle behind SSO + hardware 2FA with per-route role claims; no learner-facing route can navigate to it and no admin session shares a cookie with the product. | Auth suite: product session token rejected on every `/admin` route; role matrix test of 9 routes × 5 roles. |
| 10-ADMN-002 | Every admin action writes an `audit_log` row `{actor, route, object_id, before, after, reason?}`; policy reads are logged too. | 100 scripted actions → 100 rows; a row is unwritable-after (append-only trigger). |
| 10-ADMN-003 | Kill switch removes the document from prefilter, practice selection, Pack builds and caches, verified by the derivation closure, within the published 24-hour SLA and measurably in <5 minutes in staging. | Kill a fixture document; retrieval, item selection and cache probes return 0 references; ledger event present. |
| 10-ADMN-004 | No review UI can edit official source text — only our paraphrase, mappings and flags; the 8-gram overlap meter must read 0 before a scheme record can be confirmed. | Attempt to save a record with 8-gram overlap >0 → rejected with the overlapping span named. |
| 10-ADMN-005 | The calibration dashboard is the single source for the public calibration page and for any marketing number; publishing copies the row verbatim. | Reconciliation job: published page ≡ `calibration_records` row; a mismatch fails CI. |
| 10-ADMN-006 | Destructive actions require two-person approval and a typed confirmation; a single actor cannot kill a document, pause a paper, publish a gate or edit a tenant policy. | Single-actor attempt blocked in the API, not only the UI. |
| 10-ADMN-007 | Every flag has an owner and an expiry; an expired flag fails the nightly job and blocks the release train until resolved. | `pnpm flags:lint` on a fixture with an expired flag → non-zero exit. |
| 10-ADMN-008 | Review UIs meet the same a11y gates as the product (09.12) and the source crop is visible without scrolling at 1280×800. | axe-core on all admin routes; screenshot audit of the four review UIs. |

Why: the Pack is the moat and the ledger is the takedown defence; both are only as good as the console that makes confirming a record faster than skipping it (§06.4, SYNTHESIS §5 bet 10).


## 10.26 Ask
**Purpose.** Give the student the thing they actually open a chat product to do — ask a question right now, about anything, with no Course, no setup and no commitment — inside Margin rather than in the other tab. Ask is the general-help surface: full chat parity (§03.2), full citation and source behaviour where a Pack happens to cover the question, and an explicit, permanent statement that it is not exam-grounded study and does not count as such.

**Stage.** MVP, in full. This is the most frequent chat action in the product and the answer §00, §03.1 and 03-SCOP-006 give to "what happens when there is no Pack"; a deferred Ask means the fastest path to help is a competitor.

**Jobs served.** J4 (explain this so it sticks), J11 ("just the answer" without losing the learning), J9 (a subject Margin has no Pack for); 02-MOM-001 (the first five minutes, before any Course exists), 02-MOM-005 (21:40, one question, then bed).

**Route.** `/ask/:sessionId`. Created by the sidebar **Ask** row (10.3), by `⌘K → Ask <anything>`, by typing at `/ask`, by "Ask about {subject}" from a `waiting` Course (10.4) and by the SelectionPopover's **Ask** from any reader or Turn. `?q=<text>` pre-fills the composer without sending. The route is signed-in only: the anonymous equivalent is 10.1, which marks rather than converses. An Ask Session is a first-class Session row with `course_id = null`; it is listed under Recent with an "Ask" tag and survives until the student deletes it.

**Layout.** The Session layout (10.5) minus two things: no CourseTree in the sidebar (the sidebar shows Home, Courses, Recent, Due — Ask has no tree of its own) and no HelpLadder in the message margin (there is no gradable Item, so there is no rung to climb). Desktop ≥1280: `280px | 1fr | 480px`, centre 68 ch, right pane at its 48 px rail, opening to `--panel-w` on the first citation chip exactly as 10-CONV-002 specifies. Tablet and mobile as 10.5. The composer is identical to 10.5's in geometry and behaviour, with one difference stated below.

**Regions and elements.**

| Region | Elements |
|---|---|
| Top bar | back · Session title (auto, editable) · an **Ask** chip in `--bg-subtle` · ⋯ (Rename · Move to a Course · Export · Delete) |
| Scope line | a persistent 13 px `--text-muted` line under the top bar, never dismissible, never a banner: "Ask — general help. Not marked, not grounded in a syllabus, and not counted as study." When a Pack covers the detected subject the second half changes to "…citations come from {pack_name}; nothing here is marked or counted." |
| Turn stream | **UserMessage** and **Turn (assistant)** exactly as 10.5, with MessageActions, branching, edit-and-regenerate and MemoryChip **absent** (see Data) |
| Composer | Composer with **no ModeChip** — Ask has no Learn/Practise/Mark modes, because all three write to a Course. In its place, a single text button **Move to a Course** and the placeholder "Ask anything" |
| Right pane | Source · Scratchpad tabs only (no Cards tab, because Ask makes no Cards); citation chips resolve exactly as 10.16 |
